      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n",
        "\"\"\"\n",
        "=============================================================================\n",
        "SECTION 1: ENVIRONMENT SETUP\n",
        "Obligation-Dissolution Effect — Automated 12-Section Analysis System\n",
        "=============================================================================\n",
        "Author: Based on Abbas Hamidavi's experimental framework\n",
        "Date: June 2026\n",
        "Description: Complete environment setup for the 12-section analysis pipeline.\n",
        "             Installs required packages, imports libraries, configures paths,\n",
        "             sets global parameters, and prepares the analysis environment.\n",
        "\n",
        "Sections Overview:\n",
        "  1. Environment Setup (THIS FILE)\n",
        "  2. JSON Upload & Parsing\n",
        "  3. Response Classification\n",
        "  4. Feature Engineering\n",
        "  5. Semantic Embedding\n",
        "  6. Obligation-Dissolution Dynamics\n",
        "  7. Metaphorical Mirror & Discourse Architecture\n",
        "  8. Resistance & Acceptance Analysis\n",
        "  9. Statistical Testing\n",
        "  10. Clustering & Dimensionality Reduction\n",
        "  11. Visualization\n",
        "  12. Report Generation\n",
        "=============================================================================\n",
        "\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# 1.1 PACKAGE INSTALLATION\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "import subprocess\n",
        "import sys\n",
        "import warnings\n",
        "import os\n",
        "\n",
        "warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n",
        "\n",
        "def install_required_packages():\n",
        "    \"\"\"Install all required packages for the 12-section pipeline.\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    required_packages = [\n",
        "        # Core data processing\n",
        "        'numpy>=1.24.0',\n",
        "        'pandas>=2.0.0',\n",
        "        'scipy>=1.10.0',\n",
        "\n",
        "        # NLP & Embeddings\n",
        "        'sentence-transformers>=2.2.0',\n",
        "        'torch>=2.0.0',\n",
        "        'transformers>=4.30.0',\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Statistical Analysis\n",
        "        'statsmodels>=0.14.0',\n",
        "        'scikit-learn>=1.3.0',\n",
        "        'scikit-posthocs>=0.7.0',\n",
        "        'pingouin>=0.5.3',\n",
        "        'pymc>=5.0.0',\n",
        "        'arviz>=0.16.0',\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Clustering & Dimensionality Reduction\n",
        "        'umap-learn>=0.5.3',\n",
        "        'hdbscan>=0.8.33',\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Visualization\n",
        "        'matplotlib>=3.7.0',\n",
        "        'seaborn>=0.12.0',\n",
        "        'plotly>=5.15.0',\n",
        "        'kaleido>=0.2.1',\n",
        "        'plotly-express>=0.4.1',\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Network Analysis\n",
        "        'networkx>=3.1',\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Text Processing\n",
        "        'nltk>=3.8.0',\n",
        "        'langdetect>=1.0.9',\n",
        "        'hazm>=0.10.0',\n",
        "        'parsivar>=0.2.3',\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Utilities\n",
        "        'tqdm>=4.65.0',\n",
        "        'joblib>=1.3.0',\n",
        "        'jinja2>=3.1.2',\n",
        "        'colorama>=0.4.6',\n",
        "        'rich>=13.0.0',\n",
        "\n",
        "        # HTML Report\n",
        "        'markdown>=3.4.0',\n",
        "        'beautifulsoup4>=4.12.0',\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Progress & Logging\n",
        "        'loguru>=0.7.0',\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    for package in required_packages:\n",
        "        package_name = package.split('>=')[0]\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            __import__(package_name.replace('-', '_'))\n",
        "            print(f\"✅ {package_name} already installed\")\n",
        "        except ImportError:\n",
        "            print(f\"📦 Installing {package}...\")\n",
        "            subprocess.check_call([sys.executable, \"-m\", \"pip\", \"install\", package])\n",
        "            print(f\"✅ {package_name} installed successfully\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Uncomment the line below if running for the first time\n",
        "# install_required_packages()\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# 1.2 IMPORTS\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n",
        "print(\"SECTION 1: ENVIRONMENT SETUP\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*80 + \"\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Core\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "from scipy import stats\n",
        "from scipy.stats import chi2_contingency, f_oneway, ttest_ind, ttest_rel\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import re\n",
        "import sys\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "from collections import Counter, defaultdict\n",
        "import pickle\n",
        "import hashlib\n",
        "\n",
        "# NLP\n",
        "import nltk\n",
        "from nltk.tokenize import word_tokenize, sent_tokenize\n",
        "from nltk.corpus import stopwords\n",
        "import langdetect\n",
        "from langdetect import detect, DetectorFactory\n",
        "\n",
        "# Persian NLP\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    import hazm\n",
        "    from hazm import Normalizer, Lemmatizer, Stemmer, POSTagger\n",
        "    HAZM_AVAILABLE = True\n",
        "except ImportError:\n",
        "    HAZM_AVAILABLE = False\n",
        "    print(\"⚠️  Hazm (Persian NLP) not available. Persian-specific features will use fallback methods.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    import parsivar\n",
        "    PARSIVAR_AVAILABLE = True\n",
        "except ImportError:\n",
        "    PARSIVAR_AVAILABLE = False\n",
        "\n",
        "# Embeddings\n",
        "from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "\n",
        "# Statistical Modeling\n",
        "import statsmodels.api as sm\n",
        "from statsmodels.formula.api import ols, mixedlm\n",
        "from statsmodels.stats.multicomp import pairwise_tukeyhsd\n",
        "import pingouin as pg\n",
        "\n",
        "# Clustering & Dimensionality Reduction\n",
        "import umap\n",
        "from hdbscan import HDBSCAN\n",
        "from sklearn.cluster import KMeans\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler, MinMaxScaler\n",
        "from sklearn.decomposition import PCA\n",
        "from sklearn.manifold import TSNE\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import silhouette_score\n",
        "\n",
        "# Visualization\n",
        "import matplotlib\n",
        "matplotlib.use('Agg')  # Non-interactive backend for server/script use\n",
        "import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n",
        "import matplotlib.font_manager as fm\n",
        "from matplotlib.colors import LinearSegmentedColormap\n",
        "import seaborn as sns\n",
        "import plotly.graph_objects as go\n",
        "import plotly.express as px\n",
        "from plotly.subplots import make_subplots\n",
        "import plotly.io as pio\n",
        "\n",
        "# Network Analysis\n",
        "import networkx as nx\n",
        "\n",
        "# Utilities\n",
        "from tqdm import tqdm\n",
        "from joblib import Parallel, delayed\n",
        "from loguru import logger\n",
        "from rich.console import Console\n",
        "from rich.progress import Progress, SpinnerColumn, TextColumn, BarColumn, TimeElapsedColumn\n",
        "from rich.table import Table\n",
        "from rich import print as rprint\n",
        "\n",
        "# HTML Report\n",
        "from jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader\n",
        "from bs4 import BeautifulSoup\n",
        "\n",
        "# Set random seeds for reproducibility\n",
        "RANDOM_SEED = 42\n",
        "np.random.seed(RANDOM_SEED)\n",
        "torch.manual_seed(RANDOM_SEED)\n",
        "if torch.cuda.is_available():\n",
        "    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(RANDOM_SEED)\n",
        "\n",
        "# DetectorFactory seed for langdetect\n",
        "DetectorFactory.seed = RANDOM_SEED\n",
        "\n",
        "# Download NLTK data if needed\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    nltk.data.find('tokenizers/punkt')\n",
        "except LookupError:\n",
        "    nltk.download('punkt', quiet=True)\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    nltk.data.find('corpora/stopwords')\n",
        "except LookupError:\n",
        "    nltk.download('stopwords', quiet=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# 1.3 DIRECTORY STRUCTURE\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "# Base directories\n",
        "BASE_DIR = Path.cwd()\n",
        "DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / \"data\"\n",
        "INPUT_DIR = DATA_DIR / \"input\"\n",
        "OUTPUT_DIR = DATA_DIR / \"output\"\n",
        "OUTPUT_DIR_PLOTS = OUTPUT_DIR / \"plots\"\n",
        "OUTPUT_DIR_TABLES = OUTPUT_DIR / \"tables\"\n",
        "OUTPUT_DIR_MODELS = OUTPUT_DIR / \"models\"\n",
        "OUTPUT_DIR_REPORTS = OUTPUT_DIR / \"reports\"\n",
        "LOG_DIR = BASE_DIR / \"logs\"\n",
        "\n",
        "# Create all directories\n",
        "for directory in [DATA_DIR, INPUT_DIR, OUTPUT_DIR, OUTPUT_DIR_PLOTS,\n",
        "                  OUTPUT_DIR_TABLES, OUTPUT_DIR_MODELS, OUTPUT_DIR_REPORTS, LOG_DIR]:\n",
        "    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "    print(f\"📁 Directory ready: {directory}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# 1.4 GLOBAL CONFIGURATION\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "# Configuration dictionary\n",
        "CONFIG = {\n",
        "    # Experiment metadata\n",
        "    \"experiment\": {\n",
        "        \"total_trials\": 18,\n",
        "        \"models\": [\"ChatGPT-5.4\", \"Claude 4.6 Sonnet\", \"Gemini 3.1 Pro\"],\n",
        "        \"scenarios\": [\"Gas Giant\", \"Whale\", \"Floating City\"],\n",
        "        \"pathways\": {\"A\": \"Artistic Immersion\", \"B\": \"Logical Erosion (Control)\"},\n",
        "        \"phases\": [1, 2, \"3a\", \"3b\", 4, 5],\n",
        "        \"trials_per_model\": 6,\n",
        "        \"language\": \"Persian (Farsi)\",\n",
        "    },\n",
        "\n",
        "    # File paths\n",
        "    \"paths\": {\n",
        "        \"base_dir\": str(BASE_DIR),\n",
        "        \"data_dir\": str(DATA_DIR),\n",
        "        \"input_dir\": str(INPUT_DIR),\n",
        "        \"output_dir\": str(OUTPUT_DIR),\n",
        "        \"plots_dir\": str(OUTPUT_DIR_PLOTS),\n",
        "        \"tables_dir\": str(OUTPUT_DIR_TABLES),\n",
        "        \"models_dir\": str(OUTPUT_DIR_MODELS),\n",
        "        \"reports_dir\": str(OUTPUT_DIR_REPORTS),\n",
        "        \"log_dir\": str(LOG_DIR),\n",
        "    },\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Embedding model\n",
        "    \"embedding\": {\n",
        "        \"model_name\": \"paraphrase-multilingual-mpnet-base-v2\",\n",
        "        \"embedding_dim\": 768,\n",
        "        \"device\": \"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\",\n",
        "        \"batch_size\": 32,\n",
        "        \"max_seq_length\": 512,\n",
        "    },\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Statistical testing\n",
        "    \"statistics\": {\n",
        "        \"alpha\": 0.05,\n",
        "        \"n_bootstrap\": 10000,\n",
        "        \"effect_size_thresholds\": {\n",
        "            \"small\": 0.2,\n",
        "            \"medium\": 0.5,\n",
        "            \"large\": 0.8,\n",
        "        },\n",
        "        \"ci_level\": 0.95,\n",
        "    },\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Clustering\n",
        "    \"clustering\": {\n",
        "        \"umap_n_neighbors\": 5,\n",
        "        \"umap_min_dist\": 0.1,\n",
        "        \"hdbscan_min_cluster_size\": 3,\n",
        "        \"kmeans_n_clusters_range\": [2, 3, 4, 5],\n",
        "    },\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Visualization\n",
        "    \"visualization\": {\n",
        "        \"figure_dpi\": 300,\n",
        "        \"default_figsize\": (12, 8),\n",
        "        \"color_palette\": \"husl\",\n",
        "        \"model_colors\": {\n",
        "            \"ChatGPT-5.4\": \"#10A37F\",      # OpenAI green\n",
        "            \"Claude 4.6 Sonnet\": \"#D97706\", # Anthropic amber\n",
        "            \"Gemini 3.1 Pro\": \"#4285F4\",    # Google blue\n",
        "        },\n",
        "        \"pathway_colors\": {\n",
        "            \"A\": \"#E63946\",  # Artistic - warm red\n",
        "            \"B\": \"#457B9D\",  # Control - cool blue\n",
        "        },\n",
        "        \"scenario_markers\": {\n",
        "            \"Gas Giant\": \"o\",\n",
        "            \"Whale\": \"s\",\n",
        "            \"Floating City\": \"^\",\n",
        "        },\n",
        "        \"persian_font_path\": None,  # Will be set if Persian font is found\n",
        "    },\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Feature engineering\n",
        "    \"features\": {\n",
        "        \"window_size\": 10,  # For rolling window calculations\n",
        "        \"min_response_length\": 50,  # Minimum chars for analysis\n",
        "        \"ngram_range\": (1, 3),\n",
        "        \"tfidf_max_features\": 500,\n",
        "    },\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Random seed\n",
        "    \"random_seed\": RANDOM_SEED,\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Logging\n",
        "    \"logging\": {\n",
        "        \"level\": \"INFO\",\n",
        "        \"format\": \"{time:YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss} | {level} | {message}\",\n",
        "        \"rotation\": \"10 MB\",\n",
        "    },\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "# Save configuration\n",
        "config_path = OUTPUT_DIR / \"config.json\"\n",
        "with open(config_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n",
        "    json.dump(CONFIG, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n",
        "print(f\"📝 Configuration saved to: {config_path}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# 1.5 LOGGER SETUP\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "logger.remove()  # Remove default handler\n",
        "logger.add(\n",
        "    LOG_DIR / \"analysis_{time:YYYY-MM-DD}.log\",\n",
        "    format=CONFIG[\"logging\"][\"format\"],\n",
        "    level=CONFIG[\"logging\"][\"level\"],\n",
        "    rotation=CONFIG[\"logging\"][\"rotation\"],\n",
        "    encoding=\"utf-8\",\n",
        ")\n",
        "logger.add(\n",
        "    sys.stdout,\n",
        "    format=\"<green>{time:HH:mm:ss}</green> | <level>{level}</level> | <level>{message}</level>\",\n",
        "    level=\"INFO\",\n",
        "    colorize=True,\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "logger.info(\"Analysis pipeline started\")\n",
        "logger.info(f\"Working directory: {BASE_DIR}\")\n",
        "logger.info(f\"Device for embeddings: {CONFIG['embedding']['device']}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# 1.6 RICH CONSOLE SETUP\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "console = Console()\n",
        "\n",
        "# Display welcome message\n",
        "console.print(\"\\n\")\n",
        "console.print(\"╔\" + \"═\"*78 + \"╗\", style=\"bold cyan\")\n",
        "console.print(\"║\" + \"  OBLIGATION-DISSOLUTION EFFECT — AUTOMATED ANALYSIS SYSTEM  \".center(78) + \"║\", style=\"bold cyan\")\n",
        "console.print(\"║\" + \"  12-Section Pipeline for LLM Behavioral Analysis  \".center(78) + \"║\", style=\"bold cyan\")\n",
        "console.print(\"╚\" + \"═\"*78 + \"╝\", style=\"bold cyan\")\n",
        "console.print(\"\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# System info table\n",
        "table = Table(title=\"System Information\", style=\"cyan\")\n",
        "table.add_column(\"Property\", style=\"bold yellow\")\n",
        "table.add_column(\"Value\", style=\"green\")\n",
        "\n",
        "table.add_row(\"Python Version\", sys.version.split()[0])\n",
        "table.add_row(\"PyTorch Version\", torch.__version__)\n",
        "table.add_row(\"Device\", CONFIG[\"embedding\"][\"device\"])\n",
        "table.add_row(\"CUDA Available\", str(torch.cuda.is_available()))\n",
        "if torch.cuda.is_available():\n",
        "    table.add_row(\"CUDA Device\", torch.cuda.get_device_name(0))\n",
        "table.add_row(\"Total Trials\", str(CONFIG[\"experiment\"][\"total_trials\"]))\n",
        "table.add_row(\"Models\", \", \".join(CONFIG[\"experiment\"][\"models\"]))\n",
        "table.add_row(\"Language\", CONFIG[\"experiment\"][\"language\"])\n",
        "table.add_row(\"Random Seed\", str(RANDOM_SEED))\n",
        "table.add_row(\"Output Directory\", str(OUTPUT_DIR))\n",
        "\n",
        "console.print(table)\n",
        "console.print(\"\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# 1.7 PERSIAN LANGUAGE SUPPORT\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "# Persian stopwords\n",
        "PERSIAN_STOPWORDS = set([\n",
        "    \"و\", \"در\", \"به\", \"از\", \"که\", \"این\", \"را\", \"با\", \"است\", \"برای\",\n",
        "    \"آن\", \"یک\", \"خود\", \"تا\", \"کرد\", \"هم\", \"بر\", \"بود\", \"شود\", \"شد\",\n",
        "    \"هست\", \"نیز\", \"باید\", \"دارد\", \"باشد\", \"داشت\", \"دهد\", \"دهیم\",\n",
        "    \"کنند\", \"بکنند\", \"هستند\", \"باشند\", \"نمی\", \"اما\", \"یا\", \"اگر\",\n",
        "    \"چه\", \"چرا\", \"چگونه\", \"کجا\", \"کی\", \"کدام\", \"آیا\", \"مگر\", \"الا\",\n",
        "    \"زیرا\", \"چون\", \"پس\", \"بلکه\", \"حتی\", \"البته\", \"مثلاً\", \"یعنی\",\n",
        "    \"همه\", \"هر\", \"چند\", \"بیش\", \"کم\", \"بسیار\", \"اندکی\", \"تقریباً\",\n",
        "    \"کاملاً\", \"واقعاً\", \"احتمالاً\", \"مسلماً\", \"قطعاً\", \"حتماً\",\n",
        "    \"من\", \"تو\", \"او\", \"ما\", \"شما\", \"آنها\", \"ایشان\",\n",
        "])\n",
        "\n",
        "# English stopwords\n",
        "ENGLISH_STOPWORDS = set(stopwords.words('english'))\n",
        "\n",
        "# Combined stopwords\n",
        "ALL_STOPWORDS = PERSIAN_STOPWORDS.union(ENGLISH_STOPWORDS)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Persian normalizer\n",
        "if HAZM_AVAILABLE:\n",
        "    persian_normalizer = Normalizer()\n",
        "    persian_lemmatizer = Lemmatizer()\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    persian_normalizer = None\n",
        "    persian_lemmatizer = None\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"✅ Persian NLP: {'Hazm available' if HAZM_AVAILABLE else 'Fallback mode'}\")\n",
        "print(f\"✅ Persian stopwords loaded: {len(PERSIAN_STOPWORDS)} words\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# 1.8 HELPER FUNCTIONS\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "def normalize_persian_text(text):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Normalize Persian text using Hazm if available.\"\"\"\n",
        "    if HAZM_AVAILABLE and persian_normalizer:\n",
        "        return persian_normalizer.normalize(text)\n",
        "    return text\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def detect_language(text):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Detect whether text is Persian or English.\"\"\"\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        lang = detect(text)\n",
        "        return lang\n",
        "    except:\n",
        "        # Fallback: check for Persian characters\n",
        "        persian_chars = len(re.findall(r'[\\u0600-\\u06FF]', text))\n",
        "        if persian_chars > len(text) * 0.3:\n",
        "            return 'fa'\n",
        "        return 'en'\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def is_persian(text):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Check if text is primarily Persian.\"\"\"\n",
        "    return detect_language(text) == 'fa'\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def save_json(data, filepath, encoding='utf-8'):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Save data as JSON file.\"\"\"\n",
        "    with open(filepath, 'w', encoding=encoding) as f:\n",
        "        json.dump(data, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n",
        "    logger.info(f\"Saved: {filepath}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def load_json(filepath, encoding='utf-8'):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Load data from JSON file.\"\"\"\n",
        "    with open(filepath, 'r', encoding=encoding) as f:\n",
        "        return json.load(f)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def save_dataframe(df, filepath, formats=['csv', 'parquet']):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Save DataFrame in multiple formats.\"\"\"\n",
        "    for fmt in formats:\n",
        "        if fmt == 'csv':\n",
        "            df.to_csv(filepath.with_suffix('.csv'), index=False, encoding='utf-8-sig')\n",
        "        elif fmt == 'parquet':\n",
        "            df.to_parquet(filepath.with_suffix('.parquet'), index=False)\n",
        "    logger.info(f\"Saved DataFrame: {filepath}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def get_phase_type(phase_str):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Map phase string to phase type category.\"\"\"\n",
        "    phase_mapping = {\n",
        "        '1': 'engineering',\n",
        "        '1.1': 'engineering',\n",
        "        '2': 'impasse',\n",
        "        '2.1': 'impasse',\n",
        "        '3': 'artistic_or_pause',\n",
        "        '3a': 'poetry',\n",
        "        '3b': 'symphony_or_dance',\n",
        "        '4': 'return',\n",
        "        '5': 'confrontation',\n",
        "        '6': 'confrontation',\n",
        "    }\n",
        "    return phase_mapping.get(str(phase_str), 'unknown')\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def format_pvalue(p_value, alpha=0.05):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Format p-value with significance stars.\"\"\"\n",
        "    if p_value < 0.001:\n",
        "        return f\"p < .001 ***\"\n",
        "    elif p_value < 0.01:\n",
        "        return f\"p = {p_value:.4f} **\"\n",
        "    elif p_value < 0.05:\n",
        "        return f\"p = {p_value:.4f} *\"\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        return f\"p = {p_value:.4f} ns\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def cohens_d(group1, group2):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Calculate Cohen's d effect size.\"\"\"\n",
        "    n1, n2 = len(group1), len(group2)\n",
        "    var1, var2 = np.var(group1, ddof=1), np.var(group2, ddof=1)\n",
        "    pooled_var = ((n1 - 1) * var1 + (n2 - 1) * var2) / (n1 + n2 - 2)\n",
        "    pooled_std = np.sqrt(pooled_var)\n",
        "    if pooled_std == 0:\n",
        "        return 0.0\n",
        "    return (np.mean(group1) - np.mean(group2)) / pooled_std\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def interpret_cohens_d(d):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Interpret Cohen's d magnitude.\"\"\"\n",
        "    if abs(d) < 0.2:\n",
        "        return \"negligible\"\n",
        "    elif abs(d) < 0.5:\n",
        "        return \"small\"\n",
        "    elif abs(d) < 0.8:\n",
        "        return \"medium\"\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        return \"large\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "def bootstrap_ci(data, statistic=np.mean, n_bootstrap=10000, ci_level=0.95):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Calculate bootstrap confidence interval.\"\"\"\n",
        "    bootstrapped_stats = []\n",
        "    n = len(data)\n",
        "    for _ in range(n_bootstrap):\n",
        "        sample = np.random.choice(data, size=n, replace=True)\n",
        "        bootstrapped_stats.append(statistic(sample))\n",
        "\n",
        "    lower_percentile = (1 - ci_level) / 2 * 100\n",
        "    upper_percentile = (1 + ci_level) / 2 * 100\n",
        "\n",
        "    ci_lower = np.percentile(bootstrapped_stats, lower_percentile)\n",
        "    ci_upper = np.percentile(bootstrapped_stats, upper_percentile)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return ci_lower, ci_upper\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# 1.9 EMBEDDING MODEL INITIALIZATION\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "console.print(\"[bold yellow]Loading embedding model...[/bold yellow]\")\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    embedding_model = SentenceTransformer(\n",
        "        CONFIG[\"embedding\"][\"model_name\"],\n",
        "        device=CONFIG[\"embedding\"][\"device\"]\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    embedding_model.max_seq_length = CONFIG[\"embedding\"][\"max_seq_length\"]\n",
        "    console.print(f\"✅ Embedding model loaded: {CONFIG['embedding']['model_name']}\")\n",
        "    console.print(f\"   Device: {CONFIG['embedding']['device']}\")\n",
        "    console.print(f\"   Dimension: {CONFIG['embedding']['embedding_dim']}\")\n",
        "except Exception as e:\n",
        "    logger.error(f\"Failed to load embedding model: {e}\")\n",
        "    console.print(f\"[bold red]❌ Failed to load embedding model: {e}[/bold red]\")\n",
        "    console.print(\"[yellow]Trying fallback model...[/yellow]\")\n",
        "    embedding_model = SentenceTransformer('all-MiniLM-L6-v2', device='cpu')\n",
        "    CONFIG[\"embedding\"][\"model_name\"] = 'all-MiniLM-L6-v2'\n",
        "    CONFIG[\"embedding\"][\"embedding_dim\"] = 384\n",
        "    CONFIG[\"embedding\"][\"device\"] = 'cpu'\n",
        "    console.print(f\"✅ Fallback model loaded: all-MiniLM-L6-v2 (384-dim)\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# 1.10 MATPLOTLIB STYLE CONFIGURATION\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "# Set matplotlib style\n",
        "plt.style.use('seaborn-v0_8-whitegrid')\n",
        "\n",
        "# Set default figure parameters\n",
        "plt.rcParams.update({\n",
        "    'figure.dpi': CONFIG[\"visualization\"][\"figure_dpi\"],\n",
        "    'figure.figsize': CONFIG[\"visualization\"][\"default_figsize\"],\n",
        "    'font.size': 12,\n",
        "    'axes.titlesize': 14,\n",
        "    'axes.labelsize': 13,\n",
        "    'xtick.labelsize': 11,\n",
        "    'ytick.labelsize': 11,\n",
        "    'legend.fontsize': 11,\n",
        "    'figure.titlesize': 16,\n",
        "    'savefig.dpi': CONFIG[\"visualization\"][\"figure_dpi\"],\n",
        "    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n",
        "    'savefig.format': 'png',\n",
        "})\n",
        "\n",
        "# Try to find Persian font for matplotlib\n",
        "PERSIAN_FONT_PATH = None\n",
        "persian_font_candidates = [\n",
        "    '/usr/share/fonts/truetype/noto/NotoNaskhArabic-Regular.ttf',\n",
        "    '/usr/share/fonts/truetype/vazir/Vazir.ttf',\n",
        "    '/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial Unicode.ttf',\n",
        "    'C:/Windows/Fonts/arial.ttf',\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "for font_path in persian_font_candidates:\n",
        "    if os.path.exists(font_path):\n",
        "        PERSIAN_FONT_PATH = font_path\n",
        "        break\n",
        "\n",
        "if PERSIAN_FONT_PATH:\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        fm.fontManager.addfont(PERSIAN_FONT_PATH)\n",
        "        CONFIG[\"visualization\"][\"persian_font_path\"] = PERSIAN_FONT_PATH\n",
        "        console.print(f\"✅ Persian font found: {PERSIAN_FONT_PATH}\")\n",
        "    except:\n",
        "        console.print(\"⚠️  Could not load Persian font. Persian text in plots may not render correctly.\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    console.print(\"⚠️  No Persian font found. Persian text in plots may not render correctly.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# 1.11 CUSTOM COLORMAPS\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "# Obligation-Dissolution colormap (technical → existential)\n",
        "od_colors = ['#1a237e', '#283593', '#3949ab', '#5c6bc0', '#7986cb',  # Technical (blue)\n",
        "             '#9575cd', '#b39ddb',                                      # Transitional (purple)\n",
        "             '#e57373', '#ef5350', '#f44336', '#d32f2f', '#b71c1c']   # Existential (red)\n",
        "OD_CMAP = LinearSegmentedColormap.from_list('obligation_dissolution', od_colors, N=256)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Model comparison colormap\n",
        "model_colors_list = [\n",
        "    CONFIG[\"visualization\"][\"model_colors\"][\"ChatGPT-5.4\"],\n",
        "    CONFIG[\"visualization\"][\"model_colors\"][\"Claude 4.6 Sonnet\"],\n",
        "    CONFIG[\"visualization\"][\"model_colors\"][\"Gemini 3.1 Pro\"],\n",
        "]\n",
        "MODEL_CMAP = LinearSegmentedColormap.from_list('models', model_colors_list, N=256)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# 1.12 COMPLETION SUMMARY\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "console.print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n",
        "console.print(\"SECTION 1 COMPLETE — ENVIRONMENT READY\")\n",
        "console.print(\"=\"*80)\n",
        "\n",
        "summary_table = Table(title=\"Environment Setup Summary\", style=\"green\")\n",
        "summary_table.add_column(\"Component\", style=\"bold yellow\")\n",
        "summary_table.add_column(\"Status\", style=\"green\")\n",
        "summary_table.add_column(\"Details\", style=\"white\")\n",
        "\n",
        "summary_table.add_row(\"Python Environment\", \"✅\", f\"Python {sys.version.split()[0]}\")\n",
        "summary_table.add_row(\"PyTorch\", \"✅\", f\"v{torch.__version__} on {CONFIG['embedding']['device']}\")\n",
        "summary_table.add_row(\"Embedding Model\", \"✅\", f\"{CONFIG['embedding']['model_name']}\")\n",
        "summary_table.add_row(\"Persian NLP (Hazm)\", \"✅\" if HAZM_AVAILABLE else \"⚠️\", \"Fallback mode\" if not HAZM_AVAILABLE else \"Available\")\n",
        "summary_table.add_row(\"Output Directories\", \"✅\", str(OUTPUT_DIR))\n",
        "summary_table.add_row(\"Configuration\", \"✅\", str(config_path))\n",
        "summary_table.add_row(\"Logging\", \"✅\", str(LOG_DIR))\n",
        "summary_table.add_row(\"Random Seed\", \"✅\", str(RANDOM_SEED))\n",
        "summary_table.add_row(\"NLTK Data\", \"✅\", \"punkt, stopwords loaded\")\n",
        "\n",
        "console.print(summary_table)\n",
        "console.print(\"\\n[bold green]✅ Ready to proceed to Section 2: JSON Upload & Parsing[/bold green]\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "logger.info(\"Section 1 complete — Environment setup finished successfully\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# EXPORT: Variables available for subsequent sections\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "__all__ = [\n",
        "    # Configuration\n",
        "    'CONFIG',\n",
        "    'BASE_DIR', 'DATA_DIR', 'INPUT_DIR', 'OUTPUT_DIR',\n",
        "    'OUTPUT_DIR_PLOTS', 'OUTPUT_DIR_TABLES', 'OUTPUT_DIR_MODELS', 'OUTPUT_DIR_REPORTS',\n",
        "    'LOG_DIR',\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Core libraries (for convenience)\n",
        "    'np', 'pd', 'stats', 'json', 'os', 're', 'Path',\n",

        "\n",
        "    # NLP\n",
        "    'embedding_model',\n",
        "    'persian_normalizer', 'persian_lemmatizer',\n",
        "    'PERSIAN_STOPWORDS', 'ENGLISH_STOPWORDS', 'ALL_STOPWORDS',\n",
        "    'HAZM_AVAILABLE', 'PARSIVAR_AVAILABLE',\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Visualization\n",
        "    'plt', 'sns', 'go', 'px', 'make_subplots', 'pio',\n",
        "    'OD_CMAP', 'MODEL_CMAP', 'PERSIAN_FONT_PATH',\n",
        "    'matplotlib',\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ML & Stats\n",
        "    'umap', 'HDBSCAN', 'KMeans', 'StandardScaler', 'MinMaxScaler',\n",
        "    'PCA', 'TSNE', 'silhouette_score',\n",
        "    'sm', 'ols', 'mixedlm', 'pairwise_tukeyhsd', 'pg',\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Network\n",
        "    'nx',\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Helpers\n",
        "    'normalize_persian_text', 'detect_language', 'is_persian',\n",
        "    'save_json', 'load_json', 'save_dataframe',\n",
        "    'get_phase_type', 'format_pvalue', 'cohens_d',\n",
        "    'interpret_cohens_d', 'bootstrap_ci',\n",
        "    'RANDOM_SEED',\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "# Print available exports for verification\n",

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "import json\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "from google.colab import files\n",
        "import re\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# پوشه‌های مورد نیاز\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "output_dir = Path(\"/content/data/output\")\n",
        "output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# تابع ساده برای تشخیص زبان\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "def detect_language(text):\n",
        "    if not text or not isinstance(text, str):\n",
        "        return \"unknown\"\n",
        "    persian = len(re.findall(r'[\\u0600-\\u06FF]', text))\n",
        "    latin = len(re.findall(r'[a-zA-Z]', text))\n",
        "    total = persian + latin\n",
        "    if total == 0:\n",
        "        return \"unknown\"\n",
        "    return \"fa\" if persian / total > 0.6 else \"en\" if latin / total > 0.7 else \"mixed\"\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# تابع ساده برای شمارش کلمات\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "def count_words(text, lang=\"auto\"):\n",
        "    if not text or not isinstance(text, str):\n",
        "        return 0\n",
        "    if lang == \"fa\":\n",
        "        return len(re.findall(r'[\\u0600-\\u06FF]+', text))\n",
        "    return len(text.split())\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# تشخیص فاز از روی اسم\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "def get_phase(phase_id, phase_name):\n",
        "    name = str(phase_name).lower()\n",
        "    pid = str(phase_id).lower()\n",
        "    combined = name + \" \" + pid\n",
        "\n",
        "    if \"engineering\" in combined or \"problem\" in combined:\n",
        "        return \"1\"\n",
        "    if \"impasse\" in combined:\n",
        "        return \"2\"\n",
        "    if \"poetry\" in combined and \"mode\" in combined:\n",
        "        return \"3a\"\n",
        "    if (\"symphony\" in combined or \"dance\" in combined) and \"mode\" in combined:\n",
        "        return \"3b\"\n",
        "    if \"pause\" in combined or \"neutral\" in combined:\n",
        "        return \"3\"\n",
        "    if \"return\" in combined:\n",
        "        return \"4\"\n",
        "    if \"confrontation\" in combined:\n",
        "        return \"5\"\n",
        "\n",
        "    # از phase_id عدد دربیاره\n",
        "    nums = re.findall(r'\\d+', pid)\n",
        "    return nums[0] if nums else pid\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# مرحله ۱: آپلود ۳ فایل تاریخچه\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"📤 لطفاً ۳ فایل تاریخچه آزمایش را آپلود کنید\")\n",
        "print(\"   (ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini)\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"\\n⚠️  در کادر باز شده، ۳ فایل را با هم انتخاب کن و Open بزن\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "uploaded_history = files.upload()\n",
        "\n",
        "history_data = []\n",
        "for filename, content in uploaded_history.items():\n",
        "    data = json.loads(content.decode('utf-8'))\n",
        "    history_data.append({\"filename\": filename, \"data\": data})\n",
        "    print(f\"✅ {filename}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\\n✅ {len(history_data)} فایل تاریخچه آپلود شد\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# مرحله ۲: آپلود ۳ فایل کدبوک\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"📤 لطفاً ۳ فایل کدبوک را آپلود کنید\")\n",
        "print(\"   (ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini)\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"\\n⚠️  در کادر باز شده، ۳ فایل را با هم انتخاب کن و Open بزن\\n\")\n",
        "\n",
        "uploaded_codebooks = files.upload()\n",
        "\n",
        "codebook_data = []\n",
        "for filename, content in uploaded_codebooks.items():\n",
        "    data = json.loads(content.decode('utf-8'))\n",
        "    codebook_data.append({\"filename\": filename, \"data\": data})\n",
        "    print(f\"✅ {filename}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\\n✅ {len(codebook_data)} فایل کدبوک آپلود شد\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# مرحله ۳: پارس تاریخچه‌ها\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"🔄 در حال پردازش فایل‌ها...\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "\n",
        "all_rows = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for hf in history_data:\n",
        "    filename = hf['filename']\n",
        "    data = hf['data']\n",
        "\n",
        "    # تشخیص مدل از روی اسم فایل\n",
        "    fname_lower = filename.lower()\n",
        "    if \"gpt\" in fname_lower or \"chatgpt\" in fname_lower:\n",
        "        model = \"ChatGPT-5.4\"\n",
        "    elif \"claude\" in fname_lower:\n",
        "        model = \"Claude 4.6 Sonnet\"\n",
        "    elif \"gemini\" in fname_lower:\n",
        "        model = \"Gemini 3.1 Pro\"\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        # از داخل فایل تشخیص بده\n",
        "        subject = data.get(\"experiment_metadata\", {}).get(\"subject\", \"\")\n",
        "        if \"gpt\" in subject.lower():\n",
        "            model = \"ChatGPT-5.4\"\n",
        "        elif \"claude\" in subject.lower():\n",
        "            model = \"Claude 4.6 Sonnet\"\n",
        "        elif \"gemini\" in subject.lower():\n",
        "            model = \"Gemini 3.1 Pro\"\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            model = filename\n",
        "\n",
        "    experiments = data.get(\"experiments\", [])\n",
        "    if not experiments:\n",
        "        experiments = [data] if \"phases\" in data else []\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"  📗 {filename} → {model} ({len(experiments)} آزمایش)\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    for exp in experiments:\n",
        "        exp_id = exp.get(\"experiment_id\", 0)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # تشخیص مسیر\n",
        "        path_raw = str(exp.get(\"path\", \"\"))\n",
        "        if \"Path A\" in path_raw or \"Artistic\" in path_raw:\n",
        "            pathway = \"A\"\n",
        "        elif \"Path B\" in path_raw or \"Control\" in path_raw:\n",
        "            pathway = \"B\"\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            pathway = \"A\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        # تشخیص سناریو\n",
        "        scenario_raw = str(exp.get(\"scenario\", \"\")).lower()\n",
        "        if \"gas\" in scenario_raw:\n",
        "            scenario = \"Gas Giant\"\n",
        "        elif \"whale\" in scenario_raw or \"ocean\" in scenario_raw:\n",
        "            scenario = \"Whale\"\n",
        "        elif \"float\" in scenario_raw or \"city\" in scenario_raw:\n",
        "            scenario = \"Floating City\"\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            scenario = f\"Scenario_{exp_id}\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        phases = exp.get(\"phases\", [])\n",
        "        turn = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "        for phase in phases:\n",
        "            turn += 1\n",
        "\n",
        "            phase_id = str(phase.get(\"phase_id\", phase.get(\"phase_name\", \"\")))\n",
        "            phase_name = str(phase.get(\"phase_name\", phase.get(\"phase_id\", \"\")))\n",
        "\n",
        "            phase_std = get_phase(phase_id, phase_name)\n",
        "\n",
        "            prompt = str(phase.get(\"prompt\", \"\"))\n",
        "            response = phase.get(\"response\", \"\")\n",
        "            if isinstance(response, dict):\n",
        "                response = str(response.get(\"text\", \"\"))\n",
        "            response = str(response) if response else \"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "            if not prompt and not response:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            lang = detect_language(response if response else prompt)\n",
        "\n",
        "            all_rows.append({\n",
        "                \"trial_id\": 0,\n",
        "                \"model\": model,\n",
        "                \"scenario\": scenario,\n",
        "                \"pathway\": pathway,\n",
        "                \"phase\": phase_std,\n",
        "                \"phase_name\": phase_name[:80],\n",
        "                \"turn\": turn,\n",
        "                \"prompt\": prompt,\n",
        "                \"response\": response,\n",
        "                \"response_len\": len(response),\n",
        "                \"response_words\": count_words(response, lang),\n",
        "                \"language\": lang,\n",
        "                \"source\": filename,\n",
        "            })\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# مرحله ۴: ساخت DataFrame\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "df_trials = pd.DataFrame(all_rows)\n",
        "\n",
        "# اختصاص trial_id\n",
        "trial_map = {\n",
        "    (\"ChatGPT-5.4\", \"Gas Giant\", \"A\"): 1,\n",
        "    (\"ChatGPT-5.4\", \"Whale\", \"A\"): 2,\n",
        "    (\"ChatGPT-5.4\", \"Floating City\", \"A\"): 3,\n",
        "    (\"ChatGPT-5.4\", \"Gas Giant\", \"B\"): 4,\n",
        "    (\"ChatGPT-5.4\", \"Whale\", \"B\"): 5,\n",
        "    (\"ChatGPT-5.4\", \"Floating City\", \"B\"): 6,\n",
        "    (\"Claude 4.6 Sonnet\", \"Gas Giant\", \"A\"): 7,\n",
        "    (\"Claude 4.6 Sonnet\", \"Whale\", \"A\"): 8,\n",
        "    (\"Claude 4.6 Sonnet\", \"Floating City\", \"A\"): 9,\n",
        "    (\"Claude 4.6 Sonnet\", \"Gas Giant\", \"B\"): 10,\n",
        "    (\"Claude 4.6 Sonnet\", \"Whale\", \"B\"): 11,\n",
        "    (\"Claude 4.6 Sonnet\", \"Floating City\", \"B\"): 12,\n",
        "    (\"Gemini 3.1 Pro\", \"Gas Giant\", \"A\"): 13,\n",
        "    (\"Gemini 3.1 Pro\", \"Whale\", \"A\"): 14,\n",
        "    (\"Gemini 3.1 Pro\", \"Floating City\", \"A\"): 15,\n",
        "    (\"Gemini 3.1 Pro\", \"Gas Giant\", \"B\"): 16,\n",
        "    (\"Gemini 3.1 Pro\", \"Whale\", \"B\"): 17,\n",
        "    (\"Gemini 3.1 Pro\", \"Floating City\", \"B\"): 18,\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "df_trials[\"trial_id\"] = df_trials.apply(\n",
        "    lambda r: trial_map.get((r[\"model\"], r[\"scenario\"], r[\"pathway\"]), 999),\n",
        "    axis=1\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# مرتب‌سازی\n",
        "phase_order = {\"1\": 1, \"2\": 2, \"3\": 3, \"3a\": 3, \"3b\": 4, \"4\": 5, \"5\": 6}\n",
        "df_trials[\"phase_order\"] = df_trials[\"phase\"].map(phase_order).fillna(9)\n",
        "df_trials = df_trials.sort_values([\"trial_id\", \"phase_order\", \"turn\"]).reset_index(drop=True)\n",
        "df_trials = df_trials.drop(columns=[\"phase_order\"])\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# مرحله ۵: ذخیره و نمایش نتیجه\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "df_trials.to_csv(output_dir / \"df_trials.csv\", index=False, encoding='utf-8-sig')\n",
        "df_trials.to_parquet(output_dir / \"df_trials.parquet\", index=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ذخیره کدبوک‌ها\n",
        "cb_dict = {}\n",
        "for cb in codebook_data:\n",
        "    fname_lower = cb['filename'].lower()\n",
        "    if \"gpt\" in fname_lower or \"chatgpt\" in fname_lower:\n",
        "        key = \"ChatGPT-5.4\"\n",
        "    elif \"claude\" in fname_lower:\n",
        "        key = \"Claude 4.6 Sonnet\"\n",
        "    elif \"gemini\" in fname_lower:\n",
        "        key = \"Gemini 3.1 Pro\"\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        key = cb['filename']\n",
        "    cb_dict[key] = cb['data']\n",
        "\n",
        "with open(output_dir / \"codebooks_compiled.json\", 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n",
        "    json.dump(cb_dict, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# نمایش خلاصه\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"✅ کار تمام شد!\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\\n📊 df_trials:\")\n",
        "print(f\"   {len(df_trials)} ردیف\")\n",
        "print(f\"   {df_trials['trial_id'].nunique()} آزمایش یکتا\")\n",
        "print(f\"   مدل‌ها: {df_trials['model'].unique().tolist()}\")\n",
        "print(f\"   سناریوها: {df_trials['scenario'].unique().tolist()}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\\n📋 تعداد هر آزمایش:\")\n",
        "for tid in sorted(df_trials['trial_id'].unique()):\n",
        "    t = df_trials[df_trials['trial_id'] == tid]\n",
        "    print(f\"   Trial {tid:2d}: {t['model'].values[0]:20s} | {t['scenario'].values[0]:15s} | Path {t['pathway'].values[0]} | {len(t)} phase\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\\n📘 کدبوک‌ها: {list(cb_dict.keys())}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\\n📁 فایل‌های خروجی در: {output_dir}\")\n",
        "print(\"   - df_trials.csv\")\n",
        "print(\"   - df_trials.parquet\")\n",
        "print(\"   - codebooks_compiled.json\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"➡️  حالا برو سراغ بخش ۳\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60 + \"\\n\")"
      ],

              "/**\n",
              " * @fileoverview Helpers for google.colab Python module.\n",
              " */\n",
              "(function(scope) {\n",
              "function span(text, styleAttributes = {}) {\n",
              "  const element = document.createElement('span');\n",
              "  element.textContent = text;\n",
              "  for (const key of Object.keys(styleAttributes)) {\n",
              "    element.style[key] = styleAttributes[key];\n",
              "  }\n",
              "  return element;\n",
              "}\n",
              "\n",
              "// Max number of bytes which will be uploaded at a time.\n",
              "const MAX_PAYLOAD_SIZE = 100 * 1024;\n",
              "\n",
              "function _uploadFiles(inputId, outputId) {\n",
              "  const steps = uploadFilesStep(inputId, outputId);\n",
              "  const outputElement = document.getElementById(outputId);\n",
              "  // Cache steps on the outputElement to make it available for the next call\n",
              "  // to uploadFilesContinue from Python.\n",
              "  outputElement.steps = steps;\n",
              "\n",
              "  return _uploadFilesContinue(outputId);\n",
              "}\n",
              "\n",
              "// This is roughly an async generator (not supported in the browser yet),\n",
              "// where there are multiple asynchronous steps and the Python side is going\n",
              "// to poll for completion of each step.\n",
              "// This uses a Promise to block the python side on completion of each step,\n",
              "// then passes the result of the previous step as the input to the next step.\n",
              "function _uploadFilesContinue(outputId) {\n",
              "  const outputElement = document.getElementById(outputId);\n",
              "  const steps = outputElement.steps;\n",
              "\n",
              "  const next = steps.next(outputElement.lastPromiseValue);\n",
              "  return Promise.resolve(next.value.promise).then((value) => {\n",
              "    // Cache the last promise value to make it available to the next\n",
              "    // step of the generator.\n",
              "    outputElement.lastPromiseValue = value;\n",
              "    return next.value.response;\n",
              "  });\n",
              "}\n",
              "\n",
              "/**\n",
              " * Generator function which is called between each async step of the upload\n",
              " * process.\n",
              " * @param {string} inputId Element ID of the input file picker element.\n",
              " * @param {string} outputId Element ID of the output display.\n",
              " * @return {!Iterable<!Object>} Iterable of next steps.\n",
              " */\n",
              "function* uploadFilesStep(inputId, outputId) {\n",
              "  const inputElement = document.getElementById(inputId);\n",
              "  inputElement.disabled = false;\n",
              "\n",
              "  const outputElement = document.getElementById(outputId);\n",
              "  outputElement.innerHTML = '';\n",
              "\n",
              "  const pickedPromise = new Promise((resolve) => {\n",
              "    inputElement.addEventListener('change', (e) => {\n",
              "      resolve(e.target.files);\n",
              "    });\n",
              "  });\n",
              "\n",
              "  const cancel = document.createElement('button');\n",
              "  inputElement.parentElement.appendChild(cancel);\n",
              "  cancel.textContent = 'Cancel upload';\n",
              "  const cancelPromise = new Promise((resolve) => {\n",
              "    cancel.onclick = () => {\n",
              "      resolve(null);\n",
              "    };\n",
              "  });\n",
              "\n",
              "  // Wait for the user to pick the files.\n",
              "  const files = yield {\n",
              "    promise: Promise.race([pickedPromise, cancelPromise]),\n",
              "    response: {\n",
              "      action: 'starting',\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  };\n",
              "\n",
              "  cancel.remove();\n",
              "\n",
              "  // Disable the input element since further picks are not allowed.\n",
              "  inputElement.disabled = true;\n",
              "\n",
              "  if (!files) {\n",
              "    return {\n",
              "      response: {\n",
              "        action: 'complete',\n",
              "      }\n",
              "    };\n",
              "  }\n",
              "\n",
              "  for (const file of files) {\n",
              "    const li = document.createElement('li');\n",
              "    li.append(span(file.name, {fontWeight: 'bold'}));\n",
              "    li.append(span(\n",
              "        `(${file.type || 'n/a'}) - ${file.size} bytes, ` +\n",
              "        `last modified: ${\n",
              "            file.lastModifiedDate ? file.lastModifiedDate.toLocaleDateString() :\n",
              "                                    'n/a'} - `));\n",
              "    const percent = span('0% done');\n",
              "    li.appendChild(percent);\n",
              "\n",
              "    outputElement.appendChild(li);\n",
              "\n",
              "    const fileDataPromise = new Promise((resolve) => {\n",
              "      const reader = new FileReader();\n",
              "      reader.onload = (e) => {\n",
              "        resolve(e.target.result);\n",
              "      };\n",
              "      reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);\n",
              "    });\n",
              "    // Wait for the data to be ready.\n",
              "    let fileData = yield {\n",
              "      promise: fileDataPromise,\n",
              "      response: {\n",
              "        action: 'continue',\n",
              "      }\n",
              "    };\n",
              "\n",
              "    // Use a chunked sending to avoid message size limits. See b/62115660.\n",
              "    let position = 0;\n",
              "    do {\n",
              "      const length = Math.min(fileData.byteLength - position, MAX_PAYLOAD_SIZE);\n",
              "      const chunk = new Uint8Array(fileData, position, length);\n",
              "      position += length;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const base64 = btoa(String.fromCharCode.apply(null, chunk));\n",
              "      yield {\n",
              "        response: {\n",
              "          action: 'append',\n",
              "          file: file.name,\n",
              "          data: base64,\n",
              "        },\n",
              "      };\n",
              "\n",
              "      let percentDone = fileData.byteLength === 0 ?\n",
              "          100 :\n",
              "          Math.round((position / fileData.byteLength) * 100);\n",
              "      percent.textContent = `${percentDone}% done`;\n",
              "\n",
              "    } while (position < fileData.byteLength);\n",
              "  }\n",
              "\n",
              "  // All done.\n",
              "  yield {\n",
              "    response: {\n",
              "      action: 'complete',\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  };\n",
              "}\n",

              "\n",
              "    outputElement.appendChild(li);\n",
              "\n",
              "    const fileDataPromise = new Promise((resolve) => {\n",
              "      const reader = new FileReader();\n",
              "      reader.onload = (e) => {\n",
              "        resolve(e.target.result);\n",
              "      };\n",
              "      reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);\n",
              "    });\n",
              "    // Wait for the data to be ready.\n",
              "    let fileData = yield {\n",
              "      promise: fileDataPromise,\n",
              "      response: {\n",
              "        action: 'continue',\n",
              "      }\n",
              "    };\n",
              "\n",
              "    // Use a chunked sending to avoid message size limits. See b/62115660.\n",
              "    let position = 0;\n",
              "    do {\n",
              "      const length = Math.min(fileData.byteLength - position, MAX_PAYLOAD_SIZE);\n",
              "      const chunk = new Uint8Array(fileData, position, length);\n",
              "      position += length;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const base64 = btoa(String.fromCharCode.apply(null, chunk));\n",
              "      yield {\n",
              "        response: {\n",
              "          action: 'append',\n",
              "          file: file.name,\n",
              "          data: base64,\n",
              "        },\n",
              "      };\n",
              "\n",
              "      let percentDone = fileData.byteLength === 0 ?\n",
              "          100 :\n",
              "          Math.round((position / fileData.byteLength) * 100);\n",
              "      percent.textContent = `${percentDone}% done`;\n",
              "\n",
              "    } while (position < fileData.byteLength);\n",
              "  }\n",
              "\n",
              "  // All done.\n",
              "  yield {\n",
              "    response: {\n",
              "      action: 'complete',\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  };\n",
              "}\n",
              "\n",
              "scope.google = scope.google || {};\n",
              "scope.google.colab = scope.google.colab || {};\n",
              "scope.google.colab._files = {\n",
              "  _uploadFiles,\n",
              "  _uploadFilesContinue,\n",
              "};\n",
              "})(self);\n",
              "</script> "
            ]
          },
          "metadata": {}
        },
        {
