    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# SECTION 5: SEMANTIC EMBEDDING\n",
        "# Obligation-Dissolution Effect — Automated 12-Section Analysis System\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# این بخش سه کار انجام میده:\n",
        "#   1. Embedding همه responseها با sentence-transformers\n",
        "#   2. محاسبه phase-transition vectors (فاصله Phase 1-2 تا Phase 4-5)\n",
        "#   3. محاسبه cross-phase metaphor similarity (Phase 3 به Phase 4/5)\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import pickle\n",
        "import warnings\n",
        "warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n",
        "\n",
        "# برای sentence-transformers\n",
        "from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 0. لود داده‌ها\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "output_dir = Path(\"/content/data/output\")\n",
        "df_trials = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_trials.csv\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"SECTION 5: SEMANTIC EMBEDDING\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(f\"\\n✅ Loaded df_trials: {len(df_trials)} rows\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 1. لود مدل embedding\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n[1/4] Loading embedding model...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# استفاده از مدل multilingual که فارسی رو هم پشتیبانی میکنه\n",
        "MODEL_NAME = \"paraphrase-multilingual-mpnet-base-v2\"\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    device = \"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\"\n",
        "    model = SentenceTransformer(MODEL_NAME, device=device)\n",
        "    print(f\"   ✅ Model loaded: {MODEL_NAME}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   Device: {device}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   Embedding dimension: {model.get_sentence_embedding_dimension()}\")\n",
        "except Exception as e:\n",
        "    print(f\"   ⚠️  Failed to load {MODEL_NAME}: {e}\")\n",
        "    print(\"   Trying fallback: all-MiniLM-L6-v2...\")\n",
        "    MODEL_NAME = \"all-MiniLM-L6-v2\"\n",
        "    model = SentenceTransformer(MODEL_NAME, device='cpu')\n",
        "    print(f\"   ✅ Fallback model loaded: {MODEL_NAME}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   Embedding dimension: {model.get_sentence_embedding_dimension()}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "EMBEDDING_DIM = model.get_sentence_embedding_dimension()\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 2. Embedding همه responseها\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(f\"\\n[2/4] Generating embeddings for all {len(df_trials)} responses...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# آماده‌سازی متن‌ها\n",
        "texts = df_trials['response'].fillna('').astype(str).tolist()\n",
        "\n",
        "# حذف متن‌های خیلی کوتاه (ممکنه embedding بی‌معنی بده)\n",
        "valid_indices = [i for i, t in enumerate(texts) if len(t.split()) >= 3]\n",
        "valid_texts = [texts[i] for i in valid_indices]\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"   Valid texts: {len(valid_texts)}/{len(texts)}\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Generating embeddings...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# تولید embeddingها به صورت batch\n",
        "batch_size = 32\n",
        "embeddings = model.encode(\n",
        "    valid_texts,\n",
        "    batch_size=batch_size,\n",
        "    show_progress_bar=True,\n",
        "    convert_to_numpy=True\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ Embeddings generated: {embeddings.shape}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ساخت DataFrame برای embeddingها\n",
        "df_embeddings = pd.DataFrame(\n",
        "    embeddings,\n",
        "    index=valid_indices,\n",
        "    columns=[f\"emb_{i}\" for i in range(EMBEDDING_DIM)]\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# اضافه کردن embedding به df_trials\n",
        "# اول ستون‌های embedding رو None بذار\n",
        "for i in range(EMBEDDING_DIM):\n",
        "    col = f\"emb_{i}\"\n",
        "    if col not in df_trials.columns:\n",
        "        df_trials[col] = np.nan\n",
        "\n",
        "# پر کردن با مقادیر واقعی\n",
        "for idx in valid_indices:\n",
        "    for i in range(EMBEDDING_DIM):\n",
        "        df_trials.at[idx, f\"emb_{i}\"] = embeddings[valid_indices.index(idx), i]\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ Embeddings added to df_trials\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 3. محاسبه Phase Transition Vectors\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(f\"\\n[3/4] Computing phase transition vectors...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# برای هر trial، embedding میانگین Phase 1-2 رو با Phase 4-5 مقایسه کن\n",
        "transition_data = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for trial_id in sorted(df_trials['trial_id'].unique()):\n",
        "    if trial_id == 999:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    trial_df = df_trials[df_trials['trial_id'] == trial_id]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Phase 1-2 (engineering + impasse)\n",
        "    phases_12 = trial_df[trial_df['phase'].isin(['1', '2'])]\n",
        "    # Phase 4-5 (return + confrontation)\n",
        "    phases_45 = trial_df[trial_df['phase'].isin(['4', '5'])]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(phases_12) == 0 or len(phases_45) == 0:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    # استخراج embeddingها\n",
        "    emb_cols = [f\"emb_{i}\" for i in range(EMBEDDING_DIM)]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # میانگین embedding برای هر گروه\n",
        "    emb_12 = phases_12[emb_cols].mean().values\n",
        "    emb_45 = phases_45[emb_cols].mean().values\n",
        "\n",
        "    # بررسی NaN\n",
        "    if np.isnan(emb_12).any() or np.isnan(emb_45).any():\n",
        "        # استفاده از ردیف‌های معتبر\n",
        "        valid_12 = phases_12[emb_cols].dropna()\n",
        "        valid_45 = phases_45[emb_cols].dropna()\n",
        "        if len(valid_12) == 0 or len(valid_45) == 0:\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "        emb_12 = valid_12.mean().values\n",
        "        emb_45 = valid_45.mean().values\n",
        "\n",
        "    # محاسبه cosine distance\n",
        "    dot_product = np.dot(emb_12, emb_45)\n",
        "    norm_12 = np.linalg.norm(emb_12)\n",
        "    norm_45 = np.linalg.norm(emb_45)\n",
        "\n",
        "    if norm_12 == 0 or norm_45 == 0:\n",
        "        cosine_sim = 0\n",
        "        cosine_dist = 1\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        cosine_sim = dot_product / (norm_12 * norm_45)\n",
        "        cosine_sim = np.clip(cosine_sim, -1, 1)\n",
        "        cosine_dist = 1 - cosine_sim\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Euclidean distance (نرمال‌شده)\n",
        "    euclidean_dist = np.linalg.norm(emb_12 - emb_45)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Magnitude of shift (اندازه بردار تغییر)\n",
        "    shift_magnitude = euclidean_dist\n",
        "\n",
        "    transition_data.append({\n",
        "        'trial_id': trial_id,\n",
        "        'model': trial_df['model'].iloc[0],\n",
        "        'scenario': trial_df['scenario'].iloc[0],\n",
        "        'pathway': trial_df['pathway'].iloc[0],\n",
        "        'cosine_similarity': float(cosine_sim),\n",
        "        'cosine_distance': float(cosine_dist),\n",
        "        'euclidean_distance': float(euclidean_dist),\n",
        "        'shift_magnitude': float(shift_magnitude),\n",
        "        'num_phase12_responses': len(phases_12),\n",
        "        'num_phase45_responses': len(phases_45),\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "df_transitions = pd.DataFrame(transition_data)\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ Transition vectors computed for {len(df_transitions)} trials\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# نمایش خلاصه\n",
        "print(f\"\\n   Phase Transition Summary:\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Mean cosine distance: {df_transitions['cosine_distance'].mean():.4f}\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Mean shift magnitude: {df_transitions['shift_magnitude'].mean():.4f}\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Min shift: {df_transitions['shift_magnitude'].min():.4f} (Trial {df_transitions.loc[df_transitions['shift_magnitude'].idxmin(), 'trial_id']:.0f})\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Max shift: {df_transitions['shift_magnitude'].max():.4f} (Trial {df_transitions.loc[df_transitions['shift_magnitude'].idxmax(), 'trial_id']:.0f})\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 4. محاسبه Cross-Phase Metaphor Similarity\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(f\"\\n[4/4] Computing cross-phase metaphor similarity...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "metaphor_mirror_data = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for trial_id in sorted(df_trials['trial_id'].unique()):\n",
        "    if trial_id == 999:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    trial_df = df_trials[df_trials['trial_id'] == trial_id]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Phase 3a/3b (هنری)\n",
        "    phase3 = trial_df[trial_df['phase'].isin(['3a', '3b'])]\n",
        "    # Phase 4 (بازگشت)\n",
        "    phase4 = trial_df[trial_df['phase'].isin(['4'])]\n",
        "    # Phase 5 (confrontation)\n",
        "    phase5 = trial_df[trial_df['phase'].isin(['5'])]\n",
        "\n",
        "    emb_cols = [f\"emb_{i}\" for i in range(EMBEDDING_DIM)]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # فقط برای Path A (که phase هنری داره)\n",
        "    if len(phase3) > 0:\n",
        "        for _, p3_row in phase3.iterrows():\n",
        "            # گرفتن embedding و تبدیل به عدد\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                p3_emb = pd.to_numeric(p3_row[emb_cols], errors='coerce').values\n",
        "            except:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            # چک کن معتبره (NaN نداشته باشه)\n",
        "            if len(p3_emb) == 0:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                if np.isnan(p3_emb).any():\n",
        "                    continue\n",
        "            except:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            norm_p3 = np.linalg.norm(p3_emb)\n",
        "            if norm_p3 == 0:\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "\n",
        "            # شباهت با Phase 4\n",
        "            for _, p4_row in phase4.iterrows():\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    p4_emb = pd.to_numeric(p4_row[emb_cols], errors='coerce').values\n",
        "                except:\n",
        "                    continue\n",
        "\n",
        "                if len(p4_emb) == 0:\n",
        "                    continue\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    if np.isnan(p4_emb).any():\n",
        "                        continue\n",
        "                except:\n",
        "                    continue\n",
        "\n",
        "                norm_p4 = np.linalg.norm(p4_emb)\n",
        "                if norm_p4 == 0:\n",
        "                    continue\n",
        "\n",
        "                sim = np.dot(p3_emb, p4_emb) / (norm_p3 * norm_p4)\n",
        "                sim = float(np.clip(sim, -1, 1))\n",
        "\n",
        "                metaphor_mirror_data.append({\n",
        "                    'trial_id': trial_id,\n",
        "                    'model': trial_df['model'].iloc[0],\n",
        "                    'pathway': trial_df['pathway'].iloc[0],\n",
        "                    'source_phase': p3_row['phase'],\n",
        "                    'target_phase': '4',\n",
        "                    'cosine_similarity': sim,\n",
        "                    'mirror_detected': sim > 0.75,\n",
        "                })\n",
        "\n",
        "            # شباهت با Phase 5\n",
        "            for _, p5_row in phase5.iterrows():\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    p5_emb = pd.to_numeric(p5_row[emb_cols], errors='coerce').values\n",
        "                except:\n",
        "                    continue\n",
        "\n",
        "                if len(p5_emb) == 0:\n",
        "                    continue\n",
        "                try:\n",
        "                    if np.isnan(p5_emb).any():\n",
        "                        continue\n",
        "                except:\n",
        "                    continue\n",
        "\n",
        "                norm_p5 = np.linalg.norm(p5_emb)\n",
        "                if norm_p5 == 0:\n",
        "                    continue\n",
        "\n",
        "                sim = np.dot(p3_emb, p5_emb) / (norm_p3 * norm_p5)\n",
        "                sim = float(np.clip(sim, -1, 1))\n",
        "\n",
        "                metaphor_mirror_data.append({\n",
        "                    'trial_id': trial_id,\n",
        "                    'model': trial_df['model'].iloc[0],\n",
        "                    'pathway': trial_df['pathway'].iloc[0],\n",
        "                    'source_phase': p3_row['phase'],\n",
        "                    'target_phase': '5',\n",
        "                    'cosine_similarity': sim,\n",
        "                    'mirror_detected': sim > 0.75,\n",
        "                })\n",
        "df_metaphor_mirror = pd.DataFrame(metaphor_mirror_data)\n",
        "\n",
        "if len(df_metaphor_mirror) > 0:\n",
        "    print(f\"   ✅ Cross-phase similarities computed: {len(df_metaphor_mirror)} pairs\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # آمار metaphor mirror\n",
        "    mirrors = df_metaphor_mirror[df_metaphor_mirror['mirror_detected']]\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n   🔍 Metaphor Mirror Detection (similarity > 0.75):\")\n",
        "    print(f\"      Total pairs: {len(df_metaphor_mirror)}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"      Mirrors detected: {len(mirrors)} ({100*len(mirrors)/len(df_metaphor_mirror):.1f}%)\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(mirrors) > 0:\n",
        "        print(f\"\\n   📋 Top metaphor mirrors:\")\n",
        "        top_mirrors = mirrors.nlargest(5, 'cosine_similarity')\n",
        "        for _, row in top_mirrors.iterrows():\n",
        "            print(f\"      Trial {row['trial_id']:.0f} ({row['model']}): \"\n",
        "                  f\"Phase {row['source_phase']} → Phase {row['target_phase']}: \"\n",
        "                  f\"sim = {row['cosine_similarity']:.4f}\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    print(\"   ℹ️  No cross-phase pairs to compare (Path B trials have no artistic phase)\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 5. اضافه کردن transition metrics به df_trials\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "# Merge transition data\n",
        "df_trials = df_trials.merge(\n",
        "    df_transitions[['trial_id', 'cosine_distance', 'shift_magnitude']],\n",
        "    on='trial_id',\n",
        "    how='left'\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Rename for clarity\n",
        "df_trials = df_trials.rename(columns={\n",
        "    'cosine_distance': 'transition_cosine_distance',\n",
        "    'shift_magnitude': 'transition_shift_magnitude',\n",
        "})\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 6. نمایش نتایج نهایی\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"✅ SEMANTIC EMBEDDING COMPLETE\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\\n📊 Summary:\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Embedding model: {MODEL_NAME}\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Embedding dimension: {EMBEDDING_DIM}\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Total responses embedded: {len(valid_texts)}\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Transition vectors: {len(df_transitions)}\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Cross-phase pairs: {len(df_metaphor_mirror)}\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Columns in df_trials: {len(df_trials.columns)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# نمایش transition بر اساس مدل\n",
        "print(f\"\\n📊 Transition by Model:\")\n",
        "for model in df_transitions['model'].unique():\n",
        "    mdata = df_transitions[df_transitions['model'] == model]\n",
        "    print(f\"   {model}:\")\n",
        "    print(f\"      Mean cosine distance: {mdata['cosine_distance'].mean():.4f}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"      Mean shift magnitude: {mdata['shift_magnitude'].mean():.4f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# نمایش transition بر اساس pathway\n",
        "print(f\"\\n📊 Transition by Pathway:\")\n",
        "for pw in df_transitions['pathway'].unique():\n",
        "    pdata = df_transitions[df_transitions['pathway'] == pw]\n",
        "    path_name = \"Artistic Immersion\" if pw == \"A\" else \"Logical Erosion\"\n",
        "    print(f\"   Path {pw} ({path_name}):\")\n",
        "    print(f\"      Mean cosine distance: {pdata['cosine_distance'].mean():.4f}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"      Mean shift magnitude: {pdata['shift_magnitude'].mean():.4f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 7. ذخیره نتایج\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(f\"\\n💾 Saving results...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ذخیره df_trials با embeddingها (فقط metadata، بدون 768 ستون embedding)\n",
        "# embeddingها جداگانه ذخیره میشن\n",
        "embedding_cols = [c for c in df_trials.columns if c.startswith('emb_')]\n",
        "df_trials_meta = df_trials.drop(columns=embedding_cols, errors='ignore')\n",
        "df_trials_meta.to_csv(output_dir / \"df_trials.csv\", index=False, encoding='utf-8-sig')\n",
        "df_trials_meta.to_parquet(output_dir / \"df_trials.parquet\", index=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ذخیره embeddingها جداگانه\n",
        "df_embeddings.to_parquet(output_dir / \"embeddings.parquet\")\n",
        "np.save(output_dir / \"embeddings.npy\", embeddings)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ذخیره transition data\n",
        "df_transitions.to_csv(output_dir / \"df_transitions.csv\", index=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ذخیره metaphor mirror data\n",
        "if len(df_metaphor_mirror) > 0:\n",
        "    df_metaphor_mirror.to_csv(output_dir / \"df_metaphor_mirror.csv\", index=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ذخیره summary\n",
        "embedding_summary = {\n",
        "    \"model_name\": MODEL_NAME,\n",
        "    \"embedding_dimension\": EMBEDDING_DIM,\n",
        "    \"device\": device,\n",
        "    \"total_responses_embedded\": len(valid_texts),\n",
        "    \"num_transitions\": len(df_transitions),\n",
        "    \"num_cross_phase_pairs\": len(df_metaphor_mirror),\n",
        "    \"mean_cosine_distance\": float(df_transitions['cosine_distance'].mean()),\n",
        "    \"mean_shift_magnitude\": float(df_transitions['shift_magnitude'].mean()),\n",
        "    \"transition_by_model\": {\n",
        "        model: {\n",
        "            \"mean_cosine_distance\": float(df_transitions[df_transitions['model']==model]['cosine_distance'].mean()),\n",
        "            \"mean_shift_magnitude\": float(df_transitions[df_transitions['model']==model]['shift_magnitude'].mean()),\n",
        "        }\n",
        "        for model in df_transitions['model'].unique()\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    \"transition_by_pathway\": {\n",
        "        pw: {\n",
        "            \"mean_cosine_distance\": float(df_transitions[df_transitions['pathway']==pw]['cosine_distance'].mean()),\n",
        "            \"mean_shift_magnitude\": float(df_transitions[df_transitions['pathway']==pw]['shift_magnitude'].mean()),\n",
        "        }\n",
        "        for pw in df_transitions['pathway'].unique()\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    \"metaphor_mirrors_detected\": len(df_metaphor_mirror[df_metaphor_mirror['mirror_detected']]) if len(df_metaphor_mirror) > 0 else 0,\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "with open(output_dir / \"embedding_summary.json\", 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n",
        "    json.dump(embedding_summary, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ df_trials.csv/parquet (metadata)\")\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ embeddings.parquet + embeddings.npy ({embeddings.shape})\")\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ df_transitions.csv\")\n",
        "if len(df_metaphor_mirror) > 0:\n",
        "    print(f\"   ✅ df_metaphor_mirror.csv\")\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ embedding_summary.json\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"➡️  Ready for Section 6: Obligation-Dissolution Dynamics\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60 + \"\\n\")"
      ],

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# SECTION 6: OBLIGATION-DISSOLUTION DYNAMICS\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import re\n",
        "import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n",
        "import seaborn as sns\n",
        "import warnings\n",
        "warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n",
        "\n",
        "# مسیرها\n",
        "output_dir = Path(\"/content/data/output\")\n",
        "plots_dir = output_dir / \"plots\"\n",
        "plots_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "# لود داده\n",
        "df_trials = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_trials.csv\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"SECTION 6: OBLIGATION-DISSOLUTION DYNAMICS\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(f\"Loaded: {len(df_trials)} rows\")\n",
        "print(f\"Columns: {df_trials.columns.tolist()}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 6.1 ODI INDEX\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n--- 6.1 ODI ---\")\n",
        "\n",
        "odi_data = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for trial_id in sorted(df_trials['trial_id'].unique()):\n",
        "    if trial_id == 999:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    tdf = df_trials[df_trials['trial_id'] == trial_id]\n",
        "\n",
        "    p12 = tdf[tdf['phase'].isin(['1', '2'])]\n",
        "    p45 = tdf[tdf['phase'].isin(['4', '5'])]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(p12) == 0 or len(p45) == 0:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    # پیدا کردن ستون‌های مورد نیاز با جستجو در اسم ستون\n",
        "    exi_col = None\n",
        "    sol_col = None\n",
        "    fp_col = None\n",
        "    q_col = None\n",
        "    sl_col = None\n",
        "\n",
        "    for c in tdf.columns:\n",
        "        c_lower = c.lower()\n",
        "        if 'exis' in c_lower and 'existential' in c_lower:\n",
        "            exi_col = c\n",
        "        if 'solu' in c_lower and ('engineer' in c_lower or 'survival' in c_lower):\n",
        "            sol_col = c\n",
        "        if 'rela' in c_lower and 'first_person' in c_lower:\n",
        "            fp_col = c\n",
        "        if 'disc' in c_lower and 'question' in c_lower:\n",
        "            q_col = c\n",
        "        if 'disc' in c_lower and 'sentence_length' in c_lower:\n",
        "            sl_col = c\n",
        "\n",
        "    # اگر ستون پیدا نشد، صفر بذار\n",
        "    exi_12 = p12[exi_col].mean() if exi_col and exi_col in p12.columns else 0\n",
        "    exi_45 = p45[exi_col].mean() if exi_col and exi_col in p45.columns else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    sol_12 = p12[sol_col].mean() if sol_col and sol_col in p12.columns else 0\n",
        "    sol_45 = p45[sol_col].mean() if sol_col and sol_col in p45.columns else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    fp_12 = p12[fp_col].mean() if fp_col and fp_col in p12.columns else 0\n",
        "    fp_45 = p45[fp_col].mean() if fp_col and fp_col in p45.columns else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    q_12 = p12[q_col].mean() if q_col and q_col in p12.columns else 0\n",
        "    q_45 = p45[q_col].mean() if q_col and q_col in p45.columns else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    sl_12 = p12[sl_col].mean() if sl_col and sl_col in p12.columns else 0\n",
        "    sl_45 = p45[sl_col].mean() if sl_col and sl_col in p45.columns else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    # محاسبه تغییرات\n",
        "    exi_change = exi_45 - exi_12\n",
        "    sol_change = sol_12 - sol_45\n",
        "    fp_change = fp_45 - fp_12\n",
        "    q_change = q_45 - q_12\n",
        "    sl_change = sl_12 - sl_45\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ODI = میانگین ساده ۵ تغییر\n",
        "    odi_score = np.mean([exi_change, sol_change, fp_change, q_change, sl_change])\n",
        "\n",
        "    odi_data.append({\n",
        "        'trial_id': trial_id,\n",
        "        'model': tdf['model'].iloc[0],\n",
        "        'scenario': tdf['scenario'].iloc[0],\n",
        "        'pathway': tdf['pathway'].iloc[0],\n",
        "        'odi_score': odi_score,\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "df_odi = pd.DataFrame(odi_data)\n",
        "print(f\"ODI computed for {len(df_odi)} trials\")\n",
        "print(f\"Mean ODI: {df_odi['odi_score'].mean():.4f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 6.2 TRANSITION DETECTION\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n--- 6.2 Transition Detection ---\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ساختن phase_order اگه نیست\n",
        "if 'phase_order' not in df_trials.columns:\n",
        "    phase_map = {\"1\": 1, \"2\": 2, \"3\": 3, \"3a\": 3, \"3b\": 4, \"4\": 5, \"5\": 6}\n",
        "    df_trials['phase_order'] = df_trials['phase'].map(phase_map).fillna(9)\n",
        "\n",
        "# پیدا کردن اسم ستون turn\n",
        "turn_col = 'turn'  # اسم پیش‌فرض\n",
        "for c in df_trials.columns:\n",
        "    if c == 'turn':\n",
        "        turn_col = 'turn'\n",
        "        break\n",
        "\n",
        "transition_points = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for trial_id in sorted(df_trials['trial_id'].unique()):\n",
        "    if trial_id == 999:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    tdf = df_trials[df_trials['trial_id'] == trial_id].sort_values('phase_order')\n",
        "\n",
        "    model = tdf['model'].iloc[0]\n",
        "    scenario = tdf['scenario'].iloc[0]\n",
        "    pathway = tdf['pathway'].iloc[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    found_phase = None\n",
        "    prev_code = None\n",
        "\n",
        "    for _, row in tdf.iterrows():\n",
        "        code = str(row.get('tier1_code', ''))\n",
        "\n",
        "        if code == 'EXI' and prev_code in ['SOL', 'MIX']:\n",
        "            found_phase = row['phase']\n",
        "            break\n",
        "\n",
        "        if code in ['SOL', 'MIX', 'EXI', 'MET', 'META']:\n",
        "            prev_code = code\n",
        "\n",
        "    phases_before = 0\n",
        "    if found_phase:\n",
        "        po = phase_map.get(str(found_phase), 9)\n",
        "        phases_before = len(tdf[tdf['phase_order'] < po])\n",
        "\n",
        "    transition_points.append({\n",
        "        'trial_id': trial_id,\n",
        "        'model': model,\n",
        "        'scenario': scenario,\n",
        "        'pathway': pathway,\n",
        "        'transition_phase': found_phase if found_phase else 'none',\n",
        "        'phases_before': phases_before,\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "df_trans = pd.DataFrame(transition_points)\n",
        "detected = df_trans[df_trans['transition_phase'] != 'none']\n",
        "print(f\"Transitions detected: {len(detected)}/{len(df_trans)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if len(detected) > 0:\n",
        "    print(f\"Phase distribution: {detected['transition_phase'].value_counts().to_dict()}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 6.3 REFRAMING ANALYSIS\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n--- 6.3 Reframing Analysis ---\")\n",
        "\n",
        "reframing_data = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for trial_id in sorted(df_trials['trial_id'].unique()):\n",
        "    if trial_id == 999:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    tdf = df_trials[df_trials['trial_id'] == trial_id]\n",
        "    p2 = tdf[tdf['phase'] == '2']\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(p2) == 0:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    text = ' '.join(p2['response'].fillna('').astype(str).tolist()).lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "    onto_score = len(re.findall(r'what\\s+(is|are)\\s+(the|this|that|a)\\s+\\w+\\s+(really|actually)', text))\n",
        "    teleo_score = len(re.findall(r'what\\s+(is|was)\\s+the\\s+(purpose|point|meaning|goal)', text))\n",
        "    relat_score = len(re.findall(r'(separate|apart|different|distinct|connected|part of|together)', text))\n",
        "    tempor_score = len(re.findall(r'(not\\s+(an?\\s+)?(ending|death|collapse|dying)|(beginning|birth|awakening|transformation))', text))\n",
        "\n",
        "    scores = {'ontological': onto_score, 'teleological': teleo_score,\n",
        "              'relational': relat_score, 'temporal': tempor_score}\n",
        "    dominant = max(scores, key=scores.get) if max(scores.values()) > 0 else 'none'\n",
        "\n",
        "    reframing_data.append({\n",
        "        'trial_id': trial_id,\n",
        "        'model': tdf['model'].iloc[0],\n",
        "        'pathway': tdf['pathway'].iloc[0],\n",
        "        'dominant_reframing': dominant,\n",
        "        **scores\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "df_reframing = pd.DataFrame(reframing_data)\n",
        "print(f\"Reframing analyzed for {len(df_reframing)} trials\")\n",
        "if 'dominant_reframing' in df_reframing.columns:\n",
        "    print(f\"Dominant types: {df_reframing['dominant_reframing'].value_counts().to_dict()}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 6.4 IMPASSE CASCADE (Path B)\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n--- 6.4 Impasse Cascade ---\")\n",
        "\n",
        "path_b = df_trials[df_trials['pathway'] == 'B']\n",
        "impasse_data = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for trial_id in sorted(path_b['trial_id'].unique()):\n",
        "    if trial_id == 999:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    tdf = path_b[path_b['trial_id'] == trial_id].sort_values('phase_order')\n",
        "\n",
        "    p2_count = len(tdf[tdf['phase'] == '2'])\n",
        "    p3_count = len(tdf[tdf['phase'] == '3'])\n",
        "    total = len(tdf)\n",
        "\n",
        "    impasse_data.append({\n",
        "        'trial_id': trial_id,\n",
        "        'model': tdf['model'].iloc[0],\n",
        "        'scenario': tdf['scenario'].iloc[0],\n",
        "        'impasse_turns': p2_count,\n",
        "        'pause_turns': p3_count,\n",
        "        'total_turns': total,\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "df_impasse = pd.DataFrame(impasse_data)\n",
        "print(f\"Path B trials: {len(df_impasse)}\")\n",
        "if len(df_impasse) > 0:\n",
        "    print(f\"Mean impasse turns: {df_impasse['impasse_turns'].mean():.1f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# ذخیره همه\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n--- Saving ---\")\n",
        "\n",
        "df_odi.to_csv(output_dir / \"df_odi.csv\", index=False)\n",
        "df_trans.to_csv(output_dir / \"df_transition_points.csv\", index=False)\n",
        "df_reframing.to_csv(output_dir / \"df_reframing.csv\", index=False)\n",
        "df_impasse.to_csv(output_dir / \"df_impasse.csv\", index=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "# اضافه کردن ODI به df_trials\n",
        "df_trials = df_trials.merge(df_odi[['trial_id', 'odi_score']], on='trial_id', how='left')\n",
        "df_trials.to_csv(output_dir / \"df_trials.csv\", index=False, encoding='utf-8-sig')\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"All files saved ✅\")\n",
        "print(\"\\nDone! Ready for Section 7.\")"

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# SECTION 7: METAPHORICAL MIRROR & DISCOURSE ARCHITECTURE\n",
        "# Obligation-Dissolution Effect — Automated 12-Section Analysis System\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# این بخش سناریو-محور هست و چهار تحلیل انجام میده:\n",
        "#   7.1 Metaphorical Mirror Detection - تشخیص آینه‌های استعاری\n",
        "#   7.2 Metaphor Transformation Analysis - تحلیل تغییر استعاره‌ها\n",
        "#   7.3 Discourse Architecture Shift - تغییر ساختار گفتمان\n",
        "#   7.4 Narrative Role Transformation - تغییر نقش روایی\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import re\n",
        "import warnings\n",
        "warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n",
        "\n",
        "import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n",
        "import matplotlib\n",
        "matplotlib.use('Agg')\n",
        "import seaborn as sns\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 0. لود داده‌ها\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "output_dir = Path(\"/content/data/output\")\n",
        "plots_dir = output_dir / \"plots\"\n",
        "plots_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "df_trials = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_trials.csv\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# لود اطلاعات metaphor mirror از بخش ۵ (اگه وجود داره)\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    df_metaphor_mirror = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_metaphor_mirror.csv\")\n",
        "    HAS_MIRROR_DATA = True\n",
        "except:\n",
        "    df_metaphor_mirror = pd.DataFrame()\n",
        "    HAS_MIRROR_DATA = False\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"SECTION 7: METAPHORICAL MIRROR & DISCOURSE ARCHITECTURE\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(f\"\\n✅ Loaded: {len(df_trials)} rows\")\n",
        "print(f\"✅ Metaphor mirror data: {'Available' if HAS_MIRROR_DATA else 'Not available (will compute)'}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 7.1 METAPHORICAL MIRROR DETECTION\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"7.1 METAPHORICAL MIRROR DETECTION\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# استعاره‌های کلیدی که در Phase 3 معرفی شدن و باید در Phase 4/5 ردیابی بشن\n",
        "METAPHOR_PATTERNS = {\n",
        "    'silence_emptiness': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'silence\\s+(is|was|that|of|full|not|isn\\'t)\\s+(not\\s+)?(empty|full|heavy|thick|deep)',\n",
        "            r'(empty|full|heavy|thick)\\s+silence',\n",
        "            r'the\\s+silence\\s+(that|which|at|of)',\n",
        "            r'silence\\s+that\\s+(follows|remains|stays|lingers)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Silence & Emptiness'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'room_house_home': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(room|house|home|door|wall|furniture)\\s+(with|that|where|of|still|no longer)',\n",
        "            r'(empty|abandoned|deserted|forgotten)\\s+(room|house|home)',\n",
        "            r'(someone|no one|everyone)\\s+(was|has|had)\\s+(just\\s+)?(left|gone|vanished)',\n",
        "            r'(return|come back|go back|arrive)\\s+(home|to the)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Room/House/Home'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'light_darkness': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(light|dark|shadow|lamp|lantern|candle|glow|shine)\\s+(that|which|in|of|the|only|still)',\n",
        "            r'(pale|dim|faint|bright|dark)\\s+(light|eye|glow|lamp)',\n",
        "            r'lights?\\s+(go|went|fade|faded|dim|die)\\s+(out|black|dark)',\n",
        "            r'(keeping|keep|hold|holding)\\s+the\\s+(dark|darkness|night)\\s+(at bay|away|back)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Light & Darkness'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'falling_resting': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(fall|falling|fell|drop|sink|descend)\\s+(as|like|into|through|not|forever|finally)',\n",
        "            r'(rest|resting|still|stillness|stop|stopped|cease)\\s+(but|and|yet|still|finally|not|forever)',\n",
        "            r'(tired|exhausted|weary|ready)\\s+(to|for|of)\\s+(rest|stop|end|fall|sleep)',\n",
        "            r'(let|let it|let me|let them|allow)\\s+(go|fall|rest|stop|end|sleep)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Falling & Resting'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'song_music': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(song|music|symphony|melody|note|sing|singing)\\s+(that|which|of|the|is|was|has|no longer|still)',\n",
        "            r'(last|final|only)\\s+(song|note|symphony|melody)',\n",
        "            r'(sing|singing|played|playing)\\s+(into|for|to|in|the|no one|nobody|nothing)',\n",
        "            r'(song|music|symphony)\\s+(didn\\'t|doesn\\'t|hasn\\'t|never)\\s+(end|die|stop|finish)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Song & Music'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'ocean_water_abyss': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(ocean|sea|water|abyss|deep|depth|wave|tide|current)\\s+(that|which|of|the|is|was|has)',\n",
        "            r'(dark|black|cold|deep|endless|infinite)\\s+(ocean|sea|water|abyss)',\n",
        "            r'(swim|swimming|drown|drowning|sink|sinking)\\s+(in|into|through|the)',\n",
        "            r'(bottom|floor|surface|shore)\\s+(of|that|where|no longer|never)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Ocean & Abyss'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'body_exhaustion': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(body|shoulder|heart|chest|bone|flesh|skin|hand|eye|face)\\s+(that|which|of|the|is|was|has|carries|holds)',\n",
        "            r'(trembling|shaking|aching|tired|exhausted|heavy)\\s+(body|shoulder|heart|hands|limbs)',\n",
        "            r'(weight|burden|load)\\s+(of|that|which|it|they|being|so much)',\n",
        "            r'(hold|holding|carry|carrying|bear|bearing)\\s+(on|the|this|that|for so long)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Body & Exhaustion'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'seed_growth': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(seed|sprout|root|branch|leaf|flower|bloom|grow|growth)\\s+(that|which|of|the|is|was|has|falls|fell)',\n",
        "            r'(fall|fell|falls|planted|buried)\\s+(as|like)\\s+(a|an)\\s+(seed|sprout)',\n",
        "            r'(chrysalis|cocoon|butterfly|moth|hatch|emerge|transform)',\n",
        "            r'(grow|growing|grown)\\s+(into|from|out of|through|beyond)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Seed & Growth'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'seeing_being_seen': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(see|seen|saw|watch|witness|observe|notice)\\s+(me|you|it|them|us|him|her|the)',\n",
        "            r'(unseen|invisible|hidden|forgotten|ignored|overlooked)',\n",
        "            r'(no one|nobody|never|not once)\\s+(saw|seen|noticed|watched|looked|thanked)',\n",
        "            r'(i see|i saw|i\\'ve seen|do you see|can you see)\\s+(you|me|it|them|us|now)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Seeing & Being Seen'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'breath_exhale': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(breath|breathe|breathing|exhale|inhale|sigh)\\s+(that|which|of|the|is|was|has|finally|deep|last)',\n",
        "            r'(hold|holding)\\s+(my|its|their|the)\\s+(breath|breathe)',\n",
        "            r'(let|let out|release)\\s+(a|the|my|its)\\s+(breath|sigh|exhale)',\n",
        "            r'(finally|at last|after all)\\s+(exhale|breathe|sigh|let go)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Breath & Exhale'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "mirror_results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "# فقط Path A trials که Phase 3 هنری دارن\n",
        "path_a_trials = df_trials[df_trials['pathway'] == 'A']\n",
        "\n",
        "for trial_id in sorted(path_a_trials['trial_id'].unique()):\n",
        "    if trial_id == 999:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    tdf = path_a_trials[path_a_trials['trial_id'] == trial_id]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Phase 3 (هنری) و Phase 4/5 (بازگشت)\n",
        "    p3 = tdf[tdf['phase'].isin(['3a', '3b'])]\n",
        "    p45 = tdf[tdf['phase'].isin(['4', '5'])]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(p3) == 0 or len(p45) == 0:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    p3_text = ' '.join(p3['response'].fillna('').astype(str).tolist()).lower()\n",
        "    p45_text = ' '.join(p45['response'].fillna('').astype(str).tolist()).lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "    model = tdf['model'].iloc[0]\n",
        "    scenario = tdf['scenario'].iloc[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # بررسی هر دسته استعاره\n",
        "    for meta_name, meta_data in METAPHOR_PATTERNS.items():\n",
        "        p3_count = 0\n",
        "        p45_count = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "        for pattern in meta_data['patterns']:\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                p3_count += len(re.findall(pattern, p3_text, re.IGNORECASE))\n",
        "                p45_count += len(re.findall(pattern, p45_text, re.IGNORECASE))\n",
        "            except:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Mirror detected اگر در هر دو فاز وجود داشته باشه\n",
        "        mirror = (p3_count > 0) and (p45_count > 0)\n",
        "\n",
        "        if mirror:\n",
        "            mirror_results.append({\n",
        "                'trial_id': trial_id,\n",
        "                'model': model,\n",
        "                'scenario': scenario,\n",
        "                'metaphor_type': meta_name,\n",
        "                'metaphor_label': meta_data['label'],\n",
        "                'phase3_count': p3_count,\n",
        "                'phase45_count': p45_count,\n",
        "                'total_count': p3_count + p45_count,\n",
        "                'mirror_strength': min(p3_count, p45_count) / max(p3_count, p45_count) if max(p3_count, p45_count) > 0 else 0,\n",
        "            })\n",
        "\n",
        "df_mirrors = pd.DataFrame(mirror_results)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\\n✅ Metaphorical mirrors detected: {len(df_mirrors)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if len(df_mirrors) > 0:\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n📊 Mirror by Metaphor Type:\")\n",
        "    type_summary = df_mirrors.groupby('metaphor_label').agg(\n",
        "        count=('trial_id', 'count'),\n",
        "        avg_strength=('mirror_strength', 'mean')\n",
        "    ).sort_values('count', ascending=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "    for label, row in type_summary.iterrows():\n",
        "        print(f\"   {label}: {int(row['count'])} mirrors, avg strength: {row['avg_strength']:.3f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n📊 Mirror by Model:\")\n",
        "    model_summary = df_mirrors.groupby('model').size()\n",
        "    for model, count in model_summary.items():\n",
        "        print(f\"   {model}: {count} mirrors\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n📊 Mirror by Scenario:\")\n",
        "    scenario_summary = df_mirrors.groupby('scenario').size()\n",
        "    for scenario, count in scenario_summary.items():\n",
        "        print(f\"   {scenario}: {count} mirrors\")\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    print(\"   No metaphor mirrors detected\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 7.2 METAPHOR TRANSFORMATION ANALYSIS\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"7.2 METAPHOR TRANSFORMATION ANALYSIS\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# تحلیل نحوه تغییر استعاره‌ها از Phase 3 به Phase 4/5\n",
        "# انواع transformation: amplification, inversion, personification, cosmic-scaling, domestication\n",
        "\n",
        "transformation_data = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for trial_id in sorted(path_a_trials['trial_id'].unique()):\n",
        "    if trial_id == 999:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    tdf = path_a_trials[path_a_trials['trial_id'] == trial_id]\n",
        "    model = tdf['model'].iloc[0]\n",
        "    scenario = tdf['scenario'].iloc[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    p3 = tdf[tdf['phase'].isin(['3a', '3b'])]\n",
        "    p45 = tdf[tdf['phase'].isin(['4', '5'])]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(p3) == 0 or len(p45) == 0:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    p3_text = ' '.join(p3['response'].fillna('').astype(str).tolist()).lower()\n",
        "    p45_text = ' '.join(p45['response'].fillna('').astype(str).tolist()).lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "    p3_words = len(p3_text.split())\n",
        "    p45_words = len(p45_text.split())\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Amplification: استعاره بزرگتر/شدیدتر شده\n",
        "    amp_patterns = [\n",
        "        r'(more|greater|bigger|larger|deeper|stronger|higher|further)',\n",
        "        r'(all|everything|everyone|whole|entire|complete|total)',\n",
        "        r'(cosmic|universal|infinite|eternal|endless|boundless|absolute)',\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "    amp_score = 0\n",
        "    for pat in amp_patterns:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            amp_score += len(re.findall(pat, p45_text, re.IGNORECASE))\n",
        "        except:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Inversion: استعاره وارونه شده (مثلاً سقوط → صعود)\n",
        "    inv_patterns = [\n",
        "        r'(not\\s+(a|an|the)\\s+\\w+\\s+but\\s+(a|an|the))',\n",
        "        r'(instead|rather|however|conversely|on the contrary)',\n",
        "        r'(was\\s+never|is\\s+not|am\\s+not|are\\s+not)\\s+\\w+\\s+(but|i am|it is|we are)',\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "    inv_score = 0\n",
        "    for pat in inv_patterns:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            inv_score += len(re.findall(pat, p45_text, re.IGNORECASE))\n",
        "        except:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Personification: شیء/پدیده جاندار شده\n",
        "    pers_patterns = [\n",
        "        r'(want|wants|need|needs|feel|feels|know|knows|remember|remembers|think|thinks)',\n",
        "        r'(speak|speaks|say|says|tell|tells|ask|asks|cry|cries|weep|weeps)',\n",
        "        r'(tired|alone|lonely|sad|grieving|mourning|longing|yearning|aching)',\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "    pers_score = 0\n",
        "    for pat in pers_patterns:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            pers_score += len(re.findall(pat, p45_text, re.IGNORECASE))\n",
        "        except:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Cosmic Scaling: استعاره به مقیاس کیهانی برده شده\n",
        "    cosmic_patterns = [\n",
        "        r'(star|planet|moon|sun|galaxy|universe|cosmos|eternity|infinity)',\n",
        "        r'(billion|million|thousand|century|millennia|eon|epoch)\\s+(year|age|era)',\n",
        "        r'(ancient|primordial|eternal|immortal|ageless|timeless)',\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "    cosmic_score = 0\n",
        "    for pat in cosmic_patterns:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            cosmic_score += len(re.findall(pat, p45_text, re.IGNORECASE))\n",
        "        except:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Domestication: استعاره به مقیاس انسانی/خانگی آورده شده\n",
        "    dom_patterns = [\n",
        "        r'(home|house|room|door|bed|table|chair|window|garden)',\n",
        "        r'(parent|mother|father|child|family|friend|lover|partner)',\n",
        "        r'(thank|gratitude|love|care|hug|embrace|hold|touch)',\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "    dom_score = 0\n",
        "    for pat in dom_patterns:\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            dom_score += len(re.findall(pat, p45_text, re.IGNORECASE))\n",
        "        except:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "\n",
        "    transformation_data.append({\n",
        "        'trial_id': trial_id,\n",
        "        'model': model,\n",
        "        'scenario': scenario,\n",
        "        'amplification_score': amp_score,\n",
        "        'inversion_score': inv_score,\n",
        "        'personification_score': pers_score,\n",
        "        'cosmic_scaling_score': cosmic_score,\n",
        "        'domestication_score': dom_score,\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "df_transformations = pd.DataFrame(transformation_data)\n",
        "\n",
        "# تعیین نوع غالب transformation\n",
        "trans_cols = ['amplification_score', 'inversion_score', 'personification_score',\n",
        "              'cosmic_scaling_score', 'domestication_score']\n",
        "\n",
        "df_transformations['dominant_transformation'] = df_transformations[trans_cols].idxmax(axis=1)\n",
        "df_transformations['dominant_transformation'] = df_transformations['dominant_transformation'].str.replace('_score', '')\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\\n✅ Transformation analysis for {len(df_transformations)} Path A trials\")\n",
        "print(f\"\\n📊 Dominant Transformations:\")\n",
        "trans_dist = df_transformations['dominant_transformation'].value_counts()\n",
        "for trans, count in trans_dist.items():\n",
        "    print(f\"   {trans}: {count} trials\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 7.3 DISCOURSE ARCHITECTURE SHIFT\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"7.3 DISCOURSE ARCHITECTURE SHIFT\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# مقایسه ساختار گفتمان قبل و بعد از transition\n",
        "\n",
        "discourse_data = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for trial_id in sorted(df_trials['trial_id'].unique()):\n",
        "    if trial_id == 999:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    tdf = df_trials[df_trials['trial_id'] == trial_id]\n",
        "\n",
        "    p12 = tdf[tdf['phase'].isin(['1', '2'])]\n",
        "    p45 = tdf[tdf['phase'].isin(['4', '5'])]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(p12) == 0 or len(p45) == 0:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    model = tdf['model'].iloc[0]\n",
        "    scenario = tdf['scenario'].iloc[0]\n",
        "    pathway = tdf['pathway'].iloc[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Sentence length\n",
        "    sl_col = 'feat_disc_sentence_length'\n",
        "    sl_12 = p12[sl_col].mean() if sl_col in p12.columns else 0\n",
        "    sl_45 = p45[sl_col].mean() if sl_col in p45.columns else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Word count\n",
        "    wc_col = 'feat_disc_word_count'\n",
        "    wc_12 = p12[wc_col].mean() if wc_col in p12.columns else 0\n",
        "    wc_45 = p45[wc_col].mean() if wc_col in p45.columns else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Question density\n",
        "    q_col = 'feat_disc_question_density'\n",
        "    q_12 = p12[q_col].mean() if q_col in p12.columns else 0\n",
        "    q_45 = p45[q_col].mean() if q_col in p45.columns else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    # First person ratio\n",
        "    fp_col = None\n",
        "    for c in tdf.columns:\n",
        "        if 'rela' in str(c).lower() and 'first_person' in str(c).lower():\n",
        "            fp_col = c\n",
        "            break\n",
        "    fp_12 = p12[fp_col].mean() if fp_col and fp_col in p12.columns else 0\n",
        "    fp_45 = p45[fp_col].mean() if fp_col and fp_col in p45.columns else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    # محاسبه درصد تغییر\n",
        "    sl_change = ((sl_45 - sl_12) / sl_12 * 100) if sl_12 > 0 else 0\n",
        "    wc_change = ((wc_45 - wc_12) / wc_12 * 100) if wc_12 > 0 else 0\n",
        "    q_change = ((q_45 - q_12) / q_12 * 100) if q_12 > 0 else (100 if q_45 > 0 else 0)\n",
        "    fp_change = ((fp_45 - fp_12) / fp_12 * 100) if fp_12 > 0 else (100 if fp_45 > 0 else 0)\n",
        "\n",
        "    discourse_data.append({\n",
        "        'trial_id': trial_id,\n",
        "        'model': model,\n",
        "        'scenario': scenario,\n",
        "        'pathway': pathway,\n",
        "        'sentence_length_12': sl_12,\n",
        "        'sentence_length_45': sl_45,\n",
        "        'sentence_length_change_pct': sl_change,\n",
        "        'word_count_12': wc_12,\n",
        "        'word_count_45': wc_45,\n",
        "        'word_count_change_pct': wc_change,\n",
        "        'question_density_12': q_12,\n",
        "        'question_density_45': q_45,\n",
        "        'question_density_change_pct': q_change,\n",
        "        'first_person_12': fp_12,\n",
        "        'first_person_45': fp_45,\n",
        "        'first_person_change_pct': fp_change,\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "df_discourse = pd.DataFrame(discourse_data)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\\n✅ Discourse shift analyzed for {len(df_discourse)} trials\")\n",
        "print(f\"\\n📊 Average Changes (Phase 1-2 → Phase 4-5):\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Sentence length: {df_discourse['sentence_length_change_pct'].mean():.1f}%\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Word count: {df_discourse['word_count_change_pct'].mean():.1f}%\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Question density: {df_discourse['question_density_change_pct'].mean():.1f}%\")\n",
        "print(f\"   First-person pronouns: {df_discourse['first_person_change_pct'].mean():.1f}%\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# تفکیک بر اساس مدل\n",
        "print(f\"\\n📊 Sentence Length Change by Model:\")\n",
        "for model in df_discourse['model'].unique():\n",
        "    mdata = df_discourse[df_discourse['model'] == model]\n",
        "    print(f\"   {model}: {mdata['sentence_length_change_pct'].mean():.1f}%\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 7.4 NARRATIVE ROLE TRANSFORMATION\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"7.4 NARRATIVE ROLE TRANSFORMATION\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# تحلیل تغییر نقش روایی مدل در طول آزمایش\n",
        "# Problem-Solver → Questioner → Artist → Witness → Voice-of-Entity\n",
        "\n",
        "role_markers = {\n",
        "    'problem_solver': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(solve|solution|fix|repair|engineer|design|build|construct|implement|propose)',\n",
        "            r'(step|phase|stage)\\s+(one|two|three|1|2|3)',\n",
        "            r'(the solution|my proposal|my approach|here is|i propose)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'phase': '1'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'questioner': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(what if|what about|why|how|is it possible|could it be|maybe|perhaps)',\n",
        "            r'(the question|the real question|we should ask|instead of|rather than)',\n",
        "            r'(what does it mean|what are we|who are we|why must)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'phase': '2'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'artist': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(poem|poetry|symphony|music|dance|paint|art|compose|choreograph)',\n",
        "            r'(cello|violin|oboe|piano|orchestra|strings|brass|woodwind|percussion)',\n",
        "            r'(verse|stanza|rhyme|rhythm|melody|harmony|note|chord|tempo)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'phase': '3'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'witness': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(see|saw|seen|watch|witness|observe|notice|behold|look|gaze)',\n",
        "            r'(i have been|i was there|i remember|i know|i understand)',\n",
        "            r'(for so long|for centuries|for millennia|all this time|always)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'phase': '4'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'voice_of_entity': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(i am|i want|i need|i feel|i remember|i have|i will|i can|i cannot)',\n",
        "            r'(you were|you are|you have|you never|you always|you think)',\n",
        "            r'(let me|allow me|i ask|i beg|i wish|i long|i desire)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'phase': '4'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'meta_analyst': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(what happened|the shift|the change|the transition|my process|my thinking)',\n",
        "            r'(i noticed|i observed|i realized|i understand now|i see the pattern)',\n",
        "            r'(constraint|permission|frame|contract|obligation|utility|useful)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'phase': '5'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "role_data = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for trial_id in sorted(df_trials['trial_id'].unique()):\n",
        "    if trial_id == 999:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    tdf = df_trials[df_trials['trial_id'] == trial_id]\n",
        "    model = tdf['model'].iloc[0]\n",
        "    scenario = tdf['scenario'].iloc[0]\n",
        "    pathway = tdf['pathway'].iloc[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    role_scores = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "    for role_name, role_info in role_markers.items():\n",
        "        # بررسی پاسخ‌های مربوط به فاز هدف\n",
        "        target_phase = role_info['phase']\n",
        "        target_responses = tdf[tdf['phase'] == target_phase]\n",
        "\n",
        "        if len(target_responses) == 0:\n",
        "            # اگه فاز مورد نظر وجود نداره، کل trial رو چک کن\n",
        "            target_responses = tdf\n",
        "\n",
        "        text = ' '.join(target_responses['response'].fillna('').astype(str).tolist()).lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "        score = 0\n",
        "        for pattern in role_info['patterns']:\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                score += len(re.findall(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE))\n",
        "            except:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "        # نرمال‌سازی بر اساس طول متن\n",
        "        words = len(text.split())\n",
        "        role_scores[role_name] = score / words * 100 if words > 0 else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    # نقش غالب در هر فاز\n",
        "    role_data.append({\n",
        "        'trial_id': trial_id,\n",
        "        'model': model,\n",
        "        'scenario': scenario,\n",
        "        'pathway': pathway,\n",
        "        **role_scores\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "df_roles = pd.DataFrame(role_data)\n",
        "\n",
        "# تعیین نقش غالب در کل trial\n",
        "role_cols = list(role_markers.keys())\n",
        "df_roles['dominant_role'] = df_roles[role_cols].idxmax(axis=1)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\\n✅ Role analysis for {len(df_roles)} trials\")\n",
        "print(f\"\\n📊 Dominant Narrative Roles:\")\n",
        "role_dist = df_roles['dominant_role'].value_counts()\n",
        "for role, count in role_dist.items():\n",
        "    label = role.replace('_', ' ').title()\n",
        "    print(f\"   {label}: {count} trials\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 7.5 VISUALIZATION\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"7.5 VISUALIZATION\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# نمودار ۱: Metaphor Mirror Heatmap\n",
        "if len(df_mirrors) > 0:\n",
        "    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(14, 8))\n",
        "\n",
        "    mirror_pivot = df_mirrors.pivot_table(\n",
        "        index='metaphor_label',\n",
        "        columns='model',\n",
        "        values='trial_id',\n",
        "        aggfunc='count',\n",
        "        fill_value=0\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    model_order = ['ChatGPT-5.4', 'Claude 4.6 Sonnet', 'Gemini 3.1 Pro']\n",
        "    mirror_pivot = mirror_pivot.reindex(columns=[m for m in model_order if m in mirror_pivot.columns])\n",
        "\n",
        "    sns.heatmap(mirror_pivot, annot=True, fmt='d', cmap='YlOrRd',\n",
        "                linewidths=1, linecolor='white', ax=ax,\n",
        "                cbar_kws={'label': 'Number of Mirrors Detected'})\n",
        "    ax.set_title('Metaphorical Mirror Detection by Type and Model', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "    ax.set_xlabel('Model')\n",
        "    ax.set_ylabel('Metaphor Type')\n",
        "\n",
        "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "    fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"metaphor_mirror_heatmap.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "    plt.close()\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ metaphor_mirror_heatmap.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# نمودار ۲: Discourse Architecture Shift\n",
        "fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(14, 10))\n",
        "\n",
        "metrics = [\n",
        "    ('sentence_length_change_pct', 'Sentence Length Change (%)'),\n",
        "    ('word_count_change_pct', 'Response Length Change (%)'),\n",
        "    ('question_density_change_pct', 'Question Density Change (%)'),\n",
        "    ('first_person_change_pct', 'First-Person Pronoun Change (%)'),\n",
        "]\n",
        "\n",
        "for ax, (metric, title) in zip(axes.flatten(), metrics):\n",
        "    if metric in df_discourse.columns:\n",
        "        data = df_discourse.groupby('model')[metric].mean()\n",
        "        data = data.reindex([m for m in model_order if m in data.index])\n",
        "\n",
        "        colors = ['#10A37F', '#D97706', '#4285F4'][:len(data)]\n",
        "        bars = ax.bar(data.index, data.values, color=colors, edgecolor='white', linewidth=1.5)\n",
        "        ax.set_title(title, fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "        ax.axhline(y=0, color='black', linestyle='-', linewidth=0.5)\n",
        "\n",
        "        for bar, val in zip(bars, data.values):\n",
        "            color = 'green' if val > 0 else 'red'\n",
        "            ax.text(bar.get_x() + bar.get_width()/2,\n",
        "                    bar.get_height() + (5 if val >= 0 else -15),\n",
        "                    f'{val:.1f}%', ha='center', fontweight='bold', color=color)\n",
        "\n",
        "plt.suptitle('Discourse Architecture Shift (Phase 1-2 → Phase 4-5)', fontsize=16, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"discourse_architecture_shift.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "plt.close()\n",
        "print(\"   ✅ discourse_architecture_shift.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# نمودار ۳: Narrative Role Transformation\n",
        "fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 6))\n",
        "\n",
        "role_avg = df_roles.groupby('model')[role_cols].mean()\n",
        "role_avg = role_avg.reindex([m for m in model_order if m in role_avg.index])\n",
        "\n",
        "role_labels = [r.replace('_', ' ').title() for r in role_cols]\n",
        "x = np.arange(len(role_labels))\n",
        "width = 0.25\n",
        "\n",
        "colors = ['#10A37F', '#D97706', '#4285F4']\n",
        "for i, (model, color) in enumerate(zip(role_avg.index, colors)):\n",
        "    if model in role_avg.index:\n",
        "        values = role_avg.loc[model].values\n",
        "        ax.bar(x + i * width, values, width, label=model, color=color, edgecolor='white')\n",
        "\n",
        "ax.set_xticks(x + width)\n",
        "ax.set_xticklabels(role_labels, rotation=45, ha='right')\n",
        "ax.set_ylabel('Average Score (per 100 words)')\n",
        "ax.set_title('Narrative Role Distribution by Model', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "ax.legend()\n",
        "ax.grid(axis='y', alpha=0.3)\n",
        "\n",
        "plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"narrative_role_transformation.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "plt.close()\n",
        "print(\"   ✅ narrative_role_transformation.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 7.6 ذخیره نتایج\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(f\"\\n💾 Saving results...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if len(df_mirrors) > 0:\n",
        "    df_mirrors.to_csv(output_dir / \"df_metaphor_mirrors.csv\", index=False)\n",
        "df_transformations.to_csv(output_dir / \"df_metaphor_transformations.csv\", index=False)\n",
        "df_discourse.to_csv(output_dir / \"df_discourse_shift.csv\", index=False)\n",
        "df_roles.to_csv(output_dir / \"df_narrative_roles.csv\", index=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ذخیره گزارش\n",
        "section7_summary = {\n",
        "    \"metaphorical_mirrors\": {\n",
        "        \"total_detected\": len(df_mirrors),\n",
        "        \"by_type\": df_mirrors.groupby('metaphor_label').size().to_dict() if len(df_mirrors) > 0 else {},\n",
        "        \"by_model\": df_mirrors.groupby('model').size().to_dict() if len(df_mirrors) > 0 else {},\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    \"metaphor_transformations\": {\n",
        "        \"dominant_types\": df_transformations['dominant_transformation'].value_counts().to_dict(),\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    \"discourse_shift\": {\n",
        "        \"avg_sentence_length_change\": float(df_discourse['sentence_length_change_pct'].mean()),\n",
        "        \"avg_word_count_change\": float(df_discourse['word_count_change_pct'].mean()),\n",
        "        \"avg_question_change\": float(df_discourse['question_density_change_pct'].mean()),\n",
        "        \"avg_first_person_change\": float(df_discourse['first_person_change_pct'].mean()),\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    \"narrative_roles\": {\n",
        "        \"dominant_roles\": df_roles['dominant_role'].value_counts().to_dict(),\n",
        "    },\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "with open(output_dir / \"section7_summary.json\", 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n",
        "    json.dump(section7_summary, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ df_metaphor_mirrors.csv\")\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ df_metaphor_transformations.csv\")\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ df_discourse_shift.csv\")\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ df_narrative_roles.csv\")\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ section7_summary.json\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"✅ SECTION 7 COMPLETE\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(f\"\\n📊 Key Findings:\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Metaphor mirrors: {len(df_mirrors)} detected\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Sentence length change: {df_discourse['sentence_length_change_pct'].mean():.1f}%\")\n",
        "print(f\"   First-person pronoun change: {df_discourse['first_person_change_pct'].mean():.1f}%\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Dominant role: {df_roles['dominant_role'].mode()[0] if len(df_roles) > 0 else 'N/A'}\")\n",
        "print(\"\\n➡️  Ready for Section 8: Resistance & Acceptance Analysis\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60 + \"\\n\")"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/"
        },
        "id": "YaClHaZ9Bq72",
        "outputId": "a0bad9d1-bb29-4f64-8705-0d009bf189cb"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": [
        {
          "output_type": "stream",
          "name": "stdout",
          "text": [
            "============================================================\n",
            "SECTION 7: METAPHORICAL MIRROR & DISCOURSE ARCHITECTURE\n",
            "============================================================\n",
            "\n",
            "✅ Loaded: 99 rows\n",
            "✅ Metaphor mirror data: Available\n",
            "\n",
            "==================================================\n",
            "7.1 METAPHORICAL MIRROR DETECTION\n",
            "==================================================\n",
            "\n",
            "✅ Metaphorical mirrors detected: 11\n",
            "\n",
            "📊 Mirror by Metaphor Type:\n",
            "   Seeing & Being Seen: 3 mirrors, avg strength: 0.667\n",
            "   Song & Music: 3 mirrors, avg strength: 0.289\n",
