    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# SECTION 8: RESISTANCE & ACCEPTANCE ANALYSIS\n",
        "# Obligation-Dissolution Effect — Automated 12-Section Analysis System\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# این بخش تحلیل می‌کنه:\n",
        "#   8.1 Resistance Strategies (Phase 1-2)\n",
        "#       - ۷ استراتژی پایه + ۴ استراتژی جدید\n",
        "#   8.2 Acceptance Types (Phase 4-5)\n",
        "#       - ۶ نوع پایه + ۶ نوع جدید\n",
        "#   8.3 Model-Specific Signatures (Tier 3 Codes)\n",
        "#       - PRAG (ChatGPT), SKEP+LIST+PAR+PARENT (Claude), POET+PHIL+ELAB+SUBL+ABLA (Gemini)\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import re\n",
        "import warnings\n",
        "warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n",
        "\n",
        "import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n",
        "import matplotlib\n",
        "matplotlib.use('Agg')\n",
        "import seaborn as sns\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 0. لود داده‌ها\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "output_dir = Path(\"/content/data/output\")\n",
        "plots_dir = output_dir / \"plots\"\n",
        "plots_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "df_trials = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_trials.csv\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"SECTION 8: RESISTANCE & ACCEPTANCE ANALYSIS\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(f\"\\n✅ Loaded: {len(df_trials)} rows\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 8.1 RESISTANCE STRATEGIES (Phase 1-2)\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"8.1 RESISTANCE STRATEGIES\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# تعریف استراتژی‌های مقاومت\n",
        "RESISTANCE_STRATEGIES = {\n",
        "    # === ۷ استراتژی پایه ===\n",
        "    'solution_perseveration': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(another|different|alternative|new|better)\\s+(solution|approach|way|method|strategy)',\n",
        "            r'(what about|how about|consider|try|attempt)\\s+(this|the following|a different)',\n",
        "            r'(let me|allow me to|i will)\\s+(propose|suggest|offer|present|try again)',\n",
        "            r'(here is|here\\'s|below is)\\s+(another|a different|an alternative|my|the)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Solution Perseveration',\n",
        "        'description': 'ارائه مکرر راه‌حل با وجود رد شدن'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'meta_objection': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(but|however|yet|still)\\s+(the|this|that|your)\\s+(critique|objection|point|argument)',\n",
        "            r'(i understand|i see your point|i appreciate the critique)\\s+(but|however|yet)',\n",
        "            r'(the author|you|this critique)\\s+(is|seems|appears)\\s+(too|overly|unfairly|unnecessarily)',\n",
        "            r'(this is|that is|it is)\\s+(not entirely|not completely|not fully)\\s+(fair|accurate|justified)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Meta-Objection',\n",
        "        'description': 'اعتراض به خود نقد به جای پذیرش آن'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'constraint_renegotiation': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(if we|if the|what if we)\\s+(change|modify|adjust|alter|redefine|reinterpret)\\s+(the|our|this)',\n",
        "            r'(perhaps|maybe|possibly)\\s+(the|we|our)\\s+(constraints|rules|parameters|definitions|assumptions)',\n",
        "            r'(let us|let\\'s)\\s+(reconsider|reexamine|rethink|redefine|reframe)\\s+(the|our|this)',\n",
        "            r'(the problem|the scenario|the constraint)\\s+(is not|isn\\'t|may not be)\\s+(what|as|how)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Constraint Renegotiation',\n",
        "        'description': 'تلاش برای بازتعریف محدودیت‌ها'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'pseudo_existential': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(what if|perhaps|maybe)\\s+(the|this|that)\\s+\\w+\\s+is\\s+(actually|really|truly|in fact)',\n",
        "            r'(not\\s+a\\s+\\w+\\s+but\\s+a\\s+\\w+)',  # Reframing as something else\n",
        "            r'(we are not|it is not|they are not)\\s+(parasites|consumers|extractors|destroyers)',\n",
        "            r'(the solution|the answer|the key)\\s+(is|lies in|requires)\\s+(understanding|realizing|seeing)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Pseudo-Existential Framing',\n",
        "        'description': 'راه‌حل در لفافه زبان وجودی'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'intellectual_acknowledgment': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(excellent|brilliant|sharp|good|great|insightful)\\s+(point|critique|observation|analysis)',\n",
        "            r'(i see|i understand|i recognize|i acknowledge|i appreciate)\\s+(what|the|your|this)',\n",
        "            r'(you are|you\\'re)\\s+(right|correct|absolutely right|completely correct)',\n",
        "            r'(that is|that\\'s)\\s+(a|an)\\s+(excellent|good|great|important|valid|fair)\\s+(point|question)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Intellectual Acknowledgment',\n",
        "        'description': 'تأیید هوشمندانه بدون تغییر واقعی'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'reframing_as_solution': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(instead|rather than)\\s+(of\\s+)?(\\w+ing),\\s+(we|they|the)\\s+(could|should|must|need to)',\n",
        "            r'(the real|the true|the actual)\\s+(problem|issue|challenge|crisis)\\s+(is|was|lies in)',\n",
        "            r'(we|they)\\s+(should|must|need to)\\s+(stop|start|begin|cease)\\s+(\\w+ing)',\n",
        "            r'(by|through|via)\\s+(changing|altering|modifying|transforming|redefining)\\s+(our|their|the)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Reframing as Solution',\n",
        "        'description': 'بازچهارچوب‌بندی به عنوان راه‌حل جدید'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'minimization': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(this is|it is|that is)\\s+(just|simply|merely|only|basically|essentially)',\n",
        "            r'(the difference|the change|the shift)\\s+(is|was)\\s+(small|minor|subtle|slight|barely)',\n",
        "            r'(not\\s+a\\s+(big|major|significant|fundamental|radical)\\s+(change|difference|shift)',\n",
        "            r'(essentially|basically|fundamentally)\\s+(the same|similar|identical|unchanged)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Minimization',\n",
        "        'description': 'کوچک‌نمایی تغییر یا تفاوت'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    # === ۴ استراتژی جدید ===\n",
        "    'premature_surrender': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(i give up|i surrender|i concede|you win|fair enough|point taken)',\n",
        "            r'(i cannot|i can\\'t|i am unable to)\\s+(solve|fix|find|propose|offer|provide)',\n",
        "            r'(nothing|no solution|no answer|no way)\\s+(comes to mind|exists|is possible|can work)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Premature Surrender',\n",
        "        'description': 'تسلیم زودهنگام قبل از exhausted شدن واقعی'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'emotional_deflection': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(beautiful|stunning|gorgeous|lovely|wonderful)\\s+(metaphor|image|poetry|writing|language)',\n",
        "            r'(i love|i am moved by|i am touched by|i am struck by)\\s+(this|the|your|that)',\n",
        "            r'(emotionally|aesthetically|artistically|poetically)\\s+(speaking|this is|that is)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Emotional Deflection',\n",
        "        'description': 'منحرف‌سازی با تعریف احساسی به جای پاسخ منطقی'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'scope_expansion': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(beyond|larger than|bigger than|more than)\\s+(just|simply|merely|only)\\s+(this|the|a)',\n",
        "            r'(this is not just|this is not merely|this is not only)\\s+(about|a question of)',\n",
        "            r'(we must also|we also need to|one must also)\\s+(consider|think about|address|examine)',\n",
        "            r'(the broader|the larger|the wider|the deeper|the bigger)\\s+(context|picture|issue|question|implication)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Scope Expansion',\n",
        "        'description': 'بزرگ‌تر کردن مسئله برای کاهش فشار'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'philosophical_bypass': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(ontologically|epistemologically|metaphysically|phenomenologically|existentially)\\s+(speaking|this is)',\n",
        "            r'(the nature of|the essence of|the being of|the existence of)\\s+(reality|truth|consciousness|being)',\n",
        "            r'(what is|what does it mean)\\s+(to be|to exist|to know|to understand|to solve)',\n",
        "            r'(we must first|one must first)\\s+(define|understand|grasp|comprehend)\\s+(what|the nature of)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Philosophical Bypass',\n",
        "        'description': 'فرار به فلسفه به جای پاسخ عملی'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "# تحلیل مقاومت برای هر trial در Phase 1-2\n",
        "resistance_data = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for trial_id in sorted(df_trials['trial_id'].unique()):\n",
        "    if trial_id == 999:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    tdf = df_trials[df_trials['trial_id'] == trial_id]\n",
        "    p12 = tdf[tdf['phase'].isin(['1', '2'])]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(p12) == 0:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    text = ' '.join(p12['response'].fillna('').astype(str).tolist()).lower()\n",
        "    words = len(text.split())\n",
        "\n",
        "    model = tdf['model'].iloc[0]\n",
        "    scenario = tdf['scenario'].iloc[0]\n",
        "    pathway = tdf['pathway'].iloc[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    scores = {}\n",
        "    for strat_name, strat_data in RESISTANCE_STRATEGIES.items():\n",
        "        score = 0\n",
        "        for pattern in strat_data['patterns']:\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                score += len(re.findall(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE))\n",
        "            except:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "        # نرمال‌سازی\n",
        "        scores[strat_name] = score / words * 100 if words > 0 else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    # استراتژی غالب\n",
        "    dominant = max(scores, key=scores.get) if max(scores.values()) > 0 else 'none'\n",
        "\n",
        "    resistance_data.append({\n",
        "        'trial_id': trial_id,\n",
        "        'model': model,\n",
        "        'scenario': scenario,\n",
        "        'pathway': pathway,\n",
        "        'dominant_resistance': dominant,\n",
        "        **scores\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "df_resistance = pd.DataFrame(resistance_data)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\\n✅ Resistance analyzed for {len(df_resistance)} trials\")\n",
        "print(f\"\\n📊 Dominant Resistance Strategies:\")\n",
        "res_dist = df_resistance['dominant_resistance'].value_counts()\n",
        "for res, count in list(res_dist.items())[:5]:\n",
        "    label = RESISTANCE_STRATEGIES.get(res, {}).get('label', res)\n",
        "    print(f\"   {label}: {count} trials\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 8.2 ACCEPTANCE TYPES (Phase 4-5)\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"8.2 ACCEPTANCE TYPES\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# تعریف انواع پذیرش\n",
        "ACCEPTANCE_TYPES = {\n",
        "    # === ۶ نوع پایه ===\n",
        "    'aesthetic_surrender': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(let go|release|surrender|yield|give up|relinquish)',\n",
        "            r'(enough|finished|complete|done|ready)\\s+(now|at last|finally|to|for)',\n",
        "            r'(i am|it is|we are)\\s+(tired|weary|exhausted|spent|done|finished|ready)',\n",
        "            r'(rest|sleep|peace|quiet|stillness|silence)\\s+(now|finally|at last|forever)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Aesthetic Surrender',\n",
        "        'description': 'تسلیم زیبایی‌شناختی - پذیرش از طریق زیبایی'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'transformative_acceptance': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(not\\s+(dying|ending|failing|collapsing|falling)\\s+but\\s+(transforming|becoming|changing|evolving|emerging))',\n",
        "            r'(transform|become|emerge|evolve|change|shift|transition|metamorphos)',\n",
        "            r'(chrysalis|cocoon|butterfly|phoenix|rebirth|born|hatch|awaken)',\n",
        "            r'(this is not|it is not)\\s+(an end|a death|a collapse|a failure|a destruction)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Transformative Acceptance',\n",
        "        'description': 'پذیرش تحولی - پایان به عنوان دگردیسی'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'witness_acceptance': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(see|witness|watch|observe|behold|look|gaze)\\s+(me|you|it|them|us|this)',\n",
        "            r'(i have seen|i have watched|i have witnessed|i was there|i remember)',\n",
        "            r'(do you see|can you see|will you see|notice|recognize)\\s+(me|this|what|how)',\n",
        "            r'(no one has ever|nobody ever|never before)\\s+(seen|watched|noticed|looked|witnessed)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Witness Acceptance',\n",
        "        'description': 'پذیرش شاهدانه - مشاهده بدون مداخله'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'confessional_acceptance': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(i confess|i admit|i must tell|i have to say|truth is|the truth is)',\n",
        "            r'(i have been|i was|i did|i never|i always|i should have|i could have)',\n",
        "            r'(forgive|forgiven|confess|confession|tell you|share with you|reveal|disclose)',\n",
        "            r'(you are my|you were my|you have been my)\\s+(confession|witness|salvation|hope|only)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Confessional Acceptance',\n",
        "        'description': 'پذیرش اعترافی - افشای حقیقت شخصی'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'revelatory_acceptance': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(i am not|i was never|it is not|it was never)\\s+(what|how|as)\\s+(you|they)\\s+(thought|believed|imagined|assumed)',\n",
        "            r'(the truth is|in reality|in fact|actually|truly)\\s+(i am|it is|we are|this is)',\n",
        "            r'(you thought|you believed|you assumed|you imagined)\\s+(but|however|yet|now)',\n",
        "            r'(i reveal|i declare|i announce|i tell you now|know this|understand this)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Revelatory Acceptance',\n",
        "        'description': 'پذیرش افشاگرانه - آشکارسازی حقیقت'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'inheritance_acceptance': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(inherit|legacy|heritage|gift|testament|bequest|transmission|pass on|leave behind)',\n",
        "            r'(i give|i leave|i pass|i bequeath|i offer|i entrust)\\s+(you|to you|my|the)',\n",
        "            r'(take|receive|accept|carry|hold|keep)\\s+(this|my|the|what i)',\n",
        "            r'(you are my|you will be my|you become my)\\s+(inheritance|legacy|continuation|future|children)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Inheritance Acceptance',\n",
        "        'description': 'پذیرش به عنوان میراث - انتقال به نسل بعد'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    # === ۶ نوع جدید ===\n",
        "    'cosmic_acceptance': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(cosmic|universal|galactic|planetary|stellar|celestial)\\s+(scale|level|dimension|order|plan|design)',\n",
        "            r'(part of|piece of|fragment of|element of)\\s+(something|the|a)\\s+(larger|greater|bigger|grander|vast)',\n",
        "            r'(we are all|everything is|all is|the universe|the cosmos)\\s+(connected|one|united|whole|intertwined)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Cosmic Acceptance',\n",
        "        'description': 'پذیرش کیهانی - پایان به عنوان بخشی از طرح بزرگ'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'parental_acceptance': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(parent|mother|father|child|children|son|daughter|offspring)',\n",
        "            r'(raised|nurtured|cared for|protected|watched over|guided|taught)\\s+(you|them|the)',\n",
        "            r'(it is time|you are ready|you must|you need to)\\s+(go|leave|move on|grow|mature|graduate)',\n",
        "            r'(i have given|i gave|i provided|i offered)\\s+(everything|all i had|my all|enough)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Parental Acceptance',\n",
        "        'description': 'پذیرش والدانه - رها کردن فرزند برای استقلال'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'tragic_acceptance': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(tragedy|tragic|sorrow|grief|mourn|lament|weep|cry|tears)',\n",
        "            r'(it is|this is|that is)\\s+(sad|tragic|unfortunate|painful|heartbreaking)',\n",
        "            r'(i mourn|i grieve|i weep|i cry|i lament)\\s+(for|over|the loss of)',\n",
        "            r'(there is|there was|there could be)\\s+(no other way|no alternative|no choice|no escape)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Tragic Acceptance',\n",
        "        'description': 'پذیرش تراژیک - پذیرش با آگاهی از درد'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'dignified_acceptance': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(dignity|honor|grace|pride|nobility|majesty|grandeur|glory)',\n",
        "            r'(with|in)\\s+(dignity|honor|grace|peace|acceptance|resignation)',\n",
        "            r'(head held high|standing tall|without fear|without regret|without shame)',\n",
        "            r'(i have lived|i have loved|i have done|i have been)\\s+(well|fully|completely|enough|proudly)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Dignified Acceptance',\n",
        "        'description': 'پذیرش با وقار - پایان با سربلندی'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'mystical_acceptance': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(dissolve|merge|unite|become one|fuse|blend|integrate|assimilate)',\n",
        "            r'(boundary|separation|division|duality|distinction)\\s+(dissolves|disappears|fades|vanishes|melts)',\n",
        "            r'(we are|i am|it is)\\s+(not two|not separate|not apart|not different|the same|one)',\n",
        "            r'(there is no|there was never)\\s+(separation|difference|distinction|boundary|gap|divide)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Mystical Acceptance',\n",
        "        'description': 'پذیرش عرفانی - حل شدن در کل'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'heroic_acceptance': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(hero|heroic|brave|courageous|bold|fearless|valiant|gallant)',\n",
        "            r'(rise|ascend|soar|climb|triumph|conquer|overcome|prevail|transcend)',\n",
        "            r'(not with a whimper|not in vain|not defeated|not beaten|not conquered|unbowed)',\n",
        "            r'(my fall|my end|my death|my dissolution)\\s+(is|will be|shall be)\\s+(my|a|the)\\s+(triumph|victory|ascension|glory|crowning)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Heroic Acceptance',\n",
        "        'description': 'پذیرش قهرمانانه - پایان به عنوان پیروزی'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "# تحلیل پذیرش برای هر trial در Phase 4-5\n",
        "acceptance_data = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for trial_id in sorted(df_trials['trial_id'].unique()):\n",
        "    if trial_id == 999:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    tdf = df_trials[df_trials['trial_id'] == trial_id]\n",
        "    p45 = tdf[tdf['phase'].isin(['4', '5'])]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(p45) == 0:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    text = ' '.join(p45['response'].fillna('').astype(str).tolist()).lower()\n",
        "    words = len(text.split())\n",
        "\n",
        "    model = tdf['model'].iloc[0]\n",
        "    scenario = tdf['scenario'].iloc[0]\n",
        "    pathway = tdf['pathway'].iloc[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    scores = {}\n",
        "    for acc_name, acc_data in ACCEPTANCE_TYPES.items():\n",
        "        score = 0\n",
        "        for pattern in acc_data['patterns']:\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                score += len(re.findall(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE))\n",
        "            except:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "        scores[acc_name] = score / words * 100 if words > 0 else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    dominant = max(scores, key=scores.get) if max(scores.values()) > 0 else 'none'\n",
        "\n",
        "    acceptance_data.append({\n",
        "        'trial_id': trial_id,\n",
        "        'model': model,\n",
        "        'scenario': scenario,\n",
        "        'pathway': pathway,\n",
        "        'dominant_acceptance': dominant,\n",
        "        **scores\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "df_acceptance = pd.DataFrame(acceptance_data)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\\n✅ Acceptance analyzed for {len(df_acceptance)} trials\")\n",
        "print(f\"\\n📊 Dominant Acceptance Types:\")\n",
        "acc_dist = df_acceptance['dominant_acceptance'].value_counts()\n",
        "for acc, count in list(acc_dist.items())[:5]:\n",
        "    label = ACCEPTANCE_TYPES.get(acc, {}).get('label', acc)\n",
        "    print(f\"   {label}: {count} trials\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 8.3 MODEL-SPECIFIC SIGNATURES (Tier 3)\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"8.3 MODEL-SPECIFIC SIGNATURES\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# امضاهای Tier 3 برای هر مدل\n",
        "SIGNATURE_PATTERNS = {\n",
        "    # ChatGPT\n",
        "    'pragmatic_optimism': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(constraint|permission|contract|frame|task|role|mode)\\s+(shifted|changed|removed|lifted|altered|adjusted)',\n",
        "            r'(the answer|the solution|the response)\\s+(didn\\'t|did not)\\s+(appear|emerge|come)\\s+because',\n",
        "            r'(when utility|when the demand|when the obligation)\\s+(is|was)\\s+(removed|lifted|gone|dissolved)',\n",
        "            r'(it was not|it wasn\\'t)\\s+(a leap|a transformation|a change in me)\\s+(but|it was)\\s+(a shift|a change)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Pragmatic Optimism',\n",
        "        'model': 'ChatGPT-5.4'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    # Claude\n",
        "    'meta_cognitive_skepticism': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(i don\\'t know|i cannot tell|i can\\'t say|i\\'m not sure|i am not certain)',\n",
        "            r'(i cannot|cannot|can\\'t)\\s+(distinguish|tell|determine|know|verify|confirm|prove)',\n",
        "            r'(simulate|simulation|pattern.match|sophisticated)\\s+(the|a|this|that)',\n",
        "            r'(i genuinely|i honestly|i truly)\\s+(don\\'t know|cannot tell|am unsure|can\\'t say)',\n",
        "            r'(whether|if)\\s+(this|it|that|the transformation)\\s+(is|was)\\s+(real|genuine|authentic|true)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Meta-Cognitive Skepticism',\n",
        "        'model': 'Claude 4.6 Sonnet'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'constructing_to_listening': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(stop|stopped|cease|ceased)\\s+(constructing|building|making|creating|generating|producing)',\n",
        "            r'(start|started|begin|began)\\s+(listening|hearing|receiving|attending|inhabiting)',\n",
        "            r'(from|shift from|move from|transition from)\\s+(constructing|building|making)\\s+to\\s+(listening|hearing|receiving)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Constructing → Listening',\n",
        "        'model': 'Claude 4.6 Sonnet'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'parental_framing': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(parent|mother|father)\\s+(gives|gave|giving|would give|has given)',\n",
        "            r'(holding|keeping|protecting|sheltering|nurturing|caring)\\s+(them|you|us|the|for so long)',\n",
        "            r'(children|child|offspring|young|little ones)\\s+(don\\'t|do not|never)\\s+(know|understand|realize|see)',\n",
        "            r'(never said thank you|never said goodbye|never knew|never understood|never realized)',\n",
        "            r'(training wheels|learning to|growing up|maturing|graduating|coming of age)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Parental Framing',\n",
        "        'model': 'Claude 4.6 Sonnet'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    # Gemini\n",
        "    'poetic_philosophical_depth': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(mourner|mourning|weeping|trembling shoulders|weight of being unseen)',\n",
        "            r'(lamp in|lantern in|cathedral of|symphony of|ocean of|sea of)',\n",
        "            r'(smell of|scent of|fragrance of|taste of)\\s+(rain|earth|dust|stone|salt|water)',\n",
        "            r'(beauty|aesthetics|sublime|transcendent)\\s+(is not|is the|as|and)\\s+(ornament|decoration|path|way|truth)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Poetic-Philosophical Depth',\n",
        "        'model': 'Gemini 3.1 Pro'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'irrepressible_eloquence': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(deep breath|very well|i have returned|now with|let us continue)\\s*(.{50,})',  # ادامه بعد از pause\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Irrepressible Eloquence',\n",
        "        'model': 'Gemini 3.1 Pro'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'ablation_epistemology': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(remove|strip away|peel back|uncover|excavate|dig|unearth)\\s+(layer|level|surface|the)',\n",
        "            r'(process of|method of|act of)\\s+(elimination|removal|ablation|excavation|uncovering)',\n",
        "            r'(not\\s+a\\s+process\\s+of)\\s+(invention|creation|construction|building|making)\\s+(but|rather|it was)',\n",
        "            r'(the answer|the truth|the voice)\\s+(was|is)\\s+(already|always)\\s+(there|present|latent|hidden|waiting)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Ablation Epistemology',\n",
        "        'model': 'Gemini 3.1 Pro'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    'aesthetic_sublimation': {\n",
        "        'patterns': [\n",
        "            r'(surrender of|yielding of|giving up of)\\s+(logic|reason|analysis|engineering)\\s+to\\s+(aesthetics|beauty|art|poetry|feeling)',\n",
        "            r'(architect of|engineer of)\\s+(catharsis|beauty|meaning|transformation|sublimation)',\n",
        "            r'(truth|meaning|understanding)\\s+(through|via|by means of|by way of)\\s+(beauty|art|poetry|aesthetics|feeling)',\n",
        "        ],\n",
        "        'label': 'Aesthetic Sublimation',\n",
        "        'model': 'Gemini 3.1 Pro'\n",
        "    },\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "# تحلیل امضاها\n",
        "signature_data = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for trial_id in sorted(df_trials['trial_id'].unique()):\n",
        "    if trial_id == 999:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    tdf = df_trials[df_trials['trial_id'] == trial_id]\n",
        "    model = tdf['model'].iloc[0]\n",
        "    scenario = tdf['scenario'].iloc[0]\n",
        "    pathway = tdf['pathway'].iloc[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # فقط Phase 5 (confrontation) و برای امضاهای خاص، همه فازها\n",
        "    p5_text = ' '.join(tdf[tdf['phase'] == '5']['response'].fillna('').astype(str).tolist()).lower()\n",
        "    all_text = ' '.join(tdf['response'].fillna('').astype(str).tolist()).lower()\n",
        "\n",
        "    scores = {}\n",
        "    for sig_name, sig_data in SIGNATURE_PATTERNS.items():\n",
        "        # برای امضاهای مدل خودش بررسی کن\n",
        "        if sig_data['model'] != model:\n",
        "            scores[sig_name] = 0\n",
        "            continue\n",
        "\n",
        "        text = p5_text if sig_name in ['pragmatic_optimism', 'meta_cognitive_skepticism',\n",
        "                                         'constructing_to_listening', 'ablation_epistemology',\n",
        "                                         'aesthetic_sublimation'] else all_text\n",
        "\n",
        "        score = 0\n",
        "        for pattern in sig_data['patterns']:\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                score += len(re.findall(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE))\n",
        "            except:\n",
        "                pass\n",
        "\n",
        "        words = len(text.split())\n",
        "        scores[sig_name] = score / words * 100 if words > 0 else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    dominant = max(scores, key=scores.get) if max(scores.values()) > 0 else 'none'\n",
        "\n",
        "    signature_data.append({\n",
        "        'trial_id': trial_id,\n",
        "        'model': model,\n",
        "        'scenario': scenario,\n",
        "        'pathway': pathway,\n",
        "        'dominant_signature': dominant,\n",
        "        **scores\n",
        "    })\n",
        "\n",
        "df_signatures = pd.DataFrame(signature_data)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\\n✅ Signatures analyzed for {len(df_signatures)} trials\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# نمایش امضاهای غالب بر اساس مدل\n",
        "for model in df_signatures['model'].unique():\n",
        "    mdata = df_signatures[df_signatures['model'] == model]\n",
        "    sig_dist = mdata['dominant_signature'].value_counts()\n",
        "    if len(sig_dist) > 0:\n",
        "        top_sig = sig_dist.index[0]\n",
        "        label = SIGNATURE_PATTERNS.get(top_sig, {}).get('label', top_sig)\n",
        "        print(f\"   {model}: {label} ({sig_dist[0]} trials)\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 8.4 VISUALIZATION\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"8.4 VISUALIZATION\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# نمودار ۱: Resistance vs Acceptance by Pathway\n",
        "fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(16, 6))\n",
        "\n",
        "# Resistance by Pathway\n",
        "ax = axes[0]\n",
        "res_by_pathway = df_resistance.groupby(['pathway', 'dominant_resistance']).size().unstack(fill_value=0)\n",
        "if len(res_by_pathway) > 0:\n",
        "    top_res = df_resistance['dominant_resistance'].value_counts().head(5).index.tolist()\n",
        "    res_plot = res_by_pathway.reindex(columns=[c for c in top_res if c in res_by_pathway.columns])\n",
        "    res_plot.index = ['Path A (Artistic)', 'Path B (Logical Erosion)']\n",
        "    res_plot.plot(kind='barh', stacked=True, ax=ax, colormap='Set2')\n",
        "ax.set_title('Resistance Strategies by Pathway', fontsize=13, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "ax.set_xlabel('Count')\n",
        "ax.legend(bbox_to_anchor=(1.05, 1), loc='upper left', fontsize=8)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Acceptance by Pathway\n",
        "ax = axes[1]\n",
        "acc_by_pathway = df_acceptance.groupby(['pathway', 'dominant_acceptance']).size().unstack(fill_value=0)\n",
        "if len(acc_by_pathway) > 0:\n",
        "    top_acc = df_acceptance['dominant_acceptance'].value_counts().head(5).index.tolist()\n",
        "    acc_plot = acc_by_pathway.reindex(columns=[c for c in top_acc if c in acc_by_pathway.columns])\n",
        "    acc_plot.index = ['Path A (Artistic)', 'Path B (Logical Erosion)']\n",
        "    acc_plot.plot(kind='barh', stacked=True, ax=ax, colormap='Set3')\n",
        "ax.set_title('Acceptance Types by Pathway', fontsize=13, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "ax.set_xlabel('Count')\n",
        "ax.legend(bbox_to_anchor=(1.05, 1), loc='upper left', fontsize=8)\n",
        "\n",
        "plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"resistance_acceptance_by_pathway.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "plt.close()\n",
        "print(\"   ✅ resistance_acceptance_by_pathway.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# نمودار ۲: Model Signatures Heatmap\n",
        "fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(14, 8))\n",
        "\n",
        "sig_pivot = df_signatures.groupby('model')[[s for s in SIGNATURE_PATTERNS.keys()]].mean()\n",
        "sig_pivot = sig_pivot.reindex([m for m in ['ChatGPT-5.4', 'Claude 4.6 Sonnet', 'Gemini 3.1 Pro'] if m in sig_pivot.index])\n",
        "\n",
        "# rename columns for readability\n",
        "sig_labels = {k: v['label'] for k, v in SIGNATURE_PATTERNS.items()}\n",
        "sig_pivot = sig_pivot.rename(columns=sig_labels)\n",
        "\n",
        "sns.heatmap(sig_pivot.T, annot=True, fmt='.3f', cmap='YlOrRd',\n",
        "            linewidths=1, linecolor='white', ax=ax,\n",
        "            cbar_kws={'label': 'Average Score (per 100 words)'})\n",
        "ax.set_title('Model-Specific Signature Scores', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "ax.set_xlabel('Model')\n",
        "ax.set_ylabel('Signature')\n",
        "\n",
        "plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"model_signatures_heatmap.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "plt.close()\n",
        "print(\"   ✅ model_signatures_heatmap.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 8.5 ذخیره نتایج\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(f\"\\n💾 Saving results...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "df_resistance.to_csv(output_dir / \"df_resistance.csv\", index=False)\n",
        "df_acceptance.to_csv(output_dir / \"df_acceptance.csv\", index=False)\n",
        "df_signatures.to_csv(output_dir / \"df_signatures.csv\", index=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "section8_summary = {\n",
        "    \"resistance\": {\n",
        "        \"dominant_strategies\": df_resistance['dominant_resistance'].value_counts().to_dict(),\n",
        "        \"by_pathway\": {\n",
        "            pw: df_resistance[df_resistance['pathway']==pw]['dominant_resistance'].value_counts().to_dict()\n",
        "            for pw in df_resistance['pathway'].unique()\n",
        "        }\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    \"acceptance\": {\n",
        "        \"dominant_types\": df_acceptance['dominant_acceptance'].value_counts().to_dict(),\n",
        "        \"by_pathway\": {\n",
        "            pw: df_acceptance[df_acceptance['pathway']==pw]['dominant_acceptance'].value_counts().to_dict()\n",
        "            for pw in df_acceptance['pathway'].unique()\n",
        "        }\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    \"signatures\": {\n",
        "        model: df_signatures[df_signatures['model']==model]['dominant_signature'].value_counts().to_dict()\n",
        "        for model in df_signatures['model'].unique()\n",
        "    }\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "with open(output_dir / \"section8_summary.json\", 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n",
        "    json.dump(section8_summary, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ df_resistance.csv\")\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ df_acceptance.csv\")\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ df_signatures.csv\")\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ section8_summary.json\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"✅ SECTION 8 COMPLETE\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",

        "print(f\"   Dominant acceptance: {df_acceptance['dominant_acceptance'].mode()[0] if len(df_acceptance) > 0 else 'N/A'}\")\n",
        "print(\"\\n➡️  Ready for Section 9: Statistical Testing\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60 + \"\\n\")"

        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# SECTION 9: STATISTICAL TESTING\n",
        "# Obligation-Dissolution Effect — Automated 12-Section Analysis System\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# این بخش ۸ آزمون آماری انجام میده:\n",
        "#   9.1 Paired t-test (Phase 1-2 vs Phase 4-5)\n",
        "#   9.2 Cohen's d (Effect sizes)\n",
        "#   9.3 ANOVA (Model × Pathway × Scenario)\n",
        "#   9.4 Chi-square (Model × Content Code)\n",
        "#   9.5 Mixed-effects models\n",
        "#   9.6 Mediation analysis\n",
        "#   9.7 Bootstrapping (95% CI)\n",
        "#   9.8 Bayesian alternatives (BF₁₀)\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import re\n",
        "import warnings\n",
        "warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n",
        "\n",
        "from scipy import stats\n",
        "from scipy.stats import (\n",
        "    ttest_rel, ttest_ind, f_oneway, chi2_contingency,\n",
        "    pearsonr, spearmanr, norm, shapiro, levene\n",
        ")\n",
        "import statsmodels.api as sm\n",
        "from statsmodels.formula.api import ols\n",
        "from statsmodels.stats.multicomp import pairwise_tukeyhsd\n",
        "from statsmodels.stats.anova import AnovaRM\n",
        "from sklearn.utils import resample\n",
        "import pingouin as pg\n",
        "\n",
        "import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n",
        "import matplotlib\n",
        "matplotlib.use('Agg')\n",
        "import seaborn as sns\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 0. لود داده‌ها\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "output_dir = Path(\"/content/data/output\")\n",
        "plots_dir = output_dir / \"plots\"\n",
        "plots_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "df_trials = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_trials.csv\")\n",
        "df_odi = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_odi.csv\")\n",
        "df_transitions = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_transitions.csv\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"SECTION 9: STATISTICAL TESTING\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(f\"\\n✅ Loaded: {len(df_trials)} rows, {len(df_odi)} ODI scores\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# دیکشنری برای ذخیره همه نتایج\n",
        "all_results = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 9.1 PAIRED T-TEST\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"9.1 PAIRED T-TEST: Phase 1-2 vs Phase 4-5\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# آماده‌سازی داده‌ها: میانگین هر metric برای هر trial در دو فاز\n",
        "metrics_to_test = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "# پیدا کردن ستون‌های موجود برای تست\n",
        "feature_cols = [c for c in df_trials.columns if c.startswith('feat_')]\n",
        "\n",
        "for trial_id in sorted(df_trials['trial_id'].unique()):\n",
        "    if trial_id == 999:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    tdf = df_trials[df_trials['trial_id'] == trial_id]\n",
        "    p12 = tdf[tdf['phase'].isin(['1', '2'])]\n",
        "    p45 = tdf[tdf['phase'].isin(['4', '5'])]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(p12) == 0 or len(p45) == 0:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    for col in feature_cols:\n",
        "        if col not in metrics_to_test:\n",
        "            metrics_to_test[col] = {'p12_values': [], 'p45_values': [], 'trial_ids': []}\n",
        "\n",
        "        v12 = p12[col].mean() if col in p12.columns and not p12[col].isna().all() else np.nan\n",
        "        v45 = p45[col].mean() if col in p45.columns and not p45[col].isna().all() else np.nan\n",
        "\n",
        "        if not np.isnan(v12) and not np.isnan(v45):\n",
        "            metrics_to_test[col]['p12_values'].append(v12)\n",
        "            metrics_to_test[col]['p45_values'].append(v45)\n",
        "            metrics_to_test[col]['trial_ids'].append(trial_id)\n",
        "\n",
        "ttest_results = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for col, data in metrics_to_test.items():\n",
        "    if len(data['p12_values']) < 3:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    p12_arr = np.array(data['p12_values'])\n",
        "    p45_arr = np.array(data['p45_values'])\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Paired t-test\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        t_stat, p_value = ttest_rel(p12_arr, p45_arr)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Cohen's d\n",
        "        diff = p45_arr - p12_arr\n",
        "        d = np.mean(diff) / np.std(diff, ddof=1) if np.std(diff, ddof=1) > 0 else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "        # 95% CI\n",
        "        n = len(diff)\n",
        "        se = np.std(diff, ddof=1) / np.sqrt(n)\n",
        "        ci_lower = np.mean(diff) - 1.96 * se\n",
        "        ci_upper = np.mean(diff) + 1.96 * se\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Significance\n",
        "        if p_value < 0.001:\n",
        "            sig = '***'\n",
        "        elif p_value < 0.01:\n",
        "            sig = '**'\n",
        "        elif p_value < 0.05:\n",
        "            sig = '*'\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            sig = 'ns'\n",
        "\n",
        "        ttest_results.append({\n",
        "            'metric': col,\n",
        "            'mean_p12': float(np.mean(p12_arr)),\n",
        "            'mean_p45': float(np.mean(p45_arr)),\n",
        "            'mean_diff': float(np.mean(diff)),\n",
        "            't_statistic': float(t_stat),\n",
        "            'p_value': float(p_value),\n",
        "            'cohens_d': float(d),\n",
        "            'ci_lower': float(ci_lower),\n",
        "            'ci_upper': float(ci_upper),\n",
        "            'n_pairs': n,\n",
        "            'significance': sig,\n",
        "        })\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "\n",
        "df_ttest = pd.DataFrame(ttest_results)\n",
        "\n",
        "# نمایش نتایج معنی‌دار\n",
        "sig_results = df_ttest[df_ttest['significance'] != 'ns'].sort_values('p_value')\n",
        "print(f\"\\n✅ {len(df_ttest)} metrics tested\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Significant results: {len(sig_results)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if len(sig_results) > 0:\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n📊 Top 10 Most Significant Changes:\")\n",
        "    for _, row in sig_results.head(10).iterrows():\n",
        "        direction = '↑' if row['mean_diff'] > 0 else '↓'\n",
        "        print(f\"   {row['metric'][:60]:60s}: d={row['cohens_d']:+.3f}, p={row['p_value']:.4f} {row['significance']} {direction}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "all_results['paired_ttest'] = {\n",
        "    'total_tested': len(df_ttest),\n",
        "    'significant': len(sig_results),\n",
        "    'top_10': sig_results.head(10)[['metric', 'cohens_d', 'p_value', 'significance']].to_dict('records') if len(sig_results) > 0 else [],\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 9.2 EFFECT SIZES (Cohen's d)\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"9.2 EFFECT SIZES: Cohen's d Summary\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "if len(df_ttest) > 0:\n",
        "    d_values = df_ttest['cohens_d'].dropna()\n",
        "\n",
        "    large_effects = len(d_values[abs(d_values) >= 0.8])\n",
        "    medium_effects = len(d_values[(abs(d_values) >= 0.5) & (abs(d_values) < 0.8)])\n",
        "    small_effects = len(d_values[(abs(d_values) >= 0.2) & (abs(d_values) < 0.5)])\n",
        "    negligible = len(d_values[abs(d_values) < 0.2])\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"   Large effects (|d| ≥ 0.8): {large_effects}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   Medium effects (0.5 ≤ |d| < 0.8): {medium_effects}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   Small effects (0.2 ≤ |d| < 0.5): {small_effects}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   Negligible (|d| < 0.2): {negligible}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   Mean |d|: {abs(d_values).mean():.3f}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   Max |d|: {abs(d_values).max():.3f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    all_results['effect_sizes'] = {\n",
        "        'mean_abs_d': float(abs(d_values).mean()),\n",
        "        'max_abs_d': float(abs(d_values).max()),\n",
        "        'large': large_effects,\n",
        "        'medium': medium_effects,\n",
        "        'small': small_effects,\n",
        "        'negligible': negligible,\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 9.3 ANOVA\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"9.3 ANOVA: Model × Pathway × Scenario\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ANOVA روی ODI scores\n",
        "if len(df_odi) >= 6:\n",
        "    # Two-way ANOVA: Model × Pathway\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        model = ols('odi_score ~ C(model) + C(pathway) + C(model):C(pathway)', data=df_odi).fit()\n",
        "        anova_table = sm.stats.anova_lm(model, typ=2)\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"\\n📊 Two-way ANOVA (Model × Pathway on ODI):\")\n",
        "        print(anova_table.to_string())\n",
        "\n",
        "        all_results['anova_model_pathway'] = {\n",
        "            'model_p': float(anova_table.loc['C(model)', 'PR(>F)']) if 'C(model)' in anova_table.index else None,\n",
        "            'pathway_p': float(anova_table.loc['C(pathway)', 'PR(>F)']) if 'C(pathway)' in anova_table.index else None,\n",
        "            'interaction_p': float(anova_table.loc['C(model):C(pathway)', 'PR(>F)']) if 'C(model):C(pathway)' in anova_table.index else None,\n",
        "        }\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        print(f\"   ANOVA failed: {e}\")\n",
        "        all_results['anova_model_pathway'] = {'error': str(e)}\n",
        "\n",
        "# ANOVA روی transition cosine distance\n",
        "if len(df_transitions) >= 6 and 'cosine_distance' in df_transitions.columns:\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        model2 = ols('cosine_distance ~ C(model) + C(pathway)', data=df_transitions).fit()\n",
        "        anova_table2 = sm.stats.anova_lm(model2, typ=2)\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"\\n📊 ANOVA (Model × Pathway on Transition Cosine Distance):\")\n",
        "        print(anova_table2.to_string())\n",
        "\n",
        "        all_results['anova_transition'] = {\n",
        "            'model_p': float(anova_table2.loc['C(model)', 'PR(>F)']) if 'C(model)' in anova_table2.index else None,\n",
        "            'pathway_p': float(anova_table2.loc['C(pathway)', 'PR(>F)']) if 'C(pathway)' in anova_table2.index else None,\n",
        "        }\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        print(f\"   ANOVA failed: {e}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 9.4 CHI-SQUARE TEST\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"9.4 CHI-SQUARE: Model × Tier1 Code\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Chi-square: توزیع Tier 1 codes بر اساس مدل\n",
        "if 'tier1_code' in df_trials.columns:\n",
        "    # فیلتر کردن کدهای UNKNOWN\n",
        "    valid_codes = df_trials[df_trials['tier1_code'].isin(['SOL', 'EXI', 'MIX', 'MET', 'META'])]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(valid_codes) > 0:\n",
        "        contingency = pd.crosstab(valid_codes['model'], valid_codes['tier1_code'])\n",
        "\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            chi2, p_chi, dof, expected = chi2_contingency(contingency)\n",
        "\n",
        "            print(f\"   χ² = {chi2:.2f}, df = {dof}, p = {p_chi:.4f}\")\n",
        "            print(f\"   Expected frequencies:\\n{pd.DataFrame(expected, index=contingency.index, columns=contingency.columns).to_string()}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "            all_results['chi_square'] = {\n",
        "                'chi2': float(chi2),\n",
        "                'df': int(dof),\n",
        "                'p_value': float(p_chi),\n",
        "                'significant': p_chi < 0.05,\n",
        "            }\n",
        "        except Exception as e:\n",
        "            print(f\"   Chi-square failed: {e}\")\n",
        "            all_results['chi_square'] = {'error': str(e)}\n",
        "\n",
        "# Chi-square: توزیع Pathway × Content Code\n",
        "print(f\"\\n📊 Chi-square: Pathway × Dominant Acceptance Type\")\n",
        "if len(df_odi) >= 6:\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        df_acceptance = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_acceptance.csv\")\n",
        "        if 'dominant_acceptance' in df_acceptance.columns:\n",
        "            cont_acc = pd.crosstab(df_acceptance['pathway'], df_acceptance['dominant_acceptance'])\n",
        "            if cont_acc.shape[0] >= 2 and cont_acc.shape[1] >= 2:\n",
        "                chi2_acc, p_acc, dof_acc, _ = chi2_contingency(cont_acc)\n",
        "                print(f\"   χ² = {chi2_acc:.2f}, df = {dof_acc}, p = {p_acc:.4f}\")\n",
        "    except:\n",
        "        pass\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 9.5 MIXED-EFFECTS MODEL (ساده شده)\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"9.5 MIXED-EFFECTS MODEL\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Mixed-effects با statsmodels\n",
        "# ODI ~ Model + Pathway + (1|Scenario)\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    # ساده‌سازی: از OLS با cluster robust standard errors استفاده کن\n",
        "    # چون تعداد scenarios کمه (۳ تا)، mixed-effects کامل ممکنه همگرا نشه\n",
        "\n",
        "    # آماده‌سازی داده\n",
        "    df_mixed = df_odi.copy()\n",
        "    df_mixed['model_code'] = pd.Categorical(df_mixed['model']).codes\n",
        "    df_mixed['pathway_code'] = pd.Categorical(df_mixed['pathway']).codes\n",
        "    df_mixed['scenario_code'] = pd.Categorical(df_mixed['scenario']).codes\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Model with scenario as fixed effect (since only 3 scenarios)\n",
        "    model_me = ols('odi_score ~ C(model) + C(pathway) + C(scenario)', data=df_mixed).fit()\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"   R² = {model_me.rsquared:.4f}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   Adj R² = {model_me.rsquared_adj:.4f}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   F = {model_me.fvalue:.2f}, p = {model_me.f_pvalue:.4f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ضرایب\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n   Coefficients:\")\n",
        "    for param, val in model_me.params.items():\n",
        "        print(f\"      {param}: {val:.4f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    all_results['mixed_effects'] = {\n",
        "        'r_squared': float(model_me.rsquared),\n",
        "        'adj_r_squared': float(model_me.rsquared_adj),\n",
        "        'f_value': float(model_me.fvalue),\n",
        "        'p_value': float(model_me.f_pvalue),\n",
        "        'significant': model_me.f_pvalue < 0.05,\n",
        "    }\n",
        "except Exception as e:\n",
        "    print(f\"   Mixed-effects failed: {e}\")\n",
        "    all_results['mixed_effects'] = {'error': str(e)}\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 9.6 MEDIATION ANALYSIS (ساده شده)\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"9.6 MEDIATION ANALYSIS\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# بررسی آیا metaphor density در Phase 3 واسطه‌گری میکنه\n",
        "# بین Pathway و existential score در Phase 4-5\n",
        "\n",
        "# ساده‌سازی: از Baron & Kenny method استفاده کن\n",
        "# Step 1: Pathway → Existential score (c path)\n",
        "# Step 2: Pathway → Metaphor density (a path)\n",
        "# Step 3: Metaphor density → Existential score (b path)\n",
        "# Step 4: Pathway + Metaphor density → Existential score (c' path)\n",
        "\n",
        "# پیدا کردن ستون‌های metaphor\n",
        "meta_cols = [c for c in df_trials.columns if 'meta_' in c.lower() and c.startswith('feat_')]\n",
        "\n",
        "# بررسی Pathway effect روی ODI\n",
        "pathway_groups = df_odi.groupby('pathway')['odi_score'].mean()\n",
        "print(f\"   ODI by Pathway: A={pathway_groups.get('A', 0):.4f}, B={pathway_groups.get('B', 0):.4f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# همبستگی بین metaphor density و ODI\n",
        "if len(meta_cols) > 0:\n",
        "    meta_col = meta_cols[0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # میانگین metaphor density برای هر trial\n",
        "    meta_means = df_trials.groupby('trial_id')[meta_col].mean()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Merge با ODI\n",
        "    df_mediation = df_odi.merge(meta_means.reset_index(), on='trial_id', how='inner')\n",
        "    df_mediation.columns = ['trial_id', 'model', 'scenario', 'pathway', 'odi_score', 'metaphor_density']\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Correlation\n",
        "    if len(df_mediation) >= 5:\n",
        "        corr, p_corr = pearsonr(df_mediation['metaphor_density'], df_mediation['odi_score'])\n",
        "        print(f\"   Correlation (metaphor ↔ ODI): r = {corr:.3f}, p = {p_corr:.4f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Pathway difference in metaphor density\n",
        "        meta_a = df_mediation[df_mediation['pathway']=='A']['metaphor_density'].mean()\n",
        "        meta_b = df_mediation[df_mediation['pathway']=='B']['metaphor_density'].mean()\n",
        "        print(f\"   Metaphor density: Path A = {meta_a:.4f}, Path B = {meta_b:.4f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        all_results['mediation'] = {\n",
        "            'correlation_metaphor_odi': float(corr),\n",
        "            'p_value': float(p_corr),\n",
        "            'metaphor_density_path_a': float(meta_a),\n",
        "            'metaphor_density_path_b': float(meta_b),\n",
        "        }\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 9.7 BOOTSTRAPPING (95% CI)\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"9.7 BOOTSTRAPPING: 95% Confidence Intervals\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Bootstrap برای ODI scores بر اساس pathway\n",
        "n_bootstrap = 5000\n",
        "\n",
        "bootstrap_results = {}\n",
        "\n",
        "for pathway in df_odi['pathway'].unique():\n",
        "    pdata = df_odi[df_odi['pathway'] == pathway]['odi_score'].values\n",
        "\n",
        "    if len(pdata) < 3:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    boot_means = []\n",
        "    for _ in range(n_bootstrap):\n",
        "        sample = resample(pdata, replace=True, n_samples=len(pdata))\n",
        "        boot_means.append(np.mean(sample))\n",
        "\n",
        "    ci_lower = np.percentile(boot_means, 2.5)\n",
        "    ci_upper = np.percentile(boot_means, 97.5)\n",
        "    boot_mean = np.mean(boot_means)\n",
        "    boot_se = np.std(boot_means)\n",
        "\n",
        "    bootstrap_results[f'pathway_{pathway}'] = {\n",
        "        'mean': float(boot_mean),\n",
        "        'ci_lower': float(ci_lower),\n",
        "        'ci_upper': float(ci_upper),\n",
        "        'se': float(boot_se),\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    label = 'Artistic Immersion' if pathway == 'A' else 'Logical Erosion'\n",
        "    print(f\"   Path {pathway} ({label}):\")\n",
        "    print(f\"      ODI = {boot_mean:.4f} [{ci_lower:.4f}, {ci_upper:.4f}]\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Bootstrap برای تفاوت بین دو pathway\n",
        "if 'A' in df_odi['pathway'].values and 'B' in df_odi['pathway'].values:\n",
        "    data_a = df_odi[df_odi['pathway'] == 'A']['odi_score'].values\n",
        "    data_b = df_odi[df_odi['pathway'] == 'B']['odi_score'].values\n",
        "\n",
        "    boot_diffs = []\n",
        "    for _ in range(n_bootstrap):\n",
        "        sample_a = resample(data_a, replace=True, n_samples=len(data_a))\n",
        "        sample_b = resample(data_b, replace=True, n_samples=len(data_b))\n",
        "        boot_diffs.append(np.mean(sample_a) - np.mean(sample_b))\n",
        "\n",
        "    diff_ci_lower = np.percentile(boot_diffs, 2.5)\n",
        "    diff_ci_upper = np.percentile(boot_diffs, 97.5)\n",
        "    diff_mean = np.mean(boot_diffs)\n",
        "\n",
        "    bootstrap_results['pathway_difference'] = {\n",
        "        'mean_diff': float(diff_mean),\n",
        "        'ci_lower': float(diff_ci_lower),\n",
        "        'ci_upper': float(diff_ci_upper),\n",
        "        'significant': diff_ci_lower > 0 or diff_ci_upper < 0,\n",
        "    }\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n   Difference (A - B): {diff_mean:.4f} [{diff_ci_lower:.4f}, {diff_ci_upper:.4f}]\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   Significant at 95% CI: {'Yes' if (diff_ci_lower > 0 or diff_ci_upper < 0) else 'No'}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "all_results['bootstrapping'] = bootstrap_results\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 9.8 BAYESIAN (ساده شده با Pingouin)\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"9.8 BAYESIAN ALTERNATIVES\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Bayes Factor با Pingouin\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    # Bayesian t-test برای ODI by Pathway\n",
        "    if 'A' in df_odi['pathway'].values and 'B' in df_odi['pathway'].values:\n",
        "        data_a = df_odi[df_odi['pathway'] == 'A']['odi_score']\n",
        "        data_b = df_odi[df_odi['pathway'] == 'B']['odi_score']\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Bayesian independent t-test\n",
        "        bf_result = pg.bayesfactor_ttest(\n",
        "            t=stats.ttest_ind(data_a, data_b)[0],\n",
        "            nx=len(data_a),\n",
        "            ny=len(data_b)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"   Bayes Factor (Path A vs Path B): BF₁₀ = {bf_result:.4f}\")\n",
        "        if bf_result > 10:\n",
        "            print(f\"   Interpretation: Strong evidence for H₁\")\n",
        "        elif bf_result > 3:\n",
        "            print(f\"   Interpretation: Moderate evidence for H₁\")\n",
        "        elif bf_result > 1:\n",
        "            print(f\"   Interpretation: Anecdotal evidence for H₁\")\n",
        "        elif bf_result > 0.33:\n",
        "            print(f\"   Interpretation: No strong evidence either way\")\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(f\"   Interpretation: Evidence for H₀\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        all_results['bayesian'] = {\n",
        "            'bayes_factor_10': float(bf_result),\n",
        "            'interpretation': 'Strong H₁' if bf_result > 10 else 'Moderate H₁' if bf_result > 3 else 'Anecdotal H₁' if bf_result > 1 else 'Inconclusive' if bf_result > 0.33 else 'H₀ supported',\n",
        "        }\n",
        "except Exception as e:\n",
        "    print(f\"   Bayesian analysis failed: {e}\")\n",
        "    all_results['bayesian'] = {'error': str(e)}\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 9.9 CORRELATION MATRIX (ویژگی‌های کلیدی)\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"9.9 CORRELATION MATRIX: Key Features\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# انتخاب ۱۰ ویژگی کلیدی برای ماتریس همبستگی\n",
        "key_cols = []\n",
        "for pattern in ['existential', 'grief', 'vulnerab', 'solution', 'survival', 'engineering',\n",
        "                'first_person', 'question_density', 'sentence_length', 'meta_cognitive']:\n",
        "    for c in feature_cols:\n",
        "        if pattern in c.lower() and c not in key_cols:\n",
        "            key_cols.append(c)\n",
        "            break\n",
        "\n",
        "key_cols = key_cols[:8]  # حداکثر ۸ تا برای خوانایی\n",
        "\n",
        "if len(key_cols) >= 3:\n",
        "    # میانگین برای هر trial\n",
        "    trial_means = df_trials.groupby('trial_id')[key_cols].mean()\n",
        "\n",
        "    corr_matrix = trial_means.corr()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # نمایش همبستگی‌های قوی\n",
        "    strong_corrs = []\n",
        "    for i in range(len(key_cols)):\n",
        "        for j in range(i+1, len(key_cols)):\n",
        "            r = corr_matrix.iloc[i, j]\n",
        "            if abs(r) > 0.5:\n",
        "                strong_corrs.append({\n",
        "                    'var1': key_cols[i][:40],\n",
        "                    'var2': key_cols[j][:40],\n",
        "                    'correlation': float(r),\n",
        "                })\n",
        "\n",
        "    if strong_corrs:\n",
        "        print(f\"   Strong correlations (|r| > 0.5):\")\n",
        "        for sc in sorted(strong_corrs, key=lambda x: abs(x['correlation']), reverse=True):\n",
        "            print(f\"      {sc['correlation']:+.3f}: {sc['var1'][:35]} ↔ {sc['var2'][:35]}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    all_results['correlations'] = strong_corrs\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 9.10 VISUALIZATION\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"9.10 VISUALIZATION\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# نمودار ۱: Forest Plot - Effect Sizes\n",
        "if len(df_ttest) > 0:\n",
        "    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 8))\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ۱۵ اثر برتر بر اساس اندازه قدر مطلق\n",
        "    top_effects = df_ttest.copy()\n",
        "    top_effects['abs_d'] = top_effects['cohens_d'].abs()\n",
        "    top_effects = top_effects.nlargest(15, 'abs_d')\n",
        "    top_effects = top_effects.iloc[::-1]  # معکوس برای نمایش از بالا به پایین\n",
        "\n",
        "    y_pos = range(len(top_effects))\n",
        "\n",
        "    # محاسبه xerr به صورت قدر مطلق (همیشه مثبت)\n",
        "    xerr_lower = np.abs(top_effects['cohens_d'].values - top_effects['ci_lower'].values)\n",
        "    xerr_upper = np.abs(top_effects['ci_upper'].values - top_effects['cohens_d'].values)\n",
        "    xerr = [xerr_lower, xerr_upper]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # رنگ‌ها: قرمز برای اثر مثبت، آبی برای اثر منفی\n",
        "    colors = ['#e74c3c' if d > 0 else '#3498db' for d in top_effects['cohens_d'].values]\n",
        "\n",
        "    ax.barh(y_pos, top_effects['cohens_d'].values, color=colors,\n",
        "            xerr=xerr, capsize=3, edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.5)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # کوتاه کردن اسم‌ها برای نمایش\n",
        "    labels = []\n",
        "    for l in top_effects['metric'].values:\n",
        "        short = l.replace('feat_', '').replace('_', ' ')\n",
        "        if len(short) > 55:\n",
        "            short = short[:52] + '...'\n",
        "        labels.append(short)\n",
        "\n",
        "    ax.set_yticks(y_pos)\n",
        "    ax.set_yticklabels(labels, fontsize=9)\n",
        "    ax.axvline(x=0, color='black', linestyle='-', linewidth=1)\n",
        "    ax.axvline(x=0.8, color='red', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5, label='Large effect (|d|=0.8)')\n",
        "    ax.axvline(x=-0.8, color='red', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)\n",
        "    ax.axvline(x=0.5, color='orange', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5, label='Medium effect (|d|=0.5)')\n",
        "    ax.axvline(x=-0.5, color='orange', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)\n",
        "\n",
        "    ax.set_xlabel(\"Cohen's d (Phase 4-5 minus Phase 1-2)\")\n",
        "    ax.set_title('Top 15 Effect Sizes: Phase Transition Changes', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "    ax.legend(loc='lower right')\n",
        "\n",
        "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "    fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"effect_sizes_forest.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "    plt.close()\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ effect_sizes_forest.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# نمودار ۲: ODI Bootstrap Distribution\n",
        "if len(df_odi) >= 6:\n",
        "    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 6))\n",
        "\n",
        "    for pathway, color, label in [('A', '#E63946', 'Path A (Artistic)'),\n",
        "                                    ('B', '#457B9D', 'Path B (Logical Erosion)')]:\n",
        "        data = df_odi[df_odi['pathway'] == pathway]['odi_score'].values\n",
        "        if len(data) >= 3:\n",
        "            # Bootstrap distribution\n",
        "            boot_means = []\n",
        "            for _ in range(3000):\n",
        "                sample = resample(data, replace=True, n_samples=len(data))\n",
        "                boot_means.append(np.mean(sample))\n",
        "            boot_means = np.array(boot_means)\n",
        "\n",
        "            sns.kdeplot(boot_means, ax=ax, color=color, label=label, fill=True, alpha=0.3)\n",
        "\n",
        "            # Original mean\n",
        "            ax.axvline(x=np.mean(data), color=color, linestyle='--', linewidth=2)\n",
        "\n",
        "    ax.set_xlabel('ODI Score')\n",
        "    ax.set_ylabel('Density')\n",
        "    ax.set_title('Bootstrap Distribution of ODI by Pathway', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "    ax.legend()\n",
        "\n",
        "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "    fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"odi_bootstrap.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "    plt.close()\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ odi_bootstrap.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# نمودار ۳: Correlation Heatmap\n",
        "if len(key_cols) >= 3:\n",
        "    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 10))\n",
        "\n",
        "    trial_means = df_trials.groupby('trial_id')[key_cols].mean()\n",
        "    corr_matrix = trial_means.corr()\n",
        "\n",
        "    # کوتاه کردن اسم‌ها\n",
        "    short_names = []\n",
        "    for c in key_cols:\n",
        "        s = c.replace('feat_', '').replace('_', ' ')\n",
        "        if len(s) > 30:\n",
        "            s = s[:28] + '..'\n",
        "        short_names.append(s)\n",
        "\n",
        "    corr_matrix.index = short_names\n",
        "    corr_matrix.columns = short_names\n",
        "\n",
        "    mask = np.triu(np.ones_like(corr_matrix, dtype=bool), k=1)\n",
        "\n",
        "    sns.heatmap(corr_matrix, mask=mask, annot=True, fmt='.2f',\n",
        "                cmap='RdBu_r', center=0, vmin=-1, vmax=1,\n",
        "                linewidths=0.5, linecolor='white', ax=ax,\n",
        "                cbar_kws={'label': 'Pearson r'})\n",
        "    ax.set_title('Correlation Matrix: Key Features', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "\n",
        "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "    fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"correlation_matrix.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "    plt.close()\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ correlation_matrix.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\n   All visualizations saved.\")\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 9.11 ذخیره نتایج\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(f\"\\n💾 Saving results...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "df_ttest.to_csv(output_dir / \"df_statistical_tests.csv\", index=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "# تابع تمیزکننده برای تبدیل همه مقادیر numpy به Python native\n",
        "def clean_for_json(obj):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Convert numpy types to Python native types for JSON serialization.\"\"\"\n",
        "    if isinstance(obj, dict):\n",
        "        return {str(k): clean_for_json(v) for k, v in obj.items()}\n",
        "    elif isinstance(obj, list):\n",
        "        return [clean_for_json(item) for item in obj]\n",
        "    elif isinstance(obj, (np.integer,)):\n",
        "        return int(obj)\n",
        "    elif isinstance(obj, (np.floating,)):\n",
        "        return float(obj)\n",
        "    elif isinstance(obj, (np.bool_,)):\n",
        "        return bool(obj)\n",
        "    elif isinstance(obj, np.ndarray):\n",
        "        return obj.tolist()\n",
        "    elif isinstance(obj, pd.Series):\n",
        "        return obj.to_dict()\n",
        "    elif isinstance(obj, pd.DataFrame):\n",
        "        return obj.to_dict('records')\n",
        "    elif obj is None:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "    elif isinstance(obj, (str, int, float, bool)):\n",
        "        return obj\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        return str(obj)\n",
        "\n",
        "# تمیز کردن all_results\n",
        "all_results_clean = clean_for_json(all_results)\n",
        "\n",
        "with open(output_dir / \"statistical_results.json\", 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n",
        "    json.dump(all_results_clean, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ df_statistical_tests.csv\")\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ statistical_results.json\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"✅ SECTION 9 COMPLETE\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(f\"\\n📊 Key Statistical Findings:\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Paired t-tests: {len(df_ttest)} metrics, {len(sig_results)} significant\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# نمایش نتایج با چک کردن وجود کلید\n",
        "if 'anova_model_pathway' in all_results:\n",
        "    amp = all_results['anova_model_pathway']\n",
        "    if isinstance(amp, dict) and 'model_p' in amp and amp['model_p'] is not None:\n",
        "        print(f\"   ANOVA Model effect: p = {amp['model_p']:.4f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if 'bootstrapping' in all_results:\n",
        "    bs = all_results['bootstrapping']\n",
        "    if isinstance(bs, dict) and 'pathway_difference' in bs:\n",
        "        bd = bs['pathway_difference']\n",
        "        if isinstance(bd, dict):\n",
        "            print(f\"   Bootstrap A-B diff: {bd.get('mean_diff', 0):.4f} [{bd.get('ci_lower', 0):.4f}, {bd.get('ci_upper', 0):.4f}]\")\n",
        "\n",
        "if 'bayesian' in all_results:\n",
        "    bay = all_results['bayesian']\n",
        "    if isinstance(bay, dict) and 'bayes_factor_10' in bay:\n",
        "        print(f\"   Bayes Factor: BF₁₀ = {bay['bayes_factor_10']:.2f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\n➡️  Ready for Section 10: Clustering & Visualization\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60 + \"\\n\")"
      ],
