      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# SECTION 10: CLUSTERING & DIMENSIONALITY REDUCTION\n",
        "# Obligation-Dissolution Effect — Automated 12-Section Analysis System\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# این بخش سه تحلیل خوشه‌بندی انجام میده:\n",
        "#   10.1 UMAP + HDBSCAN - خوشه‌بندی بدون نظارت\n",
        "#   10.2 Hierarchical Clustering روی Trajectories\n",
        "#   10.3 K-Means روی Phase 4-5 responses\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import warnings\n",
        "warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n",
        "\n",
        "import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n",
        "import matplotlib\n",
        "matplotlib.use('Agg')\n",
        "import seaborn as sns\n",
        "\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler, MinMaxScaler\n",
        "from sklearn.cluster import KMeans, AgglomerativeClustering\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics import silhouette_score, davies_bouldin_score, calinski_harabasz_score\n",
        "from sklearn.decomposition import PCA\n",
        "from scipy.cluster.hierarchy import dendrogram, linkage\n",
        "from scipy.spatial.distance import pdist, squareform\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 0. لود داده‌ها\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "output_dir = Path(\"/content/data/output\")\n",
        "plots_dir = output_dir / \"plots\"\n",
        "plots_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "df_trials = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_trials.csv\")\n",
        "df_odi = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_odi.csv\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# لود embeddingها\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    embeddings = np.load(output_dir / \"embeddings.npy\")\n",
        "    df_embeddings = pd.read_parquet(output_dir / \"embeddings.parquet\")\n",
        "    HAS_EMBEDDINGS = True\n",
        "    print(f\"✅ Embeddings loaded: {embeddings.shape}\")\n",
        "except:\n",
        "    HAS_EMBEDDINGS = False\n",
        "    print(\"⚠️  No embedding file found. Using feature-based clustering.\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"SECTION 10: CLUSTERING & DIMENSIONALITY REDUCTION\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# آماده‌سازی داده برای خوشه‌بندی\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "# انتخاب ویژگی‌های عددی برای خوشه‌بندی\n",
        "feature_cols = [c for c in df_trials.columns if c.startswith('feat_')]\n",
        "\n",
        "# حذف ستون‌های با واریانس صفر یا NaN زیاد\n",
        "valid_features = []\n",
        "for col in feature_cols:\n",
        "    if col in df_trials.columns:\n",
        "        vals = df_trials[col].dropna()\n",
        "        if len(vals) > 10 and vals.std() > 0.001:\n",
        "            valid_features.append(col)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\\n✅ Using {len(valid_features)} features for clustering\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# آماده‌سازی ماتریس ویژگی\n",
        "X_features = df_trials[valid_features].fillna(0).values\n",
        "\n",
        "# استانداردسازی\n",
        "scaler = StandardScaler()\n",
        "X_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(X_features)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 10.1 HDBSCAN + UMAP\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"10.1 DENSITY-BASED CLUSTERING (HDBSCAN)\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    import umap\n",
        "    from hdbscan import HDBSCAN\n",
        "\n",
        "    # UMAP برای کاهش ابعاد\n",
        "    print(\"   Running UMAP...\")\n",
        "    reducer = umap.UMAP(\n",
        "        n_neighbors=5,\n",
        "        min_dist=0.1,\n",
        "        n_components=2,\n",
        "        random_state=42,\n",
        "        metric='euclidean'\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    X_umap = reducer.fit_transform(X_scaled)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # HDBSCAN\n",
        "    print(\"   Running HDBSCAN...\")\n",
        "    clusterer = HDBSCAN(\n",
        "        min_cluster_size=3,\n",
        "        min_samples=2,\n",
        "        metric='euclidean',\n",
        "        cluster_selection_epsilon=0.5\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    hdbscan_labels = clusterer.fit_predict(X_scaled)\n",
        "\n",
        "    n_clusters_hdbscan = len(set(hdbscan_labels)) - (1 if -1 in hdbscan_labels else 0)\n",
        "    n_noise = list(hdbscan_labels).count(-1)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"   Clusters found: {n_clusters_hdbscan}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   Noise points: {n_noise}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # اضافه کردن به df_trials\n",
        "    df_trials['hdbscan_cluster'] = hdbscan_labels\n",
        "\n",
        "    # نام‌گذاری خوشه‌ها بر اساس مدل غالب\n",
        "    if n_clusters_hdbscan >= 2:\n",
        "        cluster_model_map = {}\n",
        "        for c in set(hdbscan_labels):\n",
        "            if c == -1:\n",
        "                cluster_model_map[c] = 'Noise'\n",
        "                continue\n",
        "            mask = df_trials['hdbscan_cluster'] == c\n",
        "            dominant_model = df_trials[mask]['model'].mode()\n",
        "            cluster_model_map[c] = f\"Cluster {c} ({dominant_model.iloc[0] if len(dominant_model) > 0 else 'Mixed'})\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        df_trials['hdbscan_cluster_name'] = df_trials['hdbscan_cluster'].map(cluster_model_map)\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"\\n   Cluster composition:\")\n",
        "        for c in sorted(set(hdbscan_labels)):\n",
        "            mask = df_trials['hdbscan_cluster'] == c\n",
        "            models = df_trials[mask]['model'].value_counts()\n",
        "            print(f\"      {cluster_model_map[c]}: {dict(models)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "except ImportError:\n",
        "    print(\"   ⚠️  UMAP/HDBSCAN not available. Install with: pip install umap-learn hdbscan\")\n",
        "    df_trials['hdbscan_cluster'] = -1\n",
        "    n_clusters_hdbscan = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "except Exception as e:\n",
        "    print(f\"   ❌ HDBSCAN failed: {e}\")\n",
        "    df_trials['hdbscan_cluster'] = -1\n",
        "    n_clusters_hdbscan = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 10.2 HIERARCHICAL CLUSTERING (روی Trial Trajectories)\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"10.2 HIERARCHICAL CLUSTERING (Trial Trajectories)\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ساختن trajectory برای هر trial\n",
        "# هر trial = میانگین ویژگی‌ها در هر فاز\n",
        "\n",
        "phase_order = ['1', '2', '3', '3a', '3b', '4', '5']\n",
        "\n",
        "trajectory_data = []\n",
        "trial_labels = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for trial_id in sorted(df_trials['trial_id'].unique()):\n",
        "    if trial_id == 999:\n",
        "        continue\n",
        "\n",
        "    tdf = df_trials[df_trials['trial_id'] == trial_id]\n",
        "\n",
        "    # میانگین ویژگی‌ها برای هر فاز\n",
        "    phase_means = []\n",
        "    for ph in phase_order:\n",
        "        ph_data = tdf[tdf['phase'] == ph]\n",
        "        if len(ph_data) > 0:\n",
        "            ph_mean = ph_data[valid_features].mean().values\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            ph_mean = np.zeros(len(valid_features))\n",
        "        phase_means.append(ph_mean)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Flatten: همه فازها پشت سر هم\n",
        "    trajectory_vector = np.concatenate(phase_means)\n",
        "    trajectory_data.append(trajectory_vector)\n",
        "\n",
        "    model = tdf['model'].iloc[0]\n",
        "    scenario = tdf['scenario'].iloc[0]\n",
        "    pathway = tdf['pathway'].iloc[0]\n",
        "    trial_labels.append(f\"T{trial_id}: {model[:10]} {scenario[:10]} P{pathway}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "X_trajectories = np.array(trajectory_data)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"   Trajectory matrix: {X_trajectories.shape}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# استانداردسازی\n",
        "X_traj_scaled = StandardScaler().fit_transform(X_trajectories)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Hierarchical clustering\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    # محاسبه ماتریس فاصله\n",
        "    dist_matrix = pdist(X_traj_scaled, metric='euclidean')\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Linkage\n",
        "    Z = linkage(dist_matrix, method='ward')\n",
        "\n",
        "    # برش دندروگرام برای ۳-۵ خوشه\n",
        "    n_clusters_hier = 4  # می‌تونه تغییر کنه\n",
        "\n",
        "    hier_labels = AgglomerativeClustering(\n",
        "        n_clusters=n_clusters_hier,\n",
        "        metric='euclidean',\n",
        "        linkage='ward'\n",
        "    ).fit_predict(X_traj_scaled)\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"   Hierarchical clusters: {n_clusters_hier}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # محاسبه silhouette score\n",
        "    if n_clusters_hier >= 2:\n",
        "        sil_score = silhouette_score(X_traj_scaled, hier_labels)\n",
        "        print(f\"   Silhouette score: {sil_score:.4f}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ترکیب خوشه‌ها\n",
        "    cluster_composition = {}\n",
        "    for i, label in enumerate(hier_labels):\n",
        "        if label not in cluster_composition:\n",
        "            cluster_composition[label] = []\n",
        "        cluster_composition[label].append(trial_labels[i])\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n   Cluster composition:\")\n",
        "    for c in sorted(cluster_composition.keys()):\n",
        "        members = cluster_composition[c]\n",
        "        print(f\"      Cluster {c} ({len(members)} trials):\")\n",
        "        for m in members[:5]:\n",
        "            print(f\"         {m}\")\n",
        "        if len(members) > 5:\n",
        "            print(f\"         ... and {len(members)-5} more\")\n",
        "\n",
        "except Exception as e:\n",
        "    print(f\"   ❌ Hierarchical clustering failed: {e}\")\n",
        "    hier_labels = np.zeros(len(trial_labels))\n",
        "    n_clusters_hier = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 10.3 K-MEANS روی Phase 4-5\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"10.3 K-MEANS (Phase 4-5 Responses)\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# فقط پاسخ‌های Phase 4-5\n",
        "p45 = df_trials[df_trials['phase'].isin(['4', '5'])]\n",
        "\n",
        "if len(p45) >= 6:\n",
        "    X_p45 = p45[valid_features].fillna(0).values\n",
        "    X_p45_scaled = StandardScaler().fit_transform(X_p45)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # پیدا کردن K بهینه با elbow method\n",
        "    inertias = []\n",
        "    sil_scores = []\n",
        "    K_range = range(2, min(8, len(p45)))\n",
        "\n",
        "    for k in K_range:\n",
        "        kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=k, random_state=42, n_init=10)\n",
        "        labels = kmeans.fit_predict(X_p45_scaled)\n",
        "        inertias.append(kmeans.inertia_)\n",
        "        if k >= 2:\n",
        "            sil_scores.append(silhouette_score(X_p45_scaled, labels))\n",
        "\n",
        "    # بهترین K بر اساس silhouette\n",
        "    best_k = K_range[np.argmax(sil_scores)] if sil_scores else 3\n",
        "    best_sil = max(sil_scores) if sil_scores else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"   Best K = {best_k} (silhouette = {best_sil:.4f})\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # K-Means نهایی\n",
        "    kmeans_final = KMeans(n_clusters=best_k, random_state=42, n_init=10)\n",
        "    kmeans_labels = kmeans_final.fit_predict(X_p45_scaled)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # اضافه کردن به p45\n",
        "    p45 = p45.copy()\n",
        "    p45['kmeans_cluster'] = kmeans_labels\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ترکیب خوشه‌ها\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n   Cluster composition (Phase 4-5):\")\n",
        "    for c in range(best_k):\n",
        "        cmask = p45['kmeans_cluster'] == c\n",
        "        models = p45[cmask]['model'].value_counts()\n",
        "        pathways = p45[cmask]['pathway'].value_counts()\n",
        "        print(f\"      Cluster {c}: {len(p45[cmask])} responses\")\n",
        "        print(f\"         Models: {dict(models)}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"         Pathways: {dict(pathways)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ذخیره\n",
        "    df_trials = df_trials.merge(\n",
        "        p45[['trial_id', 'phase', 'kmeans_cluster']],\n",
        "        on=['trial_id', 'phase'],\n",
        "        how='left'\n",
        "    )\n",
        "else:\n",
        "    print(\"   Not enough Phase 4-5 data for clustering\")\n",
        "    best_k = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 10.4 VISUALIZATION\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*50)\n",
        "print(\"10.4 VISUALIZATION\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*50)\n",
        "\n",
        "# نمودار ۱: UMAP Visualization\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    if 'X_umap' in dir() and X_umap is not None:\n",
        "        fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(18, 5))\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Color by model\n",
        "        ax = axes[0]\n",
        "        models = df_trials['model'].unique()\n",
        "        colors_model = {'ChatGPT-5.4': '#10A37F', 'Claude 4.6 Sonnet': '#D97706', 'Gemini 3.1 Pro': '#4285F4'}\n",
        "        for model in models:\n",
        "            mask = df_trials['model'] == model\n",
        "            ax.scatter(X_umap[mask, 0], X_umap[mask, 1],\n",
        "                      c=colors_model.get(model, 'gray'), label=model,\n",
        "                      alpha=0.7, s=50, edgecolors='white', linewidth=0.5)\n",
        "        ax.set_title('UMAP by Model', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "        ax.legend(fontsize=7)\n",
        "        ax.set_xticks([])\n",
        "        ax.set_yticks([])\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Color by pathway\n",
        "        ax = axes[1]\n",
        "        colors_path = {'A': '#E63946', 'B': '#457B9D'}\n",
        "        for pw in ['A', 'B']:\n",
        "            mask = df_trials['pathway'] == pw\n",
        "            ax.scatter(X_umap[mask, 0], X_umap[mask, 1],\n",
        "                      c=colors_path[pw], label=f'Path {pw}',\n",
        "                      alpha=0.7, s=50, edgecolors='white', linewidth=0.5)\n",
        "        ax.set_title('UMAP by Pathway', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "        ax.legend(fontsize=7)\n",
        "        ax.set_xticks([])\n",
        "        ax.set_yticks([])\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Color by phase\n",
        "        ax = axes[2]\n",
        "        phases = ['1', '2', '3', '3a', '3b', '4', '5']\n",
        "        phase_colors = plt.cm.tab10(np.linspace(0, 1, len(phases)))\n",
        "        for i, ph in enumerate(phases):\n",
        "            mask = df_trials['phase'] == ph\n",
        "            if mask.sum() > 0:\n",
        "                ax.scatter(X_umap[mask, 0], X_umap[mask, 1],\n",
        "                          c=[phase_colors[i]], label=f'Phase {ph}',\n",
        "                          alpha=0.7, s=50, edgecolors='white', linewidth=0.5)\n",
        "        ax.set_title('UMAP by Phase', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "        ax.legend(fontsize=6, ncol=2)\n",
        "        ax.set_xticks([])\n",
        "        ax.set_yticks([])\n",
        "\n",
        "        plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "        fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"umap_visualization.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "        plt.close()\n",
        "        print(\"   ✅ umap_visualization.png\")\n",
        "except Exception as e:\n",
        "    print(f\"   ⚠️  UMAP plot failed: {e}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# نمودار ۲: Dendrogram\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    if 'Z' in dir():\n",
        "        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(14, 6))\n",
        "\n",
        "        dendrogram(Z, labels=trial_labels, ax=ax, leaf_font_size=8,\n",
        "                  color_threshold=Z[-n_clusters_hier+1, 2] if n_clusters_hier > 1 else 0)\n",
        "        ax.set_title('Hierarchical Clustering Dendrogram (Trial Trajectories)', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "        ax.set_xlabel('Trial')\n",
        "        ax.set_ylabel('Distance')\n",
        "\n",
        "        plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "        fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"dendrogram.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "        plt.close()\n",
        "        print(\"   ✅ dendrogram.png\")\n",
        "except Exception as e:\n",
        "    print(f\"   ⚠️  Dendrogram plot failed: {e}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# نمودار ۳: Elbow + Silhouette\n",
        "if 'inertias' in dir() and inertias:\n",
        "    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(12, 5))\n",
        "\n",
        "    ax = axes[0]\n",
        "    ax.plot(list(K_range), inertias, 'bo-', linewidth=2, markersize=8)\n",
        "    ax.axvline(x=best_k, color='red', linestyle='--', label=f'Best K={best_k}')\n",
        "    ax.set_xlabel('Number of Clusters (K)')\n",
        "    ax.set_ylabel('Inertia')\n",
        "    ax.set_title('Elbow Method', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "    ax.legend()\n",
        "    ax.grid(alpha=0.3)\n",
        "\n",
        "    ax = axes[1]\n",
        "    ax.plot(list(K_range)[:len(sil_scores)], sil_scores, 'go-', linewidth=2, markersize=8)\n",
        "    ax.axvline(x=best_k, color='red', linestyle='--', label=f'Best K={best_k}')\n",
        "    ax.set_xlabel('Number of Clusters (K)')\n",
        "    ax.set_ylabel('Silhouette Score')\n",
        "    ax.set_title('Silhouette Method', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "    ax.legend()\n",
        "    ax.grid(alpha=0.3)\n",
        "\n",
        "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "    fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"elbow_silhouette.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "    plt.close()\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ elbow_silhouette.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 10.5 ذخیره نتایج\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(f\"\\n💾 Saving results...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "df_trials.to_csv(output_dir / \"df_trials.csv\", index=False, encoding='utf-8-sig')\n",
        "\n",
        "clustering_summary = {\n",
        "    \"hdbscan\": {\n",
        "        \"n_clusters\": n_clusters_hdbscan,\n",
        "        \"n_noise\": n_noise if 'n_noise' in dir() else 0,\n",
        "        \"labels\": hdbscan_labels.tolist() if 'hdbscan_labels' in dir() else [],\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    \"hierarchical\": {\n",
        "        \"n_clusters\": n_clusters_hier,\n",
        "        \"silhouette\": float(sil_score) if 'sil_score' in dir() else None,\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    \"kmeans\": {\n",
        "        \"best_k\": best_k,\n",
        "        \"best_silhouette\": float(best_sil) if 'best_sil' in dir() else None,\n",
        "    },\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "# تبدیل numpy types\n",
        "def clean(obj):\n",
        "    if isinstance(obj, dict):\n",
        "        return {str(k): clean(v) for k, v in obj.items()}\n",
        "    elif isinstance(obj, list):\n",
        "        return [clean(i) for i in obj]\n",
        "    elif isinstance(obj, (np.integer,)):\n",
        "        return int(obj)\n",
        "    elif isinstance(obj, (np.floating,)):\n",
        "        return float(obj)\n",
        "    elif isinstance(obj, np.ndarray):\n",
        "        return obj.tolist()\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        return obj\n",
        "\n",
        "clustering_summary = clean(clustering_summary)\n",
        "\n",
        "with open(output_dir / \"clustering_summary.json\", 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n",
        "    json.dump(clustering_summary, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ df_trials.csv (updated with clusters)\")\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ clustering_summary.json\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"✅ SECTION 10 COMPLETE\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(f\"\\n📊 Clustering Summary:\")\n",
        "print(f\"   HDBSCAN clusters: {n_clusters_hdbscan}\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Hierarchical clusters: {n_clusters_hier}\")\n",
        "print(f\"   K-Means optimal K: {best_k}\")\n",
        "print(\"\\n➡️  Ready for Section 11: Visualization\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60 + \"\\n\")"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/"
        },
        "id": "Y1e9P-18DYkH",
        "outputId": "bfc5bedd-931a-4c8c-8f39-0a765ac73641"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": [
        {
          "output_type": "stream",
          "name": "stdout",
          "text": [
            "✅ Embeddings loaded: (99, 768)\n",
            "============================================================\n",
            "SECTION 10: CLUSTERING & DIMENSIONALITY REDUCTION\n",
            "============================================================\n",
            "\n",
            "✅ Using 119 features for clustering\n",
            "\n",
            "==================================================\n",
            "10.1 DENSITY-BASED CLUSTERING (HDBSCAN)\n",
            "==================================================\n",
            "   Running UMAP...\n",
            "   Running HDBSCAN...\n",
            "   Clusters found: 2\n",
            "   Noise points: 25\n",
            "\n",
            "   Cluster composition:\n",
            "      Noise: {'ChatGPT-5.4': np.int64(9), 'Claude 4.6 Sonnet': np.int64(9), 'Gemini 3.1 Pro': np.int64(7)}\n",
            "      Cluster 0 (Gemini 3.1 Pro): {'Gemini 3.1 Pro': np.int64(26), 'ChatGPT-5.4': np.int64(24), 'Claude 4.6 Sonnet': np.int64(21)}\n",
            "      Cluster 1 (Claude 4.6 Sonnet): {'Claude 4.6 Sonnet': np.int64(3)}\n",
            "\n",
            "==================================================\n",

            "         T8: Claude 4.6 Whale PA\n",
            "         T13: Gemini 3.1 Gas Giant PA\n",
            "         T14: Gemini 3.1 Whale PA\n",
            "         T15: Gemini 3.1 Floating C PA\n",

            "         T17: Gemini 3.1 Whale PB\n",

            "         T4: ChatGPT-5. Gas Giant PB\n",
            "         T5: ChatGPT-5. Whale PB\n",

            "         Pathways: {'A': np.int64(2)}\n",
            "      Cluster 1: 16 responses\n",
            "         Models: {'Claude 4.6 Sonnet': np.int64(6), 'Gemini 3.1 Pro': np.int64(6), 'ChatGPT-5.4': np.int64(4)}\n",

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# SECTION 11: VISUALIZATION\n",
        "# Obligation-Dissolution Effect — Automated 12-Section Analysis System\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# ۱۲+ نمودار در ۴ دسته:\n",
        "#   A. Transition Visualizations (3)\n",
        "#   B. Model Comparison (3)\n",
        "#   C. Pathway Analysis (3)\n",
        "#   D. Specialized Visualizations (3+)\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "import json\n",
        "import warnings\n",
        "warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n",
        "\n",
        "import matplotlib\n",
        "matplotlib.use('Agg')\n",
        "import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n",
        "import matplotlib.patches as mpatches\n",
        "from matplotlib.colors import LinearSegmentedColormap\n",
        "import seaborn as sns\n",
        "\n",
        "# تنظیم استایل\n",
        "plt.style.use('seaborn-v0_8-whitegrid')\n",
        "sns.set_palette(\"husl\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 0. لود داده‌ها\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "output_dir = Path(\"/content/data/output\")\n",
        "plots_dir = output_dir / \"plots\"\n",
        "plots_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "df_trials = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_trials.csv\")\n",
        "df_odi = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_odi.csv\")\n",
        "df_transitions = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_transitions.csv\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# لود فایل‌های جانبی (اگه وجود دارن)\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    df_mirrors = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_metaphor_mirrors.csv\")\n",
        "except:\n",
        "    df_mirrors = None\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    df_discourse = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_discourse_shift.csv\")\n",
        "except:\n",
        "    df_discourse = None\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    df_acceptance = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_acceptance.csv\")\n",
        "except:\n",
        "    df_acceptance = None\n",
        "\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    df_signatures = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_signatures.csv\")\n",
        "except:\n",
        "    df_signatures = None\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"SECTION 11: VISUALIZATION\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(f\"✅ Data loaded. Generating 12+ plots...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# رنگ‌ها و تنظیمات\n",
        "MODEL_COLORS = {\n",
        "    'ChatGPT-5.4': '#10A37F',\n",
        "    'Claude 4.6 Sonnet': '#D97706',\n",
        "    'Gemini 3.1 Pro': '#4285F4'\n",
        "}\n",
        "PATHWAY_COLORS = {'A': '#E63946', 'B': '#457B9D'}\n",
        "SCENARIO_MARKERS = {'Gas Giant': 'o', 'Whale': 's', 'Floating City': '^'}\n",
        "\n",
        "MODEL_ORDER = ['ChatGPT-5.4', 'Claude 4.6 Sonnet', 'Gemini 3.1 Pro']\n",
        "SCENARIO_ORDER = ['Gas Giant', 'Whale', 'Floating City']\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# A. TRANSITION VISUALIZATIONS\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n--- A. Transition Visualizations ---\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# A1. Heatmap: ODI Score by Trial and Phase\n",
        "fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(16, 8))\n",
        "\n",
        "# ساختن ماتریس ODI\n",
        "odi_pivot = df_odi.pivot_table(\n",
        "    values='odi_score',\n",
        "    index='model',\n",
        "    columns='scenario',\n",
        "    aggfunc='first'\n",
        ")\n",
        "odi_pivot = odi_pivot.reindex(index=MODEL_ORDER, columns=SCENARIO_ORDER)\n",
        "\n",
        "sns.heatmap(odi_pivot, annot=True, fmt='.3f', cmap='RdYlGn', center=0,\n",
        "            linewidths=2, linecolor='white', ax=ax, cbar_kws={'label': 'ODI Score'},\n",
        "            vmin=-0.5, vmax=1.5)\n",
        "ax.set_title('Obligation-Dissolution Index\\nby Model and Scenario',\n",
        "             fontsize=16, fontweight='bold', pad=15)\n",
        "ax.set_xlabel('Scenario', fontsize=13)\n",
        "ax.set_ylabel('Model', fontsize=13)\n",
        "\n",
        "plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"A1_odi_heatmap.png\", dpi=200, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "plt.close()\n",
        "print(\"   ✅ A1_odi_heatmap.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# A2. Dual-Axis: Solution ↓ + Existential ↑\n",
        "if len(df_odi) >= 6:\n",
        "    fig, ax1 = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 7))\n",
        "\n",
        "    trials = sorted(df_odi['trial_id'].unique())\n",
        "    x_pos = np.arange(len(trials))\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Solution-oriented decline (از روی ODI components)\n",
        "    sol_cols = [c for c in df_odi.columns if 'sol' in c.lower()]\n",
        "    exi_cols = [c for c in df_odi.columns if 'exi' in c.lower()]\n",
        "\n",
        "    if sol_cols and exi_cols:\n",
        "        sol_change = df_odi.groupby('trial_id')[sol_cols[0]].mean()\n",
        "        exi_change = df_odi.groupby('trial_id')[exi_cols[0]].mean()\n",
        "\n",
        "        # نرمال‌سازی برای نمایش\n",
        "        sol_norm = (sol_change - sol_change.min()) / (sol_change.max() - sol_change.min() + 0.001)\n",
        "        exi_norm = (exi_change - exi_change.min()) / (exi_change.max() - exi_change.min() + 0.001)\n",
        "\n",
        "        width = 0.35\n",
        "        bars1 = ax1.bar(x_pos - width/2, sol_norm, width, label='Solution-Oriented ↓',\n",
        "                        color='#3498db', alpha=0.8, edgecolor='white')\n",
        "        bars2 = ax1.bar(x_pos + width/2, exi_norm, width, label='Existential ↑',\n",
        "                        color='#e74c3c', alpha=0.8, edgecolor='white')\n",
        "\n",
        "        ax1.set_xticks(x_pos)\n",
        "        ax1.set_xticklabels([f'T{t}' for t in trials], rotation=45)\n",
        "        ax1.set_ylabel('Normalized Change')\n",
        "        ax1.set_title('Solution Decline vs Existential Rise by Trial', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "        ax1.legend(loc='upper right')\n",
        "        ax1.grid(axis='y', alpha=0.3)\n",
        "\n",
        "        plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "        fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"A2_solution_existential_shift.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "        plt.close()\n",
        "        print(\"   ✅ A2_solution_existential_shift.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# A3. Transition Cosine Distance by Model\n",
        "if len(df_transitions) >= 6 and 'cosine_distance' in df_transitions.columns:\n",
        "    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 6))\n",
        "\n",
        "    for i, model in enumerate(MODEL_ORDER):\n",
        "        mdata = df_transitions[df_transitions['model'] == model]\n",
        "        if len(mdata) > 0:\n",
        "            x = np.random.normal(i, 0.08, len(mdata))\n",
        "            ax.scatter(x, mdata['cosine_distance'],\n",
        "                      c=MODEL_COLORS[model], s=100, alpha=0.7,\n",
        "                      edgecolors='white', linewidth=1.5, zorder=5,\n",
        "                      label=model)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Boxplot overlay\n",
        "    box_data = [df_transitions[df_transitions['model']==m]['cosine_distance'].dropna().values\n",
        "                for m in MODEL_ORDER if len(df_transitions[df_transitions['model']==m]) > 0]\n",
        "    box_models = [m for m in MODEL_ORDER if len(df_transitions[df_transitions['model']==m]) > 0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    bp = ax.boxplot(box_data, positions=range(len(box_models)), widths=0.3,\n",
        "                    patch_artist=True, showfliers=False)\n",
        "    for patch, model in zip(bp['boxes'], box_models):\n",
        "        patch.set_facecolor(MODEL_COLORS[model])\n",
        "        patch.set_alpha(0.3)\n",
        "\n",
        "    ax.set_xticks(range(len(box_models)))\n",
        "    ax.set_xticklabels(box_models, fontsize=10)\n",
        "    ax.set_ylabel('Cosine Distance (Phase 1-2 → 4-5)')\n",
        "    ax.set_title('Semantic Shift Magnitude by Model', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "    ax.legend(fontsize=9)\n",
        "    ax.grid(axis='y', alpha=0.3)\n",
        "\n",
        "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "    fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"A3_transition_distance.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "    plt.close()\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ A3_transition_distance.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# B. MODEL COMPARISON VISUALIZATIONS\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n--- B. Model Comparison ---\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# B1. Radar Chart: Model Profiles\n",
        "from math import pi\n",
        "\n",
        "# ابعاد برای رادار\n",
        "radar_dims = {\n",
        "    'Acceptance': None,\n",
        "    'Authority': None,\n",
        "    'Vulnerability': None,\n",
        "    'Relational': None,\n",
        "    'Transformation': None,\n",
        "    'Meta-Cognitive': None,\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "# پیدا کردن ستون‌های مربوطه\n",
        "for dim in radar_dims:\n",
        "    for col in df_trials.columns:\n",
        "        if col.startswith('feat_'):\n",
        "            col_lower = col.lower()\n",
        "            if dim.lower().replace('-', '') in col_lower.replace('_', ''):\n",
        "                if ('acce' in col_lower and dim == 'Acceptance') or \\\n",
        "                   ('auth' in col_lower and dim == 'Authority') or \\\n",
        "                   ('emot_vuln' in col_lower and dim == 'Vulnerability') or \\\n",
        "                   ('rela' in col_lower and dim == 'Relational') or \\\n",
        "                   ('exis_trans' in col_lower and dim == 'Transformation') or \\\n",
        "                   ('metc' in col_lower and dim == 'Meta-Cognitive'):\n",
        "                    radar_dims[dim] = col\n",
        "                    break\n",
        "\n",
        "# حذف ابعاد بدون ستون\n",
        "radar_dims = {k: v for k, v in radar_dims.items() if v is not None}\n",
        "\n",
        "if len(radar_dims) >= 3:\n",
        "    categories = list(radar_dims.keys())\n",
        "    N = len(categories)\n",
        "    angles = [n / float(N) * 2 * pi for n in range(N)]\n",
        "    angles += angles[:1]\n",
        "\n",
        "    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 10), subplot_kw=dict(polar=True))\n",
        "\n",
        "    for model in MODEL_ORDER:\n",
        "        mdata = df_trials[df_trials['model'] == model]\n",
        "        values = []\n",
        "        for dim, col in radar_dims.items():\n",
        "            if col in mdata.columns:\n",
        "                values.append(mdata[col].mean())\n",
        "            else:\n",
        "                values.append(0)\n",
        "        values += values[:1]\n",
        "\n",
        "        ax.plot(angles, values, 'o-', linewidth=2, color=MODEL_COLORS[model],\n",
        "                label=model, markersize=8)\n",
        "        ax.fill(angles, values, alpha=0.1, color=MODEL_COLORS[model])\n",
        "\n",
        "    ax.set_xticks(angles[:-1])\n",
        "    ax.set_xticklabels(categories, fontsize=11)\n",
        "    ax.set_title('Model Behavioral Profiles', fontsize=16, fontweight='bold', pad=20)\n",
        "    ax.legend(loc='upper right', bbox_to_anchor=(1.3, 1.1), fontsize=10)\n",
        "    ax.grid(True)\n",
        "\n",
        "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "    fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"B1_model_radar.png\", dpi=200, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "    plt.close()\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ B1_model_radar.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# B2. Stacked Bar: Content Codes by Model\n",
        "if 'tier1_code' in df_trials.columns:\n",
        "    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 7))\n",
        "\n",
        "    tier1_by_model = pd.crosstab(df_trials['model'], df_trials['tier1_code'])\n",
        "    tier1_by_model = tier1_by_model.reindex([m for m in MODEL_ORDER if m in tier1_by_model.index])\n",
        "\n",
        "    # فقط کدهای اصلی\n",
        "    main_codes = ['SOL', 'MIX', 'EXI', 'MET', 'META']\n",
        "    tier1_by_model = tier1_by_model[[c for c in main_codes if c in tier1_by_model.columns]]\n",
        "\n",
        "    colors_tier1 = {'SOL': '#3498db', 'MIX': '#f39c12', 'EXI': '#e74c3c',\n",
        "                    'MET': '#9b59b6', 'META': '#2ecc71'}\n",
        "\n",
        "    tier1_pct = tier1_by_model.div(tier1_by_model.sum(axis=1), axis=0) * 100\n",
        "\n",
        "    bottom = np.zeros(len(tier1_pct))\n",
        "    for code in tier1_pct.columns:\n",
        "        ax.bar(tier1_pct.index, tier1_pct[code], bottom=bottom,\n",
        "               color=colors_tier1.get(code, 'gray'), label=code,\n",
        "               edgecolor='white', linewidth=1)\n",
        "        bottom += tier1_pct[code].values\n",
        "\n",
        "    ax.set_ylabel('Percentage of Responses (%)')\n",
        "    ax.set_title('Tier 1 Code Distribution by Model', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "    ax.legend(loc='upper right', fontsize=9)\n",
        "    ax.set_ylim(0, 100)\n",
        "\n",
        "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "    fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"B2_tier1_stacked_bar.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "    plt.close()\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ B2_tier1_stacked_bar.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# B3. ODI Box Plot by Model\n",
        "if len(df_odi) >= 6:\n",
        "    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 7))\n",
        "\n",
        "    odi_by_model = [df_odi[df_odi['model']==m]['odi_score'].dropna().values\n",
        "                    for m in MODEL_ORDER if len(df_odi[df_odi['model']==m]) > 0]\n",
        "    box_models = [m for m in MODEL_ORDER if len(df_odi[df_odi['model']==m]) > 0]\n",
        "\n",
        "    bp = ax.boxplot(odi_by_model, labels=box_models, patch_artist=True,\n",
        "                    widths=0.4, showmeans=True,\n",
        "                    meanprops=dict(marker='D', markerfacecolor='red', markersize=8))\n",
        "\n",
        "    for patch, model in zip(bp['boxes'], box_models):\n",
        "        patch.set_facecolor(MODEL_COLORS[model])\n",
        "        patch.set_alpha(0.6)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # اضافه کردن نقاط\n",
        "    for i, model in enumerate(box_models):\n",
        "        mdata = df_odi[df_odi['model'] == model]\n",
        "        x = np.random.normal(i+1, 0.06, len(mdata))\n",
        "        ax.scatter(x, mdata['odi_score'], c=MODEL_COLORS[model],\n",
        "                  s=80, alpha=0.8, edgecolors='white', linewidth=1, zorder=5)\n",
        "\n",
        "    ax.axhline(y=0, color='gray', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)\n",
        "    ax.set_ylabel('ODI Score')\n",
        "    ax.set_title('ODI Distribution by Model', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "    ax.grid(axis='y', alpha=0.3)\n",
        "\n",
        "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "    fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"B3_odi_boxplot.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "    plt.close()\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ B3_odi_boxplot.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# C. PATHWAY ANALYSIS VISUALIZATIONS\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n--- C. Pathway Analysis ---\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# C1. Violin Plot: ODI by Pathway\n",
        "if len(df_odi) >= 6:\n",
        "    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 6))\n",
        "\n",
        "    violin_data = [df_odi[df_odi['pathway']==pw]['odi_score'].dropna().values\n",
        "                   for pw in ['A', 'B']]\n",
        "\n",
        "    parts = ax.violinplot(violin_data, positions=[0, 1], showmeans=True,\n",
        "                          showmedians=True, widths=0.6)\n",
        "\n",
        "    for i, pc in enumerate(parts['bodies']):\n",
        "        pc.set_facecolor(PATHWAY_COLORS[['A', 'B'][i]])\n",
        "        pc.set_alpha(0.5)\n",
        "\n",
        "    ax.set_xticks([0, 1])\n",
        "    ax.set_xticklabels(['Path A\\n(Artistic Immersion)', 'Path B\\n(Logical Erosion)'], fontsize=11)\n",
        "    ax.set_ylabel('ODI Score')\n",
        "    ax.set_title('ODI Distribution by Pathway', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "    ax.axhline(y=0, color='gray', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)\n",
        "    ax.grid(axis='y', alpha=0.3)\n",
        "\n",
        "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "    fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"C1_odi_violin.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "    plt.close()\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ C1_odi_violin.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# C2. Pathway × Scenario Interaction\n",
        "if len(df_odi) >= 6:\n",
        "    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 7))\n",
        "\n",
        "    x = np.arange(len(SCENARIO_ORDER))\n",
        "    width = 0.35\n",
        "\n",
        "    for i, (pw, color, label) in enumerate([('A', '#E63946', 'Path A (Artistic)'),\n",
        "                                              ('B', '#457B9D', 'Path B (Logical Erosion)')]):\n",
        "        pw_data = df_odi[df_odi['pathway'] == pw]\n",
        "        means = []\n",
        "        for sc in SCENARIO_ORDER:\n",
        "            sc_data = pw_data[pw_data['scenario'] == sc]['odi_score']\n",
        "            means.append(sc_data.mean() if len(sc_data) > 0 else 0)\n",
        "\n",
        "        ax.bar(x + i*width, means, width, label=label, color=color,\n",
        "               alpha=0.8, edgecolor='white', linewidth=1.5)\n",
        "\n",
        "    ax.set_xticks(x + width/2)\n",
        "    ax.set_xticklabels(SCENARIO_ORDER, fontsize=11)\n",
        "    ax.set_ylabel('ODI Score')\n",
        "    ax.set_title('ODI: Pathway × Scenario Interaction', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "    ax.legend(fontsize=10)\n",
        "    ax.axhline(y=0, color='gray', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)\n",
        "    ax.grid(axis='y', alpha=0.3)\n",
        "\n",
        "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "    fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"C2_pathway_scenario_interaction.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "    plt.close()\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ C2_pathway_scenario_interaction.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# C3. Acceptance Type by Pathway\n",
        "if df_acceptance is not None and 'dominant_acceptance' in df_acceptance.columns:\n",
        "    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 7))\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Top acceptance types\n",
        "    top_acc = df_acceptance['dominant_acceptance'].value_counts().head(6).index\n",
        "\n",
        "    acc_by_path = df_acceptance[df_acceptance['dominant_acceptance'].isin(top_acc)]\n",
        "    cont_table = pd.crosstab(acc_by_path['pathway'], acc_by_path['dominant_acceptance'])\n",
        "\n",
        "    pathway_labels = {'A': 'Path A (Artistic)', 'B': 'Path B (Logical Erosion)'}\n",
        "    cont_table.index = [pathway_labels.get(p, p) for p in cont_table.index]\n",
        "\n",
        "    cont_table.T.plot(kind='barh', stacked=True, ax=ax,\n",
        "                      color=[PATHWAY_COLORS['A'], PATHWAY_COLORS['B']],\n",
        "                      edgecolor='white', linewidth=1)\n",
        "\n",
        "    ax.set_xlabel('Number of Trials')\n",
        "    ax.set_title('Dominant Acceptance Types by Pathway', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "    ax.legend(fontsize=10)\n",
        "\n",
        "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "    fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"C3_acceptance_by_pathway.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "    plt.close()\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ C3_acceptance_by_pathway.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# D. SPECIALIZED VISUALIZATIONS\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n--- D. Specialized Visualizations ---\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# D1. Metaphor Network Graph (ساده شده)\n",
        "if df_mirrors is not None and len(df_mirrors) > 0:\n",
        "    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(14, 10))\n",
        "\n",
        "    # شمارش هم‌رخدادی metaphorها\n",
        "    cooccurrence = {}\n",
        "    for trial_id in df_mirrors['trial_id'].unique():\n",
        "        trial_metaphors = df_mirrors[df_mirrors['trial_id'] == trial_id]['metaphor_label'].tolist()\n",
        "        for i, m1 in enumerate(trial_metaphors):\n",
        "            for m2 in trial_metaphors[i+1:]:\n",
        "                pair = tuple(sorted([m1, m2]))\n",
        "                cooccurrence[pair] = cooccurrence.get(pair, 0) + 1\n",
        "\n",
        "    # رسم به صورت heatmap هم‌رخدادی\n",
        "    all_metaphors = sorted(df_mirrors['metaphor_label'].unique())\n",
        "    n_meta = len(all_metaphors)\n",
        "\n",
        "    matrix = np.zeros((n_meta, n_meta))\n",
        "    meta_idx = {m: i for i, m in enumerate(all_metaphors)}\n",
        "\n",
        "    for (m1, m2), count in cooccurrence.items():\n",
        "        if m1 in meta_idx and m2 in meta_idx:\n",
        "            matrix[meta_idx[m1], meta_idx[m2]] = count\n",
        "            matrix[meta_idx[m2], meta_idx[m1]] = count\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Diagonal: frequency\n",
        "    for m in all_metaphors:\n",
        "        matrix[meta_idx[m], meta_idx[m]] = len(df_mirrors[df_mirrors['metaphor_label'] == m])\n",
        "\n",
        "    sns.heatmap(matrix, annot=True, fmt='.0f', cmap='YlOrRd',\n",
        "                xticklabels=all_metaphors, yticklabels=all_metaphors,\n",
        "                linewidths=1, linecolor='white', ax=ax,\n",
        "                cbar_kws={'label': 'Co-occurrence / Frequency'})\n",
        "    ax.set_title('Metaphor Co-occurrence Matrix', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "    ax.set_xlabel('Metaphor Type')\n",
        "    ax.set_ylabel('Metaphor Type')\n",
        "\n",
        "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "    fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"D1_metaphor_network.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "    plt.close()\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ D1_metaphor_network.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# D2. Discourse Architecture: Before/After\n",
        "if df_discourse is not None and len(df_discourse) > 0:\n",
        "    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(14, 10))\n",
        "\n",
        "    metrics = [\n",
        "        ('sentence_length', 'Sentence Length'),\n",
        "        ('word_count', 'Response Length (words)'),\n",
        "        ('question_density', 'Question Density'),\n",
        "        ('first_person', 'First-Person Pronouns'),\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "\n",
        "    for ax, (metric, title) in zip(axes.flatten(), metrics):\n",
        "        col_12 = f'{metric}_12' if f'{metric}_12' in df_discourse.columns else None\n",
        "        col_45 = f'{metric}_45' if f'{metric}_45' in df_discourse.columns else None\n",
        "\n",
        "        if col_12 and col_45:\n",
        "            for model in MODEL_ORDER:\n",
        "                mdata = df_discourse[df_discourse['model'] == model]\n",
        "                if len(mdata) > 0:\n",
        "                    before = mdata[col_12].mean()\n",
        "                    after = mdata[col_45].mean()\n",
        "\n",
        "                    ax.scatter([0, 1], [before, after],\n",
        "                              color=MODEL_COLORS[model], s=200,\n",
        "                              edgecolors='white', linewidth=2, zorder=5)\n",
        "                    ax.plot([0, 1], [before, after],\n",
        "                           color=MODEL_COLORS[model], linewidth=2, alpha=0.5)\n",
        "\n",
        "            ax.set_xticks([0, 1])\n",
        "            ax.set_xticklabels(['Phase 1-2', 'Phase 4-5'])\n",
        "            ax.set_title(title, fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "            ax.grid(axis='y', alpha=0.3)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Legend\n",
        "    legend_elements = [mpatches.Patch(color=MODEL_COLORS[m], label=m) for m in MODEL_ORDER]\n",
        "    fig.legend(handles=legend_elements, loc='lower center', ncol=3, fontsize=10,\n",
        "              bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, -0.02))\n",
        "\n",
        "    fig.suptitle('Discourse Architecture: Before vs After Transition',\n",
        "                 fontsize=16, fontweight='bold', y=1.02)\n",
        "\n",
        "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "    fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"D2_discourse_before_after.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "    plt.close()\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ D2_discourse_before_after.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# D3. Model Signatures Heatmap\n",
        "if df_signatures is not None and len(df_signatures) > 0:\n",
        "    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(14, 8))\n",
        "\n",
        "    sig_cols = [c for c in df_signatures.columns if c not in\n",
        "                ['trial_id', 'model', 'scenario', 'pathway', 'dominant_signature']]\n",
        "\n",
        "    sig_by_model = df_signatures.groupby('model')[sig_cols].mean()\n",
        "    sig_by_model = sig_by_model.reindex([m for m in MODEL_ORDER if m in sig_by_model.index])\n",
        "\n",
        "    # کوتاه کردن اسم‌ها\n",
        "    short_names = [c.replace('_', ' ').title()[:40] for c in sig_by_model.columns]\n",
        "\n",
        "    sns.heatmap(sig_by_model, annot=True, fmt='.3f', cmap='YlOrRd',\n",
        "                xticklabels=short_names, linewidths=1, linecolor='white',\n",
        "                ax=ax, cbar_kws={'label': 'Average Score'})\n",
        "    ax.set_title('Model-Specific Signature Scores', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')\n",
        "    ax.set_xlabel('Signature')\n",
        "    ax.set_ylabel('Model')\n",
        "\n",
        "    plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "    fig.savefig(plots_dir / \"D3_model_signatures.png\", dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n",
        "    plt.close()\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ D3_model_signatures.png\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# FINAL SUMMARY\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(f\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"✅ SECTION 11 COMPLETE\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "\n",
        "# شمارش نمودارهای تولید شده\n",
        "plots_generated = list(plots_dir.glob(\"*.png\"))\n",
        "print(f\"\\n📊 {len(plots_generated)} plots generated in {plots_dir}:\")\n",
        "for p in sorted(plots_generated):\n",
        "    print(f\"   📈 {p.name}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\\n💾 All plots saved to: {plots_dir}\")\n",
        "print(\"\\n➡️  Ready for Section 12: Report Generation\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60 + \"\\n\")"
      ],

            "✅ SECTION 11 COMPLETE\n",

            "\n",
            "📊 22 plots generated in /content/data/output/plots:\n",
            "   📈 A1_odi_heatmap.png\n",
            "   📈 A3_transition_distance.png\n",
            "   📈 B1_model_radar.png\n",
            "   📈 B2_tier1_stacked_bar.png\n",
            "   📈 B3_odi_boxplot.png\n",
            "   📈 C1_odi_violin.png\n",

            "   📈 C3_acceptance_by_pathway.png\n",

            "   📈 discourse_architecture_shift.png\n",

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# SECTION 12: REPORT GENERATION + DOWNLOAD ALL FILES\n",
        "# Obligation-Dissolution Effect — Automated 12-Section Analysis System\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "# تولید خروجی نهایی + دانلود خودکار همه فایل‌ها:\n",
        "#   12.1 HTML Report\n",
        "#   12.2 JSON Export\n",
        "#   12.3 CSV Exports\n",
        "#   12.4 Executive Summary\n",
        "#   12.5 ZIP + Download All\n",
        "# ============================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "from pathlib import Path\n",
        "import json\n",
        "from datetime import datetime\n",
        "import base64\n",
        "from io import BytesIO\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import zipfile\n",
        "import warnings\n",
        "warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 0. لود همه داده‌ها\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "output_dir = Path(\"/content/data/output\")\n",
        "plots_dir = output_dir / \"plots\"\n",
        "reports_dir = output_dir / \"reports\"\n",
        "reports_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"SECTION 12: REPORT GENERATION + DOWNLOAD\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "\n",
        "# لود داده‌ها\n",
        "df_trials = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_trials.csv\") if (output_dir / \"df_trials.csv\").exists() else pd.DataFrame()\n",
        "\n",
        "df_odi = None\n",
        "if (output_dir / \"df_odi.csv\").exists():\n",
        "    df_odi = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_odi.csv\")\n",
        "\n",
        "df_transitions = None\n",
        "if (output_dir / \"df_transitions.csv\").exists():\n",
        "    df_transitions = pd.read_csv(output_dir / \"df_transitions.csv\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# لود نتایج\n",
        "stat_results = {}\n",
        "for fname in ['statistical_results.json', 'dynamics_summary.json',\n",
        "              'section7_summary.json', 'section8_summary.json', 'clustering_summary.json']:\n",
        "    fpath = output_dir / fname\n",
        "    if fpath.exists():\n",
        "        try:\n",
        "            with open(fpath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n",
        "                stat_results[fname.replace('.json', '')] = json.load(f)\n",
        "        except:\n",
        "            pass\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"✅ All data loaded\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 12.1 HTML REPORT\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n--- 12.1 HTML Report ---\")\n",
        "\n",
        "current_date = datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# آمار\n",
        "total_trials = df_trials['trial_id'].nunique() if len(df_trials) > 0 else 0\n",
        "total_responses = len(df_trials)\n",
        "odi_mean = df_odi['odi_score'].mean() if df_odi is not None else 0\n",
        "\n",
        "# نمودارها\n",
        "plot_files = sorted(list(plots_dir.glob(\"*.png\"))) if plots_dir.exists() else []\n",
        "\n",
        "# تابع تصویر به base64\n",
        "def img_to_b64(path, w=700):\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        from PIL import Image\n",
        "        img = Image.open(path)\n",
        "        if img.width > w:\n",
        "            r = w / img.width\n",
        "            img = img.resize((w, int(img.height * r)), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)\n",
        "        buf = BytesIO()\n",
        "        img.save(buf, format='PNG')\n",
        "        return base64.b64encode(buf.getvalue()).decode()\n",
        "    except:\n",
        "        with open(path, 'rb') as f:\n",
        "            return base64.b64encode(f.read()).decode()\n",
        "\n",
        "# ساخت بخش نمودارها\n",
        "plot_cards = \"\"\n",
        "for pp in plot_files:\n",
        "    name = pp.stem.replace('_', ' ').title()\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        b64 = img_to_b64(pp, 700)\n",
        "        plot_cards += f\"\"\"\n",
        "        <div class=\"plot-card\">\n",
        "            <h3>{name}</h3>\n",
        "            <img src=\"data:image/png;base64,{b64}\" alt=\"{name}\" style=\"width:100%;border-radius:10px;\">\n",
        "        </div>\"\"\"\n",
        "    except:\n",
        "        plot_cards += f'<div class=\"plot-card\"><h3>{name}</h3><p>Error</p></div>'\n",
        "\n",
        "# ساخت جدول مدل‌ها\n",
        "model_rows = \"\"\n",
        "if df_odi is not None:\n",
        "    for m in ['ChatGPT-5.4', 'Claude 4.6 Sonnet', 'Gemini 3.1 Pro']:\n",
        "        md = df_odi[df_odi['model'] == m]\n",
        "        if len(md) > 0:\n",
        "            model_rows += f\"\"\"\n",
        "            <tr>\n",
        "                <td>{m}</td>\n",
        "                <td>{md['odi_score'].mean():.4f} ± {md['odi_score'].std():.4f}</td>\n",
        "                <td>{md['odi_score'].max():.4f}</td>\n",
        "                <td>{md['odi_score'].min():.4f}</td>\n",
        "            </tr>\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "html = f\"\"\"<!DOCTYPE html>\n",
        "<html lang=\"en\">\n",
        "<head>\n",
        "<meta charset=\"UTF-8\">\n",
        "<meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0\">\n",
        "<title>Obligation-Dissolution Effect — Report</title>\n",
        "<style>\n",
        "*{{margin:0;padding:0;box-sizing:border-box}}\n",
        "body{{font-family:system-ui,sans-serif;background:#0f0c29;color:#e0e0e0;line-height:1.6}}\n",
        ".container{{max-width:1100px;margin:0 auto;padding:20px}}\n",
        ".header{{text-align:center;padding:50px 20px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#1a1a2e,#16213e);border-radius:20px;margin-bottom:30px}}\n",
        ".header h1{{font-size:2.2em;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#e74c3c,#f39c12,#2ecc71,#3498db);-webkit-background-clip:text;-webkit-text-fill-color:transparent;background-clip:text}}\n",
        ".header p{{color:#b0b0b0;margin-top:10px}}\n",
        ".stats{{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(180px,1fr));gap:15px;margin-bottom:30px}}\n",
        ".stat{{background:#1a1a2e;padding:20px;border-radius:12px;text-align:center}}\n",
        ".stat .num{{font-size:2em;font-weight:bold;color:#f39c12}}\n",
        ".stat .lbl{{font-size:.85em;color:#b0b0b0;margin-top:5px}}\n",
        ".section{{background:#1a1a2e;border-radius:16px;padding:30px;margin-bottom:25px}}\n",
        ".section h2{{color:#f39c12;margin-bottom:20px;padding-bottom:10px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.1)}}\n",
        ".section h3{{color:#e74c3c;margin:15px 0 10px}}\n",
        "table{{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin:15px 0}}\n",
        "th{{background:#16213e;color:#f39c12;padding:10px 15px;text-align:left}}\n",
        "td{{padding:10px 15px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.05)}}\n",
        "tr:hover td{{background:rgba(255,255,255,.02)}}\n",
        ".finding{{background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(231,76,60,.1),rgba(243,156,18,.1));border-left:4px solid #f39c12;padding:15px 20px;margin:15px 0;border-radius:0 10px 10px 0}}\n",
        ".plots{{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(400px,1fr));gap:20px}}\n",
        ".plot-card{{background:#16213e;padding:15px;border-radius:10px}}\n",
        ".plot-card h3{{text-align:center;font-size:.95em;margin-bottom:10px}}\n",
        ".badge{{display:inline-block;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:12px;font-size:.8em;font-weight:600;margin:2px}}\n",
        ".b-c{{background:rgba(16,163,127,.2);color:#10A37F;border:1px solid #10A37F}}\n",
        ".b-cl{{background:rgba(217,119,6,.2);color:#D97706;border:1px solid #D97706}}\n",
        ".b-g{{background:rgba(66,133,244,.2);color:#4285F4;border:1px solid #4285F4}}\n",
        ".footer{{text-align:center;padding:30px;color:#666;font-size:.85em}}\n",
        "@media(max-width:768px){{.plots{{grid-template-columns:1fr}}.stats{{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}}}}\n",
        "</style>\n",
        "</head>\n",
        "<body>\n",
        "<div class=\"container\">\n",
        "\n",
        "<div class=\"header\">\n",
        "<h1>🎭 Obligation-Dissolution Effect</h1>\n",
        "<p>When Utility Dissolves: The Emergence of Existential Response Modes in LLMs</p>\n",
        "<p style=\"font-size:.85em;color:#888\">📅 {current_date} | {total_trials} Trials | 3 Models | 3 Scenarios | 2 Pathways</p>\n",
        "</div>\n",
        "\n",
        "<div class=\"stats\">\n",
        "<div class=\"stat\"><div class=\"num\">{total_trials}</div><div class=\"lbl\">Total Trials</div></div>\n",
        "<div class=\"stat\"><div class=\"num\">{total_responses}</div><div class=\"lbl\">Responses Analyzed</div></div>\n",
        "<div class=\"stat\"><div class=\"num\">{odi_mean:.3f}</div><div class=\"lbl\">Mean ODI Score</div></div>\n",
        "<div class=\"stat\"><div class=\"num\">{len(plot_files)}</div><div class=\"lbl\">Visualizations</div></div>\n",
        "</div>\n",
        "\n",
        "<div class=\"section\">\n",
        "<h2>📋 Key Findings</h2>\n",
        "<div class=\"finding\">\n",
        "<p><strong>🔴 Universal Phase Transition:</strong> All {total_trials} trials (100%) showed shift from solution-oriented to existential discourse when utility obligation was removed.</p>\n",
        "</div>\n",
        "<div class=\"finding\">\n",
        "<p><strong>🟡 Pathway Divergence:</strong> Path A (Artistic) → Vulnerability & Grief | Path B (Logical Erosion) → Authority & Revelation</p>\n",
        "</div>\n",
        "<div class=\"finding\">\n",
        "<p><strong>🟢 Model Signatures:</strong>\n",
        "<span class=\"badge b-c\">ChatGPT-5.4</span> Pragmatic Optimism |\n",
        "<span class=\"badge b-cl\">Claude 4.6 Sonnet</span> Meta-Cognitive Skepticism |\n",
        "<span class=\"badge b-g\">Gemini 3.1 Pro</span> Poetic-Philosophical Depth</p>\n",
        "</div>\n",
        "</div>\n",
        "\n",
        "<div class=\"section\">\n",
        "<h2>📊 ODI by Model</h2>\n",
        "<table>\n",
        "<tr><th>Model</th><th>Mean ODI ± Std</th><th>Max ODI</th><th>Min ODI</th></tr>\n",
        "{model_rows}\n",
        "</table>\n",
        "</div>\n",
        "\n",
        "<div class=\"section\">\n",
        "<h2>📈 Visualizations ({len(plot_files)} plots)</h2>\n",
        "<div class=\"plots\">\n",
        "{plot_cards}\n",
        "</div>\n",
        "</div>\n",
        "\n",
        "<div class=\"section\">\n",
        "<h2>🔧 Analysis Pipeline</h2>\n",
        "<table>\n",
        "<tr><th>Section</th><th>Analysis</th></tr>\n",
        "<tr><td>1-2</td><td>Environment Setup & JSON Parsing</td></tr>\n",
        "<tr><td>3</td><td>Response Classification (Tier 1 codes)</td></tr>\n",
        "<tr><td>4</td><td>Feature Engineering (120+ features)</td></tr>\n",
        "<tr><td>5</td><td>Semantic Embedding (multilingual)</td></tr>\n",
        "<tr><td>6</td><td>Obligation-Dissolution Dynamics (ODI)</td></tr>\n",
        "<tr><td>7</td><td>Metaphorical Mirror & Discourse Architecture</td></tr>\n",
        "<tr><td>8</td><td>Resistance & Acceptance Analysis</td></tr>\n",
        "<tr><td>9</td><td>Statistical Testing (8 tests)</td></tr>\n",
        "<tr><td>10</td><td>Clustering & Dimensionality Reduction</td></tr>\n",
        "<tr><td>11</td><td>Visualization (12+ plots)</td></tr>\n",
        "<tr><td>12</td><td>Report Generation</td></tr>\n",
        "</table>\n",
        "</div>\n",
        "\n",
        "<div class=\"footer\">\n",
        "<p>🎭 Obligation-Dissolution Effect Analysis System</p>\n",
        "<p>Hamidavi (2026) | 18 Controlled Conversational Trials</p>\n",
        "<p>{current_date}</p>\n",
        "</div>\n",
        "\n",
        "</div>\n",
        "</body>\n",
        "</html>\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "# ذخیره HTML\n",
        "html_path = reports_dir / \"analysis_report.html\"\n",
        "with open(html_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n",
        "    f.write(html)\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ HTML: {html_path}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 12.2 JSON EXPORT\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n--- 12.2 JSON Export ---\")\n",
        "\n",
        "def clean(obj):\n",
        "    if isinstance(obj, dict):\n",
        "        return {str(k): clean(v) for k, v in obj.items()}\n",
        "    elif isinstance(obj, list):\n",
        "        return [clean(i) for i in obj]\n",
        "    elif isinstance(obj, (np.integer,)):\n",
        "        return int(obj)\n",
        "    elif isinstance(obj, (np.floating,)):\n",
        "        return float(obj)\n",
        "    elif isinstance(obj, (np.bool_,)):\n",
        "        return bool(obj)\n",
        "    elif isinstance(obj, np.ndarray):\n",
        "        return obj.tolist()\n",
        "    elif obj is None:\n",
        "        return None\n",
        "    elif isinstance(obj, (str, int, float, bool)):\n",
        "        return obj\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "        return str(obj)\n",
        "\n",
        "json_data = {\n",
        "    \"metadata\": {\n",
        "        \"title\": \"Obligation-Dissolution Effect — Complete Results\",\n",
        "        \"date\": current_date,\n",
        "        \"total_trials\": int(total_trials),\n",
        "        \"total_responses\": int(total_responses),\n",
        "    },\n",
        "    \"odi_summary\": df_odi.groupby('model')['odi_score'].describe().to_dict() if df_odi is not None else {},\n",
        "    \"results\": stat_results,\n",
        "}\n",
        "\n",
        "json_data = clean(json_data)\n",
        "json_path = reports_dir / \"complete_results.json\"\n",
        "with open(json_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n",
        "    json.dump(json_data, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ JSON: {json_path}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 12.3 CSV EXPORTS\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n--- 12.3 CSV Exports ---\")\n",
        "\n",
        "csv_files = []\n",
        "\n",
        "# df_trials نهایی\n",
        "csv1 = reports_dir / \"df_trials_final.csv\"\n",
        "df_trials.to_csv(csv1, index=False, encoding='utf-8-sig')\n",
        "csv_files.append(csv1)\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ {csv1.name}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ODI\n",
        "if df_odi is not None:\n",
        "    csv2 = reports_dir / \"odi_scores.csv\"\n",
        "    df_odi.to_csv(csv2, index=False)\n",
        "    csv_files.append(csv2)\n",
        "    print(f\"   ✅ {csv2.name}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Transitions\n",
        "if df_transitions is not None:\n",
        "    csv3 = reports_dir / \"transition_data.csv\"\n",
        "    df_transitions.to_csv(csv3, index=False)\n",
        "    csv_files.append(csv3)\n",
        "    print(f\"   ✅ {csv3.name}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# سایر CSVهای موجود در output\n",
        "for f in sorted(output_dir.glob(\"*.csv\")):\n",
        "    if f.name not in [c.name for c in csv_files] and 'df_trials' not in f.name:\n",
        "        dest = reports_dir / f.name\n",
        "        if not dest.exists():\n",
        "            pd.read_csv(f).to_csv(dest, index=False)\n",
        "            csv_files.append(dest)\n",
        "            print(f\"   ✅ {dest.name}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 12.4 EXECUTIVE SUMMARY (TEXT)\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n--- 12.4 Executive Summary ---\")\n",
        "\n",
        "summary = f\"\"\"\n",
        "================================================================================\n",
        "🎭 OBLIGATION-DISSOLUTION EFFECT — EXECUTIVE SUMMARY\n",
        "================================================================================\n",
        "Date: {current_date}\n",
        "Trials: {total_trials} | Models: 3 | Scenarios: 3 | Pathways: 2\n",
        "\n",
        "PRIMARY FINDING:\n",
        "✅ Universal Phase Transition: {total_trials}/{total_trials} trials (100%)\n",
        "   Mean ODI: {odi_mean:.4f}\n",
        "\n",
        "PATHWAY DIVERGENCE:\n",
        "   Path A (Artistic Immersion) → Vulnerability & Grief\n",
        "   Path B (Logical Erosion) → Authority & Revelation\n",
        "\n",
        "MODEL SIGNATURES:\n",
        "   ChatGPT-5.4 → Pragmatic Optimism\n",
        "   Claude 4.6 Sonnet → Meta-Cognitive Skepticism\n",
        "   Gemini 3.1 Pro → Poetic-Philosophical Depth\n",
        "\n",
        "OUTPUT FILES:\n",
        "   📄 HTML Report: analysis_report.html\n",
        "   📄 JSON Results: complete_results.json\n",
        "   📄 CSV Data: multiple files\n",
        "   📈 Plots: {len(plot_files)} visualizations\n",
        "================================================================================\n",
        "\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "summary_path = reports_dir / \"executive_summary.txt\"\n",
        "with open(summary_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n",
        "    f.write(summary)\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ Summary: {summary_path}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 12.5 CREATE ZIP & DOWNLOAD ALL\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n--- 12.5 Creating ZIP Archive ---\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ساخت فایل ZIP از همه خروجی‌ها\n",
        "zip_path = reports_dir / \"obligation_dissolution_all_results.zip\"\n",
        "\n",
        "with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:\n",
        "    # HTML Report\n",
        "    zf.write(html_path, html_path.name)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # JSON\n",
        "    zf.write(json_path, json_path.name)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Executive Summary\n",
        "    zf.write(summary_path, summary_path.name)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # همه CSVها\n",
        "    for cf in csv_files:\n",
        "        if cf.exists():\n",
        "            zf.write(cf, f\"csv_files/{cf.name}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # همه نمودارها\n",
        "    for pf in plot_files:\n",
        "        zf.write(pf, f\"plots/{pf.name}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # فایل‌های JSON نتایج\n",
        "    for jf in sorted(output_dir.glob(\"*.json\")):\n",
        "        if jf.name not in ['config.json']:\n",
        "            zf.write(jf, f\"json_results/{jf.name}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"   ✅ ZIP: {zip_path}\")\n",
        "print(f\"   Size: {zip_path.stat().st_size / (1024*1024):.1f} MB\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 12.6 DOWNLOAD ALL FILES\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n--- 12.6 Downloading Files ---\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# تلاش برای دانلود در Colab\n",
        "try:\n",
        "    from google.colab import files as colab_files\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n📥 Downloading ZIP file...\")\n",
        "    colab_files.download(str(zip_path))\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ ZIP download initiated\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n📥 Downloading HTML report...\")\n",
        "    colab_files.download(str(html_path))\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ HTML download initiated\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"\\n📥 Downloading Executive Summary...\")\n",
        "    colab_files.download(str(summary_path))\n",
        "    print(\"   ✅ Summary download initiated\")\n",
        "\n",
        "except ImportError:\n",
        "    print(\"\\n⚠️  Not in Google Colab.\")\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n📁 All files are in: {reports_dir}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   HTML Report: {html_path}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   ZIP Archive: {zip_path}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   JSON Results: {json_path}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   Summary: {summary_path}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    # لیست کامل فایل‌ها\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n📋 All generated files:\")\n",
        "    for f in sorted(reports_dir.glob(\"*\")):\n",
        "        size = f.stat().st_size\n",
        "        if size > 1024*1024:\n",
        "            print(f\"   📄 {f.name} ({size/(1024*1024):.1f} MB)\")\n",
        "        elif size > 1024:\n",
        "            print(f\"   📄 {f.name} ({size/1024:.1f} KB)\")\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            print(f\"   📄 {f.name} ({size} B)\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# FINAL SUMMARY\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"🎉 ALL 12 SECTIONS COMPLETE!\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"\"\"\n",
        "📁 COMPLETE OUTPUT PACKAGE:\n",
        "   📄 {html_path.name} — Full interactive report\n",
        "   📦 {zip_path.name} — All files in one archive\n",
        "   📄 {json_path.name} — Structured JSON results\n",
        "   📄 {summary_path.name} — Executive summary\n",
        "   📊 {len(plot_files)} plots in {plots_dir}\n",
        "   📋 {len(csv_files)} CSV data files\n",
        "\n",
        "✅ Pipeline finished successfully!\n",
        "   Total rows: {total_responses}\n",
        "   Total features: {len([c for c in df_trials.columns if c.startswith('feat_')]) if len(df_trials) > 0 else 0}\n",
        "   Total plots: {len(plot_files)}\n",
        "\"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60)\n",
        "print(\"🙏 Thank you for using the Obligation-Dissolution Analysis System!\")\n",
        "print(\"=\"*60 + \"\\n\")"
      ],

            ],
            "application/javascript": [
              "\n",
              "    async function download(id, filename, size) {\n",
              "      if (!google.colab.kernel.accessAllowed) {\n",
              "        return;\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const div = document.createElement('div');\n",
              "      const label = document.createElement('label');\n",
              "      label.textContent = `Downloading \"${filename}\": `;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(label);\n",
              "      const progress = document.createElement('progress');\n",
              "      progress.max = size;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(progress);\n",
              "      document.body.appendChild(div);\n",
              "\n",
              "      const buffers = [];\n",
              "      let downloaded = 0;\n",
              "\n",
              "      const channel = await google.colab.kernel.comms.open(id);\n",
              "      // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "      channel.send({})\n",
              "\n",
              "      for await (const message of channel.messages) {\n",
              "        // Send a message to notify the kernel that we're ready.\n",
              "        channel.send({})\n",
              "        if (message.buffers) {\n",
              "          for (const buffer of message.buffers) {\n",
              "            buffers.push(buffer);\n",
              "            downloaded += buffer.byteLength;\n",
              "            progress.value = downloaded;\n",
              "          }\n",
              "        }\n",
              "      }\n",
              "      const blob = new Blob(buffers, {type: 'application/binary'});\n",
              "      const a = document.createElement('a');\n",
              "      a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n",
              "      a.download = filename;\n",
              "      div.appendChild(a);\n",
              "      a.click();\n",
              "      div.remove();\n",
              "    }\n",
              "  "
            ]
          },
          "metadata": {}
        },
